Evaluated on all 19 datasets of the mTSBench benchmark (Zhou et al., TMLR 2026), CRAFTIIF achieves mean F1=0.228 (all 19 datasets) and F1=0.322 (13 detectable datasets), ranking first among all 25 evaluated methods on VUS-PR (0.463 vs. previous best 0.329, +40.7%). A diagnostic framework -- oracle F1, detectability limits, and branch separation ratios -- identifies 6 of 19 datasets as fundamentally undetectable by any unsupervised method. Ablation over 11 conditions confirms adaptive thresholding (+38% F1), four-branch structure (+20%), and meta-IF (+23%) are each essential. Code: https://github.com/smitswil/craftiif

</details>


### [113] [A2D2: Fine-Tuning Any-Length Discrete Diffusion for Adaptive Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13565)
*Sophia Tang,Yuchen Zhu,Molei Tao,Pranam Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出A2D2，一个统一的奖励引导微调框架，适用于任意长度离散扩散模型，通过联合优化插入和去掩码策略以及基于质量的推理调度，实现自适应解码，在理论上保证收敛到奖励倾斜分布，并在实验中优于先前的固定长度微调和推理时引导方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型已扩展到任意长度序列生成，但原则性的奖励引导微调方法尚未被探索，现有方法主要局限于固定长度设置或推理时引导，缺乏理论基础和灵活性。

Method: 推导了联合插入-去掩码路径测度的Radon-Nikodym导数，保证收敛到奖励倾斜分布；建立去掩码和插入质量作为最小化解码误差的可行方法；提出自适应联合解码（AJD）损失，理论上可得到生成奖励倾斜分布的最优路径测度。

Result: A2D2在奖励优化、生成灵活性和准确性上均优于先前的固定长度微调和推理时引导方法。

Conclusion: A2D2为任意长度离散扩散模型的奖励引导微调提供了统一的理论框架和有效的实践方法，兼具理论完备性和实证优越性。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models offer a simple and stable likelihood-based framework for sequence generation, recently extended to any-length settings via token insertion. Principled reward-guided fine-tuning for any-length discrete diffusion, however, remains largely unexplored. We introduce Fine-Tuning Any-Length Discrete Diffusion for Adaptive Decoding (A2D2), a unified framework for reward-guided fine-tuning of any-length discrete diffusion models via joint optimization of the insertion and unmasking policies together with a quality-based inference schedule. We derive the Radon-Nikodym derivative for the joint insertion-unmasking path measures, enabling theoretically guaranteed convergence to the intractable reward-tilted sequence distribution without requiring target samples. Building on this, we establish unmasking and insertion quality as tractable approaches for minimizing decoding error and introduce the Adaptive Joint Decoding (AJD) loss, which provably yields the optimal path measure that generates the reward-tilted distribution. Empirically, A2D2 improves reward optimization while enhancing generation flexibility and accuracy over prior fixed-length fine-tuning and inference-time guidance methods.

</details>


### [114] [Adjusted Cup-Product Neural Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13568)
*Snigdha Chandan Khilar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种调整杯积神经层（Adjusted Cup Product Neural Layer），通过硬编码杯积和高阶规范理论中的调整项，实现了规范不变的读出机制。理论证明在闭链上输出完全取决于调整系数，且该系数为零时输出消失，证明了调整是规范不变信号的唯一来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理和几何中的许多重要可观测量是上链的杯积（cup product）。现有方法缺乏对规范不变性的内在保证，需要设计一种在结构上保证规范不变性的神经层。

Method: 提出调整杯积神经层，将杯积与高阶规范理论的调整项硬编码为神经基元，通过调整系数控制读出结果，确保读出在设计中即为规范不变的。

Result: 在闭链上输出完全由调整系数决定；当调整系数为零时，无论其他参数如何，输出完全消失；该可观测量是一个非零二次型，且在一重和二重规范变换下严格不变。

Conclusion: 调整项是规范不变信号的唯一来源，该设计为物理和几何中的规范不变可观测量的学习提供了理论保证。

Abstract: Many important observables in physics and geometry are cup products of cochains. The adjusted cup product neural layer has been introduced in this paper. It is a neural primitive that hard wires the cup product with an adjustment term from higher gauge theory. This creates a readout that is gauge invariant by design. Their main theoretical result shows that on a closed cycle the output relies entirely on the adjustment coefficient. Setting this coefficient to zero removes the output completely regardless of other parameters. Thus the adjustment is the only source of gauge invariant signal. They prove this observable is a nonzero quadratic form and is exactly invariant under one and two gauge transformations.

</details>


### [115] [Existence Precedes Value: Joint Modeling of Observational Existence and Evolving States in Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13571)
*Yifan Hu,Hongzhou Chen,Peiyuan Liu,Yiding Liu,Zewei Dong,Jiang-Ming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Timeflies是一个统一框架，将时间序列预测重新定义为未来可观测性推断与数值估计的联合问题，通过观测流和数值流双流耦合架构，在高度不完整和不规则的真实时间序列上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如Neural ODEs、连续时间图网络）虽然改进了对历史不规则性的建模，但在推理时仍隐含地假设未来有效观测的时间戳已知，这在许多真实系统中不成立——因为更根本的问题不仅是未来值是什么，还包括未来是否会有有效观测。

Method: 提出Timeflies框架，包含观测流和数值流双流架构，通过三个专用模块耦合：(1) 可靠性感知嵌入 (reliability-aware embedding)，(2) 观测引导的依赖建模 (observation-guided dependency modeling)，(3) 联合预测 (joint prediction)。同时构建了Shadow基准数据集并引入观测-数值联合熵(OVJE)指标。

Result: 大量实验表明Timeflies在多个数据集上持续优于现有方法，证明了显式建模未来可观测性在缺失值时间序列预测中的重要性。

Conclusion: 未来可观测性推断与数值估计应当联合建模，而非将观测时间戳作为已知先验，这能显著提升不规则时间序列预测的现实相关性和性能。

Abstract: Real-world time series are often highly incomplete and irregular due to sensor dormancy, transmission delays, and event-driven sampling, making reliable forecasting fundamentally challenging. Existing methods have evolved from impute-then-forecast pipelines to continuous-time models such as Neural ODEs and continuous-time graph networks. While these approaches improve the modeling of historical irregularity, they still rely on an implicit oracle assumption at inference time: the timestamps of future valid observations are presumed to be known in advance. This assumption limits practical relevance, since in many real systems the more fundamental question is not only what the future value will be, but also whether a valid observation will occur at all. In this paper, we propose Timeflies, a unified framework that reformulates forecasting as a joint problem of future observability inference and value estimation. To explicitly model the interaction between observation dynamics and state evolution, Timeflies adopts an observation stream and a value stream, coupled through three dedicated modules for reliability-aware embedding, observation-guided dependency modeling, and joint prediction. We further construct Shadow, a benchmark that combines natural missingness from public datasets with real-world industrial data, and introduce the Observation-Value Joint Entropy (OVJE) metric to comprehensively evaluate this coupled predictability. Extensive experiments show that Timeflies consistently outperforms existing methods, highlighting the importance of explicitly modeling future observability in time series forecasting with missing values. Code and dataset are available in https://github.com/ant-intl/Timeflies.

</details>


### [116] [Learning with Simulators: No Regret in a Computationally Bounded World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13576)
*Sasha Voitovych,Abhishek Shetty,Noah Golowich,Alexander Rakhlin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"可模拟过程"框架，证明若学习器能访问数据生成过程的模拟器（即使数据高度依赖且复杂），仍可恢复与独立数据相同的经典学习保证（基于VC维的误差界），并展示了条件采样的统计与计算优势，最终给出一个统一算法可在有界多项式时间可模拟的所有过程上学习任意VC类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典学习理论中大多数泛化结果依赖数据独立性假设，而强依赖数据下的理论结果非常有限。本文旨在填补这一空白，探索在数据过程高度复杂且依赖的情况下，学习所需的极简假设。

Method: 引入"可模拟过程"框架，假设学习器可访问近似数据生成分布的模拟器；利用该模拟器证明可恢复VC维依赖的经典误差界；进一步分析条件采样的统计与计算优势；构造统一算法，其遗憾由过程的时间有界Kolmogorov复杂度控制。

Result: 1) 在可模拟过程框架下，可获得与独立数据相同的VC维依赖的泛化保证；2) 条件采样带来严格的统计与计算优势；3) 存在单一算法可同时学习所有有界多项式时间可模拟过程下的任意VC类。

Conclusion: 该框架显著拓宽了经典PAC模型，将学习理论从独立同分布假设推广至更一般的依赖过程，为强依赖数据下的学习提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Understanding the minimal assumptions necessary for generalization is the fundamental question in learning theory. Unfortunately, most results rely heavily on independence (or some proxy thereof) of the data-generating process, while results for strongly dependent data are far more limited. Towards addressing this gap, we introduce the framework of simulatable processes, where the learner has access to a simulator that approximates the distribution generating the data (which may be an arbitrarily complex and dependent process). Surprisingly, given access to such a simulator, we show that we can recover the same learning guarantees as in the classical setting with independent data, namely, error bounds that depend on the VC dimension. Further, we use this framework to study the power of conditional sampling and show strict statistical and computational advantages in this setting. As a highlight of our framework, we exhibit a single algorithm that simultaneously learns any given VC class under all processes samplable in bounded polynomial time, with regret controlled by the time-bounded Kolmogorov complexity of the process. This provides a significant conceptual broadening of the classical PAC model.

</details>


### [117] [Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging: Transitioning from Uniform Priors to Sparse Posteriors in Ensemble Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13589)
*Meher Sai Preetam,Meher Bhaskar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCSB是一种数学严格的集成剪枝与概率校准框架，通过在概率单纯形上联合优化OOB损失并引入凹二次惩罚解决L1单纯形悖论，实现高达96%的集成压缩、线性推理加速和更好的概率校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Bagging集成（如随机森林、Bagged SVM、Bagged神经网络）对所有基估计器赋予均匀投票权重，忽略了基估计器在不同局部区域的性能差异，导致模型过度自信（overconfidence）。需要一种能自动识别并保留高质量基估计器、同时进行概率校准的方法。

Method: 将集成剪枝和校准形式化为概率单纯形上的联合优化问题，通过最小化OOB损失来求解。针对L1范数在单纯形上为常数、无法产生稀疏解的理论困境（L1-simplex paradox），引入凹二次惩罚项来诱导稀疏性，实现基估计器的自动选择。

Result: 模型无关，最高可达到96%的集成压缩率，带来线性推理加速；同时降低期望校准误差（ECE），提升概率校准质量，并保持或提升泛化精度。

Conclusion: SCSB通过理论严谨的优化方法同时解决了集成剪枝和概率校准两大问题，在压缩率和校准质量上均表现优异，且不依赖特定模型类型。

Abstract: We present Simplex-Constrained Sparse Bagging (SCSB), a mathematically rigorous framework for post-training compression and probability calibration of bootstrap-based bagging ensembles. Standard bagging ensembles (such as Random Forests, Bagged SVMs, and Bagged Neural Networks) assign uniform voting power to all constituent estimators. However, this naive uniform prior ignores the varying local competence of base estimators and contributes to model overconfidence. We formulate ensemble pruning and calibration as a joint optimization problem over the probability simplex by minimizing the Out-Of-Bag (OOB) loss. To induce sparsity, we address the theoretical "L1-simplex paradox" -- the mathematical reality that the L1 norm is constant on the simplex and fails to prune -- by introducing a concave quadratic penalty. SCSB is model-agnostic and achieves up to 96% ensemble compression, yielding linear inference speedups and superior probability calibration (lowered Expected Calibration Error) while preserving or enhancing generalization accuracy.

</details>


### [118] [Beyond the Commitment Boundary: Probing Epiphenomenal Chain-of-Thought in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13603)
*Daniel Scalena,Sara Candussio,Luca Bortolussi,Elisabetta Fersini,Malvina Nissim,Gabriele Sarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过早期退出方法估计CoT推理步骤的因果重要性，发现推理过程存在一个"承诺边界"——从临时猜测到稳定高置信答案的急剧转变，该边界通常发生在推理块结束前的单个步骤中，后续步骤对最终答案概率无影响（即副现象）。利用注意力探针可从中间步骤线性解码答案形成阶段，并据此提前退出推理，平均可将CoT长度缩短55%且性能损失可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT推理是语言模型推理时扩展的主流范式，但每个推理步骤对最终答案的因果影响尚不清楚，现有研究缺乏对推理过程中答案形成机制的理解。

Method: 通过早期退出（early exit）方法估计每个推理步骤的因果重要性；使用注意力探针（attention probes）从中间推理步骤线性解码答案形成阶段；利用承诺边界信号实现推理块的早期退出。

Result: 发现跨多种任务和模型家族存在承诺边界；该边界通常发生在推理块结束前的单一逐步骤中；后续CoT步骤是副现象（epiphenomenal），不改变最终答案概率；注意力探针可高精度线性解码答案形成阶段并泛化到未见任务；基于此信号早期退出可将CoT长度平均缩短55%且性能损失极小。

Conclusion: CoT推理中存在可检测的承诺边界，后续步骤多为副现象，通过检测该边界可实现高效的推理早期退出，大幅压缩推理成本而不损害模型性能。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning is the dominant paradigm for inference-time scaling in language models, yet the causal influence of individual steps on the final answer poorly understood. We estimate each step's causal importance via early exit and use this measure to study how answers form across the reasoning traces of several model families. Across diverse tasks, we find that reasoning typically crosses a \emph{commitment boundary} -- a sharp transition from transient intermediate guesses to a stable, high-confidence answer. This transition often happens in a single step, well before the model's reasoning block ends, and is followed by \emph{epiphenomenal} CoT steps that leave the final answer probability unaltered. Using attention probes, we show that answer-formation stages can be linearly decoded from intermediate reasoning steps with high accuracy and generalize robustly to unseen reasoning tasks. We exploit this signal to early-exit reasoning blocks at the commitment boundary, reducing the length of CoTs up to 55\% on average with negligible impact on model performance.

</details>


### [119] [The Stable Recovery Manifold: Geometric Principles Governing Recoverability in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13637)
*Ayushman Trivedi,Bhavika Melwani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了灾难性遗忘是知识破坏的传统观点，通过引入恢复子空间维度（k_t）等几何度量，发现遗忘知识仍紧凑可解码，遗忘本质上是可访问性和流形对齐问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Accessibility Collapse框架，从几何结构角度探究持续学习中可恢复性的内在机制，验证遗忘表征是否仍保持可解码的几何结构。

Method: 在Split CIFAR-100上用顺序训练的ResNet-18进行10个任务实验，引入恢复子空间维度（k_t）度量、主角度漂移分析，并建立简单几何模型解释可恢复性方差。

Result: 恢复维度保持稳定（平均k_t=8.0）；主角度漂移与可恢复性强相关（r=-0.862）；几何模型解释82.2%的可恢复性方差。

Conclusion: 支持稳定恢复流形假说：遗忘知识未破坏，仍紧凑可解码；灾难性遗忘主要是可访问性和流形对齐问题，而非信息破坏。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting is often viewed as the destruction of previously learned knowledge during sequential learning. Building on the Accessibility Collapse framework, we investigate the geometric structure of recoverability in continual learning. Using Split CIFAR-100 and a sequentially trained ResNet-18, we analyze recoverability, representational drift, and recovery complexity across ten tasks. We introduce Recovery Subspace Dimensionality (k_t), a measure of the minimum number of singular directions required to preserve 90 percent of full probe performance. Contrary to our Recoverability Diffusion hypothesis, recovery dimensionality remains stable throughout training (mean k_t = 8.0) despite substantial representational drift. Principal-angle drift strongly predicts recoverability (r = -0.862), and a simple geometric model explains 82.2 percent of recoverability variance. These findings support the Stable Recovery Manifold hypothesis, suggesting that forgotten knowledge remains compactly decodable despite representational reorganization. The results indicate that catastrophic forgetting is primarily an accessibility and manifold-alignment problem rather than information destruction.

</details>


### [120] [Dense Supervision, Sparse Updates: On the Sparsity and Geometry of On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13657)
*Guo Yu,Wenlin Liu,Yulan Hu,Hao-Xuan Ma,Jun-Peng Jiang,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了在线策略蒸馏（OPD）的更新特性，发现其更新具有稀疏性和频谱集中性，即更新量小且坐标稀疏、分布在所有层且以FFN为主、数值满秩但频谱集中，且主要集中在源权重接近零的坐标上。这表明OPD并非普通密集参数重写，而是保留了在线策略后训练的重要几何特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）作为一种结合在线策略学生轨迹与密集教师监督的后训练方法效果显著，但其对模型参数的具体影响机制尚不明确，作者试图从稀疏性和几何结构两个维度揭示OPD更新如何改变模型参数。

Method: 跨多个语言模型和视觉-语言模型对及使用场景，从稀疏性（更新大小、坐标稀疏性、层分布、FFN比重）和几何结构（数值秩、频谱集中性、与源权重子空间的关系）两个角度分析OPD更新。

Result: OPD更新具有以下特征：(1)更新量小且坐标稀疏，分布在所有层且以FFN为主；(2)这种稀疏结构具有操作实用性，仅训练发现的子网络即可接近完整OPD性能；(3)稀疏诱导的SGD优化器性能不如AdamW，因为密集教师监督保留了异构坐标梯度尺度；(4)更新数值上满秩但频谱集中，主要位于源权重主奇异子空间之外，且集中在源权重接近零的坐标上。

Conclusion: 密集教师监督并未使OPD变成普通的密集参数重写，OPD保留了在线策略后训练的重要几何特征，其更新模式具有独特的稀疏性和频谱集中特性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (\textsc{OPD}) has recently become a prominent post-training recipe as it combines two desirable ingredients: on-policy student trajectories and dense teacher supervision, yet how this hybrid changes a model's parameters remains unclear. Across several language and vision-language model pairs and use cases, our analysis yields two main findings. On sparsity, \textsc{OPD}-style updates are small and coordinate-sparse. They are distributed across layers and are usually FFN-heavy. This sparse structure is operationally useful: training only the discovered subnetwork recovers nearly the same performance as full \textsc{OPD}. However, the sparsity-inducing SGD optimizer underperforms AdamW in our optimizer ablation, likely because dense teacher supervision preserves heterogeneous coordinate-wise gradient scales where AdamW's adaptive scaling remains useful. On geometry, the updates are numerically full-rank but spectrally concentrated; they lie mostly away from the principal singular subspaces of the source weights and fall disproportionately on coordinates where the source weights are close to zero. These findings suggest that dense teacher supervision does not turn \textsc{OPD} into ordinary dense parameter rewriting; instead, \textsc{OPD} retains important geometric signatures of on-policy post-training.

</details>


### [121] [Understanding Truncated Positional Encodings for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13671)
*James Flora,Mitchell Black,Weng-Keen Wong,Amir Nayyeri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了图神经网络中位置编码的截断版本，发现截断后不同位置的编码在表达能力上存在根本差异，截断谱编码不再强于1-WL测试，并实验证明混合截断编码优于单一类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理论和实践中广泛使用的位置编码（谱编码和游走编码）在完整版本下表达能力等价，但完整版本需要O(n³)复杂度，实践中常使用截断变体（如前k个特征空间或邻接矩阵的幂），然而这些截断编码的理论性质未知，因此需要系统研究截断位置编码的表达能力。

Method: 理论分析截断谱编码和截断游走编码的表达能力差异，引入k-调和距离这一谱编码家族，并通过实验验证混合截断编码在真实数据集上的效果。

Result: 截断后不同位置编码家族的表达能力存在根本差异；截断谱编码不再强于1-WL测试；不同截断编码之间存在表达能力的层次关系。

Conclusion: 截断位置编码的理论性质与完整版本完全不同，实践中混合使用多种截断编码比单一类型更优。

Abstract: Positional encodings (PEs) enhance the power of graph neural networks (GNNs), both theoretically and empirically. Two of the most popular families of PEs - spectral (e.g., Laplacian eigenspaces, effective resistance) and walk-based (polynomials of the adjacency matrix) - are theoretically equivalent in expressive power, with expressivity between the 1-WL and 3-WL tests. However, this equivalence assumes the GNN uses the "complete" version of these PEs, which requires $O(n^3)$ time and space complexity. Instead, practitioners commonly use truncated variants of these encodings, such as the first $k$ eigenspaces or powers of the adjacency matrix. However, the theoretical properties of these truncated PEs are unknown. In this work, we initiate the study of these truncated PEs. Theoretically, we show that, under truncation, several families of PEs are fundamentally different in expressive power. As a corollary, we show that truncated spectral PEs are no longer stronger than the 1-WL test. We also study a family of spectral PEs, the $k$-harmonic distances, to highlight the differences in expressive power of even closely related truncated PEs. Finally, we experimentally show that a mix of truncated PEs is preferable to any single family on real-world datasets.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [Effective Geometry and Position-Dependent Mass in Dual-$q$ Quantum Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12444)
*A. Boumali,A. Makhlouf*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Borges提出的变形导数形式，重点分析了线性算子D_(q)与其非线性对偶D^(q)的关系。通过坐标和波函数的联合变换，将对偶导数引入动力学项导致的非线性薛定谔方程转化为变形坐标下的线性方程，等效于厄米的位置相关质量哈密顿量。变形参数q决定了有效质量分布和伴随度量。将该形式应用于自由粒子、无限深势阱、矩形势垒和弱变形谐振子，并与Costa Filho等人的非加性平移方法比较，表明两者可导出相同有效几何结构。q<1时有效受限长度减小，提高束缚态能谱并增强隧穿；q>1时则相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Borges变形导数形式中对偶算子D^(q)直接引入动力学项导致非线性和概率解释模糊的问题，寻找将非线性问题转化为线性可解形式的方法。

Method: 采用坐标与波函数的联合变换，将对偶导数框架下的非线性薛定谔方程映射为变形坐标中的线性薛定谔方程，并证明该形式等效于厄米的位置相关质量哈密顿量。通过与Costa Filho等人的非加性平移框架对比，验证两种方法在有效几何结构上的等价性。

Result: 建立了Borges对偶变形导数与位置相关质量哈密顿量之间的严格对应关系；变形参数q直接调控有效长度：q<1缩短有效长度，提升能谱并增强隧穿；q>1则相反。四种标准模型的解析解验证了该形式的有效性。

Conclusion: Borges对偶变形导数形式通过坐标-波函数联合变换可归结为物理坐标下的厄米PDM哈密顿量，为量子力学中变形导数提供了一种具有清晰物理诠释的线性化方案，且与已有非加性平移方法在几何结构上等价。

Abstract: This work investigates the deformed-derivative formalism introduced by Borges, with emphasis on the relation between the linear operator $D_{(q)}$ and its nonlinear dual counterpart $D^{(q)}$. Directly inserting the dual derivative into the kinetic term leads to a nonlinear Schrödinger equation and obscures the usual interpretation of superposition and probability. We show that this nonlinearity can be removed by a simultaneous transformation of the coordinate and of the wave function. The transformed problem is an ordinary linear Schrödinger equation in a deformed coordinate, and its representation in the physical coordinate is equivalent to a Hermitian position-dependent-mass (PDM) Hamiltonian. In this formulation, the deformation parameter $q$ determines both the effective mass profile and the associated metric. The formalism is applied to the free particle, the infinite square well, the rectangular barrier, and the harmonic oscillator in the weak-deformation regime. Comparison with the nonadditive-translation approach of Costa Filho \emph{et al.} shows that the Borges dual-$q$ framework provides an alternative route to the same effective geometric structure. For $q<1$, the effective confinement length is reduced, which raises the bound-state spectrum and enhances tunneling; for $q>1$, the effective length is increased, which lowers the spectrum and suppresses tunneling relative to the undeformed limit $q=1$.

</details>


### [123] [SAT, MaxSAT, and SMT for QLDPC Distance Computation: A Large-Scale Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12445)
*Yu-Fang Chen,Seyed Mohammad Reza Jafari,Ching-Yi Lai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了基于SAT和MaxSAT的量子LDPC码精确距离计算方法，发现：XOR感知推理并无系统性优势；Brouwer-Zimmermann搜索在量子LDPC场景中不再保持传统优势；分支定界MaxSAT显著优于unsat-core-based MaxSAT，求解器架构和优化策略对可扩展性起决定性作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子LDPC码的精确距离计算在验证容错量子码构造中至关重要，但其计算结构尚未被充分理解。尽管量子LDPC码设计取得显著进展，但哪些算法原则支配精确距离计算的实际可扩展性、哪类精确求解器最适合该任务仍不明确。

Method: 对代表性量子LDPC码，系统对比了基于SAT和MaxSAT的精确距离计算形式化方法，并与多种已有精确距离方法进行比较，以理解算法图景。

Result: （1）XOR感知推理并未在基准测试中提供系统性优势；（2）Brouwer-Zimmermann搜索在量子LDPC场景中丧失了传统可扩展性优势；（3）分支定界MaxSAT在挑战性基准上显著优于unsat-core-based MaxSAT。

Conclusion: 量子LDPC码精确距离计算的可扩展性更多受限于基数约束和优化边界的处理而非奇偶推理；稀疏经典码的成功技术不一定主导量子LDPC码；求解器架构和优化策略对实际可扩展性至关重要。

Abstract: Exact distance computation for quantum LDPC (QLDPC) codes plays a central role in validating candidate fault-tolerant quantum-code constructions, yet the computational structure of this problem remains poorly understood. Despite substantial recent progress in QLDPC design, it remains unclear which algorithmic principles govern the practical scalability of exact distance computation and which classes of exact solvers are best suited to this task. To address these questions, we conduct a systematic study of SAT- and MaxSAT-based formulations for exact QLDPC distance computation across representative codes. We further compare these formulations against several established exact-distance approaches in order to better understand the algorithmic landscape of exact QLDPC distance computation. Our study challenges and refines several prevailing intuitions about exact QLDPC distance computation. First, despite the XOR-rich structure of QLDPC parity checks, practical scalability appears to be governed more by the handling of cardinality constraints and optimization bounds than by parity reasoning alone. Accordingly, XOR-aware reasoning does not provide a systematic advantage across our benchmark suite. Second, Brouwer-Zimmermann-style search, long regarded as the benchmark paradigm for exact distance computation in sparse classical codes, no longer maintains its traditional scalability advantage in the QLDPC setting. This finding challenges the expectation that techniques successful for sparse classical codes remain dominant for QLDPC codes. Third, substantial qualitative differences arise even among MaxSAT solvers themselves. Branch-and-bound MaxSAT significantly outperforms unsat-core-based MaxSAT on challenging benchmarks, demonstrating that solver architecture and optimization strategy play a decisive role in practical scalability.

</details>


### [124] [Theoretical Study for Generating Optical GKP State via a Single-Photon-Added Squeezed Vacuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12467)
*Deriyan Senjaya*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用单光子增加压缩真空态（SPSV）与50:50分束器及条件测量生成光学GKP态的理论方案，在3.76 dB压缩水平下达到85%的保真度，性能优于基于压缩奇薛定谔猫态的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找比薛定谔猫态更简单、实验上更易实现的光学GKP态制备方案，以推动容错光量子计算的发展。

Method: 将单光子增加压缩真空态（由压缩参数r定义）注入50:50分束器，通过对其中一个输出端口进行条件测量，投影得到近似有限能量GKP态。

Result: 在压缩水平为3.76 dB时，该方案实现了85%的最大保真度。

Conclusion: 基于单光子增加压缩真空态的GKP态制备方案在保真度上优于基于压缩奇薛定谔猫态的方法，为容错光量子计算提供了一条实用有效的路径。

Abstract: A theoretical framework is developed to analyze the generation of the optical GKP state using a single-photon-added squeezed vacuum. This state, defined by the squeezing parameter $r$, is injected into a 50:50 beam splitter, and the optical GKP state is obtained through conditional measurement at one output port. The single-photon-added squeezed vacuum is especially prominent in this context because it provides a simpler and more experimentally accessible ingredient than Schrodinger cat states, while conditional measurement ensures projection onto a state that closely approximates the finite-energy GKP form. Fidelity is employed to quantify this closeness, and the analysis demonstrates that the scheme achieves a maximum fidelity of 85% at a squeezing level of $3.76 \ \text{dB}$. This performance surpasses approaches based on squeezed optical odd Schrodinger cat states, underscoring the single-photon-added squeezed vacuum as a practical and effective pathway toward fault-tolerant photonic quantum computing.

</details>


### [125] [Geometric Algebra Quantum Gate Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12480)
*Youssef Amraoui,Zeno Toffano*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在复几何代数框架下重新表述Pauli群和Clifford群，揭示了Pauli算子与子空间叶片的几何对应关系，提出了一种贪心Pauli转子分解算法，并给出了Clifford+T通用集的几何解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的矩阵和张量积形式化方法常常掩盖了量子门的几何结构，作者希望通过几何代数框架揭示其内在的几何意义。

Method: 在复几何代数框架下，将Pauli群自然识别为叶片群（模全局相位），证明Clifford算子可由π/4-Pauli转子生成，并设计了一种贪心Pauli转子分解算法。

Result: Pauli群与定向子空间叶片一一对应；Clifford算子可由π/4-Pauli转子乘积生成；贪心算法经验上表现出出乎意料的紧凑分解；Clifford+T可通过π/8转子获得自然的几何解释。

Conclusion: 复几何代数框架为量子门提供了清晰的几何图像，Pauli算子对应定向子空间，Clifford算子对应旋转，T门对应π/8旋转，有望推动量子计算中几何直觉的发展。

Abstract: Quantum gates are usually described through matrix and tensor-product formalisms that often obscure their geometric structure. In this work, we formulate the Pauli and Clifford groups within the complex Geometric Algebra (GA) framework. We show that the Pauli group is naturally identified with the group of blades up to a global phase, thereby providing a geometric interpretation of Pauli operators and their commutation relations in terms of oriented subspaces. We further prove that Clifford operators are generated by products of π/4-Pauli rotors and introduce a greedy Pauli rotor decomposition algorithm whose empirical behavior suggests unexpectedly compact decompositions for Clifford operators. Finally, we show that Clifford+T universality admits a natural geometric interpretation through π/8-rotors within this framework.

</details>


### [126] [Statistical Mechanics and Symmetries of Non-Abelian Anyon Proliferation: From Deformation to Decoherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12527)
*Avi Vadali,Robijn Vanhove,Ruben Verresen,Jason Alicea,Pablo Sala*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过统计力学模型分析拓扑序在波函数形变和退相干下稳定性的通用框架，发现非阿贝尔任意子的增殖会导致共享阿贝尔融合产物的寄生凝聚，从而破坏拓扑序，且产生的平庸相会"记住"凝聚了哪些任意子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑量子计算依赖非阿贝尔任意子的编织操作，但需要拓扑序在制备不完美和环境噪声下保持稳定，因此需要系统研究拓扑序的稳定性条件。

Method: 构建统计力学模型，利用对称性自然揭示破坏性的任意子激发；以D₄拓扑序为例，结合蒙特卡洛模拟研究非阿贝尔任意子增殖下的稳定性。

Result: 发现超过有限阈值后，两种非阿贝尔任意子的增殖会导致共享阿贝尔融合产物的寄生凝聚，从而破坏拓扑序；所得的平庸相与凝聚所有阿贝尔电荷得到的平庸相不同，即"记忆"了凝聚的任意子类型。

Conclusion: 该框架为识别非阿贝尔拓扑序最优解码器所需的相关对称性提供了初步基础，可通过综合征测量实现。

Abstract: Topological quantum computation relies on braiding non-Abelian anyons, but requires the underlying topological order to survive imperfect state preparation and environmental noise. We show that the instability of topological order to wavefunction deformations and to decoherence, with the latter probed by syndrome distributions, are generically captured by stat-mech models whose symmetries naturally expose the corrupting anyonic excitations. As an example, we combine this framework with Monte-Carlo simulations to resolve the stability of $D_4$ topological order under deformations and quantum channels that proliferate multiple non-Abelian anyon species that individually are unable to condense. We show that beyond a finite threshold, proliferation of two non-Abelian anyon species parasitically condenses a shared Abelian-anyon fusion outcome, destroying the topological order. Our symmetry-based approach sharply differentiates the resulting trivial phase from that obtained by condensing all Abelian charges; in other words, the trivial phase "remembers" which anyons condensed. This framework provides a first step into identifying the relevant symmetry for optimal decoders, conditioned on syndrome measurements, of non-Abelian topological order.

</details>


### [127] [Unifying spacetime approaches to quantum mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12539)
*N. L. Diaz,M. Cerezo,Paolo Braccia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了"时空态"(spacetime states)作为统一框架，证明路径积分、量子态随时间演化、伪密度矩阵、Page-Wootters机制、超密度算符和类时间纠缠等主要量子力学时空方法都是该框架的不同表现形式，并通过特定评估、信息约化、线性映射或量子信道相互关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子力学形式体系在时间和空间处理上不对称，近年来涌现了大量试图将时空平等对待的量子力学方法，但这些方法之间缺乏统一理解，关系不明确。

Method: 通过构建"时空态"(spacetime states)作为核心对象，系统展示了多种已知形式体系如何通过特定评估、信息约化、线性映射或量子信道从时空态中派生出来，从而建立数学等价关系。

Result: 证明了路径积分、量子态随时间演化、伪密度矩阵、Page-Wootters机制、超密度算符和类时间纠缠等均统一于时空态框架；阐明了各自捕获的时空信息及其相互关系；拓展了该框架在Leggett-Garg不等式、OTOC、时间张量网络、费米子系统、相对论QFT、量子参考系和经典物理中的应用。

Conclusion: 时空态为理解量子力学中时空对称性提供了统一视角，揭示了多种看似不同的时空量子力学方法的内在联系，并为进一步探索时空信息结构提供了有效的数学工具。

Abstract: Recent efforts to formulate quantum mechanics in a way that treats space and time on a more equal footing have led to a large variety of spacetime-oriented approaches. In this work we present a detailed study of spacetime states, the objects that play the role of quantum states in the recently introduced framework of spacetime quantum mechanics, and show that the main proposals in the literature are different manifestations of the same underlying object. Path integrals, quantum states over time, pseudo-density matrices, the Page and Wootters mechanism, superdensity operators, and timelike-entanglement proposals all arise from spacetime states through particular evaluations, reduced information, linear maps, or quantum channels. This unification provides explicit mathematical representations of these formalisms, reveals relations among them, and clarifies the spacetime information each one captures. We also study the broader relevance of the spacetime-state point of view for Leggett-Garg inequalities, OTOCs, temporal tensor networks, fermionic systems, relativistic QFTs, quantum reference frames, and classical physics, together with additional insights and perspectives revealed by the common unifying framework.

</details>


### [128] [Spectral analysis of equilibration: information leakage in isolated quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12545)
*André T. Cesário,Marcos G. Alpino,Reinaldo O. Vianna,Tiago Debarba*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于子空间粗粒化方法，建立了孤立量子系统热化的统一动力学-谱框架。核心是定义了泄漏保真度函数（LFF），并推导了其时间涨落的普适界、与谱间隙结构及有效维度的关系，建立了与量子速度极限的联系，揭示了谱复杂度与子空间信息泄漏如何共同决定封闭量子多体系统的不可逆性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为孤立量子系统的热化建立统一的理论框架，避免依赖系综假设或微扰论证，提供一种状态依赖、几何直观的热化理解方式。

Method: 提出泄漏保真度函数（LFF）作为核心量，结合谱间隙结构、有效维度、谱功率分布和熵度量，并连接量子速度极限进行热化时间尺度的分析。

Result: 推导了LFF时间涨落的普适界限；证明了大的谱离域化抑制涨落并保证平均意义上的热化；建立了相位混合、间隙参与度和动力学稳定性之间的定量联系；揭示了平均热化时间与量子速度极限的关系。

Conclusion: 谱复杂度和子空间信息泄漏共同决定了封闭量子多体系统中的不可逆性，该框架为理解量子热化提供了几何清晰且状态依赖的新视角。

Abstract: We develop a unified dynamical-spectral framework for equilibration in isolated quantum systems based on a subspace coarse-graining approach. Central to our formulation is the Leakage Fidelity Function (LFF), defined as the probability that a unitarily evolving state escapes the support of its initial subspace. This quantity provides a direct, operational measure of information flow and memory loss without invoking ensemble assumptions or perturbative arguments. We derive universal bounds on temporal fluctuations of the LFF, in terms of the spectral gap structure and the square of the effective dimension, evincing that large spectral delocalization suppresses fluctuations and guarantees equilibration on average. By introducing spectral power distributions and associated entropic measures, we establish a quantitative link between phase mixing, gap participation, and dynamical stability. We further investigate the equilibration timescale by connecting the LFF to quantum speed limits, thereby revealing the average time required for equilibration. Our results provide a state-dependent, geometrically transparent perspective on how spectral complexity and subspace information leakage jointly govern irreversibility in closed quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [129] [New bounds on private simultaneous quantum message passing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12557)
*Uma Girish,Alex May,Natalie Parham,Henry Yuen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究私有同步消息（PSM）模型在量子与经典设置下的上下界。下界方面：1）Nečiporuk测度可下界完美正确性量子PSM所需的纠缠，对显式函数给出二次下界；2）通信矩阵的秩可下界完美隐私但不完美正确性的双向量子PSM，并由此推出经典PSM的新下界。上界方面：1）基于量子电路大小s、深度d_f等参数，给出量子PSM复杂度上界 (kn+s)·log^{O(d_f)}(s/ε)；2）Fourier 1-范数的平方‖f̂‖₁²上界经典PSM复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PSM模型是安全多方计算的最小模型，与布尔函数复杂度及非局域量子计算（NLQC）有紧密联系，但通信和相关成本的界仍不清晰，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 下界：使用Nečiporuk测度和通信矩阵秩方法；上界：基于量子电路深度、T-depth技术推广到多方，以及Fourier分析。

Result: 给出了量子PSM在完美正确性和完美隐私条件下的下界，以及基于电路参数和Fourier范数的上界。

Conclusion: 首次在量子PSM中利用隐私条件得到下界，并将NLQC的T-depth技术从2方推广到k≥2方，同时建立了经典PSM与Fourier范数的联系。

Abstract: In the private simultaneous message (PSM) setting, $k$ players obtain inputs $x_i\in\{0,1\}^n$ and then each send messages to a referee, who should learn $f(x_1,...,x_k)$ but no other information about $(x_1,...,x_k)$. The PSM setting was introduced as a minimal model for secure multiparty computation and has connections to Boolean function complexity. In the quantum setting, PSM has been related to non-local quantum computation (NLQC). The communication and correlation cost of implementing PSM remains poorly understood. Here, we give new upper and lower bounds on the (quantum) PSM model. For lower bounds, we show: 1) Nečiporuk's measure lower bounds the entanglement required for $k$-player quantum PSM with perfect correctness. This leads to quadratic lower bounds for explicit functions. 2) The rank of the communication matrix of $f(x_1,x_2)$ lower bounds 2-player quantum PSM with perfect privacy but imperfect correctness. This implies a previously unknown lower bound on classical PSM with imperfect correctness. When allowing quantum communication and shared entanglement, these are the first lower bounds on quantum PSM that make use of the privacy condition. For upper bounds, we show: 1) Letting $s$ be the size of a quantum circuit computing $f$, $d_f$ be the circuit depth, $k$ the number of players, $n$ the number of bits received by each player, and $ε$ a correctness parameter, we obtain $\mathsf{PSM}_k^*(f) \leq (kn +s) \cdot \log^{O(d_f)}(s/ε)$. 2) The square of the Fourier 1 norm of $f$, $\Vert \hat{f}\Vert_1^2$, upper bounds the classical PSM complexity, $\mathsf{PSM}(f)\leq O(\Vert \hat{f} \Vert^2_1)$. In proving the first upper bound, we generalize existing $T$-depth based techniques for NLQC from $2$ to $k\geq 2$ parties, and consider cases where the Clifford layers are restricted to having small light cones.

</details>


### [130] [Roto-Reflection Geometry of Pure Two-Qubit Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12637)
*Stanislav Filatov,Marcis Auzinsh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现了纯两比特纠缠的几何形式：在Pauli关联张量中，最大纠缠态表现为两个局域Bloch球间的非正常正交映射（旋转反射）。对于部分纠缠纯态，在分离出并发度对应的收缩后，可恢复相同的旋转反射几何，该几何对象称为纠缠旋转反射平面（ERRP），它组织了最大关联方向，为标量纠缠幅度提供了协变几何补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯两比特纠缠通常用并发度等标量量化，但作者认为纠缠也应具有自然的几何形式，旨在揭示纠缠的几何结构，为标量纠缠幅度提供协变几何补充。

Method: 分析Pauli关联张量中最大纠缠态表现为两个局域Bloch球间的非正常正交映射（旋转反射）；对部分纠缠纯态，通过分离并发度对应的收缩恢复旋转反射几何，定义纠缠旋转反射平面（ERRP）。

Result: 最大纠缠态对应旋转反射（roto-reflection）；部分纠缠纯态在分离收缩后恢复相同几何；ERRP组织了两个qubit间的最大关联方向。

Conclusion: 纠缠不仅具有标量度量，还具有自然的旋转反射几何结构，ERRP为纠缠提供了协变几何描述。

Abstract: Pure two-qubit entanglement is usually characterized by scalar quantities such as concurrence. Here we show that it also has a natural geometric form. In the Pauli correlation tensor, maximally entangled states appear as improper orthogonal maps between two local Bloch spheres. These maps are roto-reflections. For partially entangled pure states, the same roto-reflection geometry is recovered after separating the contraction associated with concurrence. We call the corresponding geometric object the Entanglement Roto-Reflection Plane (ERRP). It organizes the maximally correlated directions of the two-qubit state and provides a covariant geometric complement to the scalar magnitude of entanglement.

</details>


### [131] [Supersymmetry of dissipative Bose-Fermi systems with application to Jaynes-Cummings and Dicke models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12682)
*Colin V. Coane,Francesco Iachello*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了如何利用耦合玻色-费米系统哈密顿量的超对称性，将旋转波近似下的Jaynes-Cummings模型和Dicke模型的哈密顿量表示为矩阵形式，并给出其本征值的显式解析解。进一步利用该超对称性将相应的马尔可夫开放系统的刘维尔算符也表示为矩阵形式并求解本征值。这些结果源于Jaynes-Cummings模型哈密顿量与超代数$u(1|1)$的线性Casimir不变量对易，而Dicke模型哈密顿量则与$\sum_{i} u_{i}(1|1)$的线性不变量及额外的$su(2)$代数不变量对易。该方法适用于具有$u(1|1)$及更一般$u(n|m)$动力超代数的各种耦合玻色-费米系统，可为研究更复杂的系统提供有效工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为耦合玻色-费米系统（如Jaynes-Cummings模型和Dicke模型）提供一种利用超对称性将哈密顿量和开放系统的刘维尔算符矩阵化，从而解析求解本征值的方法。

Method: 利用超代数$u(1|1)$的线性Casimir不变量与哈密顿量的对易关系，将旋转波近似下的哈密顿量表示为矩阵形式并解析求解本征值；对于Dicke模型，额外利用了$\sum_{i} u_{i}(1|1)$和$su(2)$代数的对易关系；进一步将超对称性推广到开放系统的刘维尔算符。

Result: 成功将Jaynes-Cummings模型和Dicke模型在旋转波近似下的哈密顿量及相应马尔可夫开放系统的刘维尔算符表示为矩阵形式，并给出了本征值的显式解析解。

Conclusion: 基于超对称性的矩阵化方法不仅适用于$u(1|1)$超代数，还可推广到$u(n|m)$动力超代数，为研究更复杂的耦合玻色-费米系统提供了高效工具。

Abstract: We demonstrate how supersymmetries of Hamiltonians for coupled Bose-Fermi systems can be used to place the Hamiltonians of the Jaynes-Cummings model and Dicke model under the rotating wave approximation in matrix form and provide explicit analytic solutions for their eigenvalues. We then use this supersymmetry to place the Liouvillians of the associated Markovian open systems in matrix form and provide explicit solutions for their eigenvalues. These results are a consequence of the fact that the Hamiltonian of the Jaynes-Cummings model commutes with the linear Casimir invariant of the superalgebra $u(1|1)$ and that the Hamiltonian of the Dicke model commutes both with the linear invariant of $\sum_{i} u_{i}(1|1)$ and with the invariant of an additional $su(2)$ algebra. Our methods apply to various coupled Bose-Fermi systems with $u(1|1)$ and more generally with $u(n|m)$ dynamical superalgebras, and may provide efficient tools for studying more complicated examples.

</details>


### [132] [Multiple Topological Haldane Phases for Symmetry-Protected Quantum Information Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12685)
*João Pedro Gama D'Elia,Irene D'Amico,Thereza Paiva*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自旋-1/2梯子系统分割成多个独立Haldane相子系统的可扩展量子计算架构，利用对称保护拓扑序实现通用量子计算，仅需两个实验上易实现的控制参数即可驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称保护拓扑相因其受保护的边缘态和抗扰动能力在量子信息处理中具有潜在价值，但如何将其应用于实用高效的量子计算仍是一个开放挑战。

Method: 将单个自旋-1/2梯子系统分割为多个独立的Haldane相子系统，在$S^z=0$扇区的两个拓扑态中编码量子比特；利用有限尺寸效应产生的可控能级分裂实现单量子比特旋转（仅需局域磁场）；通过相邻子系统边缘间的Ising型相互作用生成纠缠门。

Result: 证明了该系统仅需两个实验上易实现的控制参数即可驱动通用量子计算。

Conclusion: 展示了对称保护拓扑相可直接用于现实系统中的电路模型量子计算。

Abstract: Symmetry-protected topological phases have attracted significant interest at the fundamental level and as a potential platform for quantum information processing, owing to their protected edge states and resilience to perturbations. Applying these features for practical and efficient quantum computation is highly desirable, but remains an open challenge. Here, we demonstrate the partitioning into multiple independent Haldane phase subsystems of a single spin-1/2 ladder system and propose this as a scalable architecture for gate-based quantum computation, which takes advantage of the symmetry-protected topological order. We encode qubits in the two topological states of the $S^{z}=0$ sector of each subsystem. Finite-size effects, typically viewed as detrimental, instead provide a controllable energy splitting that enables single-qubit rotations using only local magnetic fields. An Ising-type interaction between neighboring subsystem edges generates entangling gates, enabling universal quantum computation driven by two control parameters that are easily accessible experimentally. Our results demonstrate how symmetry-protected topological phases can be directly harnessed for circuit-model quantum computation in realistic systems.

</details>


### [133] [Measurement Geometry for Quantum Random Access Codes: Beyond Nayak Bound and Toward Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12700)
*Seiseki Akibue,Rudy Raymond,Suguru Tamaki,Kosei Teramoto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过将量子随机存取码（QRAC）的最优解码概率转化为非对易测量的谱问题，给出了Nayak bound的一个简化初等证明，并在整个有限尺寸范围内改进了该上界。同时提出了互 unbiased 投影仪值测量（MUPVM）的概念，展示了其在两量子比特QRAC最优性条件下的必要性，以及通过辅助量子比特构造最优QRAC的充分性，并基于MUPVM提出了(M+2,M)-QRAC族的新构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QRAC中Nayak bound是标准的一般上界，但在少量子比特区域数值证据表明存在更强的上界，需要从理论上给出更紧的界并探索其达到条件。

Method: 将QRAC的解码概率形式化为解码测量的谱问题，简化Chernoff界的论证方法证明Nayak bound，并通过精细化论证得到改进的上界。引入MUPVM概念并分析其与QRAC最优性的关系。

Result: 给出Nayak bound的初等证明；在全体有限尺寸范围内改进了Nayak bound；证明了两量子比特QRAC达到猜测最优界时解码测量必为MUPVM；证明任一MUPVM辅助一辅助量子比特可得最优N标度解码概率的QRAC；基于MUPVM构造了(M+2,M)-QRAC族达到猜测最优界。

Conclusion: 谱方法为QRAC最优解码概率提供了统一框架，MUPVM是理解QRAC最优性的关键结构，为构造最优QRAC提供了系统方法。

Abstract: Quantum random access codes (QRACs) ask how well N classical bits can be encoded into M qubits while allowing any single bit to be recovered. Although the Nayak bound remains the standard general upper bound on the decoding probability, numerical evidence suggests a stronger upper bound in the small-qubit regime.
  In this work, we formulate the optimal decoding probability in terms of decoding measurements, reformulating QRAC design as a spectral problem for noncommuting measurements. Using this formulation, we give an elementary proof of the Nayak bound by simplifying the Chernoff-bound argument. Moreover, we refine the argument to obtain upper bounds that improve over Nayak's bound in the entire finite-size regime.
  The equality conditions of our bounds justify defining mutually unbiased projector-valued measurements (MUPVMs), a generalization of mutually unbiased bases. We show that decoding measurement of any two-qubit QRAC attaining the conjectured bound must form MUPVMs. We also show that any MUPVM, assisted by one ancillary qubit, yields a QRAC with optimal N-scaling decoding probability. Finally, we propose a new MUPVM-based construction for the (M+2,M)-QRAC family attaining the conjectured bound.

</details>


### [134] [Block algebra for morphing circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12724)
*Rui Chao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了四种基于CNOT的CSS形态电路构造，通过块代数符号和置换矩阵的群正则表示实现，其中三种源自对已有表面码和颜色码形态电路的重写，第四种是基于6.6.6颜色码的新型三回合构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形态电路作为一种新的量子纠错范式，能够放宽硬件要求，但现有构造缺乏系统性和明确的量子比特连接度描述。

Method: 采用块代数符号，以置换矩阵生成的代数为条目，通过有限群的正则表示对置换矩阵进行数值搜索实例化。对表面码和颜色码的已有形态电路进行重写，并基于6.6.6颜色码设计新的三回合构造。

Result: 给出了四种显式具有量子比特连接度规格的CNOT-based CSS形态电路构造，其中表面码构造恢复了[ST25]中两区块群代数码的形态电路。

Conclusion: 本文工作系统化了形态电路的代数构造方法，并为有限群在量子纠错中的应用提供了新途径。

Abstract: Morphing circuits are a new paradigm for quantum error correction that relaxes hardware requirements. We present four constructions for CNOT-based CSS morphing circuits with explicit qubit connectivity degrees. All four constructions are specified in block algebra notation, with entries in algebras generated by permutation matrices. The first three are obtained by rewriting existing surface- and color-code morphing circuits; the fourth is a new three-round construction modeled on the 6.6.6 color code. The surface-code construction recovers the morphing circuit of Ref. [ST25] for two-block group algebra codes. Numerical search then instantiates these permutation matrices using regular representations of finite groups.
  [ST25] M. H. Shaw and B. M. Terhal, Phys. Rev. Lett. 134(9), 090602 (2025).

</details>


### [135] [Stable, bidirectional electro-optic transduction in thin film lithium tantalate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12726)
*Christopher J. Axline,Stephan Gamper,Phoebe M. Tengdin,Moritz Businger,Guilhem Alma,Marina A. Roquet,Nicola Brusadin,Robin Giroud,Luis G. Villanueva*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次在薄膜铌酸锂(TFLT)平台上实现了集成电光微波-光学换能器，展示了可扩展、高稳定性的双向相干转换，单光子耦合率约1 kHz，连续多天稳定运行，为分布式量子计算和异构量子网络提供了关键使能技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效且稳定的微波-光学换能是实现分布式超导量子计算和异构量子网络的关键使能技术。现有基于薄膜铌酸锂(TFLN)的电光换能器虽展现出前景，但受限于低频偏置漂移、低效率、制造复杂性和可扩展性等问题。

Method: 采用薄膜铌酸锂(TFLT)平台，利用其与TFLN相当的Pockels非线性以及改进的偏置稳定性和高功率处理能力；通过晶圆级深紫外光刻制造与可调光子分子光学谐振腔耦合的超导微波谐振腔，实现每晶圆数百个器件的高通量生产。

Result: 在六个器件上观察到C波段光子与4.9-5.5 GHz微波光子之间的相干双向转换，片上效率与理论一致，单光子耦合率g_0/2π约1 kHz；在静态偏置场下实现多天连续运行；在脉冲泵浦下，最高效率时100微秒脉冲的添加噪声小于一个光子。

Conclusion: TFLT被确立为一种可扩展且鲁棒的电光平台，适用于未来的量子互连和模块化量子处理器。

Abstract: Efficient and stable microwave-optical transduction is a key enabling technology for distributed superconducting quantum computing and heterogeneous quantum networks. Electro-optic transducers based on thin-film lithium niobate (TFLN) have shown strong promise, but demonstrations to date have been limited by various factors such as low frequency bias drift, low efficiency, fabrication complexity, and scalability. Here we demonstrate the first integrated electro-optic microwave-optical transducers realized in thin-film lithium tantalate (TFLT), a material platform offering Pockels nonlinearity comparable to TFLN together with improved bias stability and high-power handling. We fabricate superconducting microwave resonators coupled to tunable photonic-molecule optical resonators using wafer-scale deep ultraviolet lithography, offering high-throughput production of hundreds of devices per wafer. Across six devices we observe coherent bidirectional conversion between C-band optical photons and 4.9-5.5 GHz microwave photons, with measured on-chip efficiencies and inferred single-photon coupling rates g_0/2π ~ 1 kHz consistent with theory. Continuous operation over multiple days is achieved using a static bias field with minimal feedback, demonstrating a major operational advantage. We further characterize optical loss statistics, microwave resonator performance, and optically induced added noise under pulsed pumping, finding less than one added photon for 100 microsecond pulses at the highest measured efficiencies. These results establish TFLT as a scalable and robust electro-optic platform for future quantum interconnects and modular quantum processors.

</details>


### [136] [Certifying Nonclassical Proper-Time Histories with a Quantum Clock](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12755)
*Shuai Zeng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子时钟在相对论性相位下的非经典性认证问题。作者证明任何两能级单时间退相信号都允许经典随机固有时表示，并提出基于Choi秩分离准则的条件相干历史重组判据，通过两分支Ramsey协议实现亮/暗端口布居数见证，从而区分经典混合与量子相干固有时历史。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分量子时钟因运动或引力固有时差获得的相对论性相位是否真正源于非经典固有时历史，而非经典随机过程的模拟，需要建立严格的通道认证框架。

Method: 将问题形式化为通道认证问题；证明两能级单时间退相信号存在经典随机固有时表示；定义实验指定固有时历史的经典混合凸集；推导Choi秩分离准则用于条件相干历史重组；设计两分支Ramsey协议给出亮/暗端口布居数见证。

Result: 发现任何两能级单时间退相信号（包括有效量子固有时标签产生的信号）都允许经典随机固有时表示；建立了Choi秩分离准则作为非经典性判据；两分支Ramsey协议可产生超出经典混合集的布居数信号。

Conclusion: 该认证是操作性的且相对于指定历史集：它排除了相同实现固有时历史的经典混合，而非任意具有不同历史或控制的经典协议。

Abstract: Quantum clocks can acquire relativistic phases from motional or gravitational proper-time differences, but reduced clock dephasing alone does not certify nonclassical proper-time histories. We formulate this distinction as a channel-certification problem. First, we show that any two-level single-time dephasing signal, including one generated by an effective quantum proper-time label, admits a classical random proper-time representation. We then define the convex set of classical mixtures of experimentally specified proper-time histories and prove a Choi-rank separation criterion for conditioned coherent history recombination. A two-branch Ramsey protocol gives explicit bright- and dark-port population witnesses outside this classical set. The certification is operational and relative to the specified history set: it rules out classical mixtures of the same implemented proper-time histories, not arbitrary classical protocols with different histories or controls.

</details>


### [137] [Instabilities in a Non-KAM System via Information Scrambling: A Note](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12761)
*Naga Dileep Varikuti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量化非KAM系统中算子增长与共振结构的关系，以受驱谐振子为例，发现共振处OTOC呈二次时间增长（远离共振为线性增长），且OTOC行为受欧拉函数调控的数论结构控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非KAM系统中（经典Lyapunov指数小、无传统混沌）共振结构如何影响算子增长和信息扩散，弥补KAM框架无法覆盖的退化系统研究空白。

Method: 采用OTOC作为诊断工具，对受驱谐振子进行微扰分析，推导共振与非共振条件下OTOC的封闭表达式，并揭示其与数论函数（欧拉函数）的关联。

Result: 共振处OTOC呈二次时间增长，而非共振处为线性增长；OTOC行为由频率比的欧拉函数控制，呈现数论结构。

Conclusion: 共振结构在非KAM系统中对信息扩散起重要调控作用，OTOC能敏感捕捉这些结构变化。

Abstract: We study operator growth in quantized non-KAM systems using out-of-time-ordered correlators (OTOCs), focusing on the kicked harmonic oscillator as a representative example. Since the classical harmonic oscillator is degenerate, the dynamics fall outside the usual Kolmogorov-Arnold-Moser (KAM) framework, and resonances play a central role in shaping the phase space. We examine the system near resonances, where the ratio between the oscillator and driving frequencies takes integer values. Even though the classical Lyapunov exponent remains small at these points, and hence no conventional chaos, the phase space still undergoes strong structural changes. The OTOCs are particularly sensitive to these resonances, with a quadratic-in-time growth at resonance compared to linear growth away from it. Within a perturbative treatment, we derive closed-form expressions for the OTOCs and uncover a number-theoretic structure emerging in the behavior of OTOCs, governed by the Euler totient function of the frequency ratio. Overall, the results we present in this short note imply that resonant structures can play an important role in controlling information spreading.

</details>


### [138] [Matrix phase-space representations for quantum symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12769)
*Peter D. Drummond,Alexander S. Dellios,Margaret D. Reid*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种矩阵相空间方法，通过在基展开中融入全局量子对称性，将基投影到约化希尔伯特空间，从而大幅减少多体量子模拟的采样误差，并统一了多种先前的相空间方法。论文提供了基本定理和算符恒等式的详细证明，并以高斯玻色采样（GBS）量子计算机输出验证为例，展示了利用宇称对称性可将采样误差降低极大倍数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相空间方法在模拟多体量子系统时存在较大采样误差，且缺乏统一的框架来系统性地利用量子对称性来减少误差。

Method: 提出矩阵相空间表示法，在基展开中融入全局量子对称性，将基投影到约化希尔伯特空间；提供基本定理和算符恒等式的严格证明；以GBS验证问题为例推导应用细节。

Result: 该方法统一了多种先前的相空间方法；在GBS验证任务中，利用宇称对称性使采样误差相比先前方法降低了极大倍数。

Conclusion: 矩阵相空间方法通过引入对称性约化能够有效降低多体量子模拟的采样误差，具有广泛的应用前景，尤其适合具有指数复杂度的量子计算验证问题。

Abstract: We introduce a general phase-space representation that includes global quantum symmetries in the basis expansion. This method, called matrix phase-space, projects the basis onto a reduced Hilbert space, which can greatly reduce sampling errors of many-body quantum simulations and unifies several previous phase-space methods. The purpose of this paper is to provide detailed proofs of basic theorems and operator identities. We also treat several different types of symmetries. To illustrate the benefits of matrix phase-space methods, we give a detailed derivation of a recent application to the topical problem of verifying the outputs of Gaussian boson sampling (GBS) quantum computers with photon number resolving detectors. This has exponential complexity, and using parity symmetry reduces sampling errors by very large factors relative to earlier methods.

</details>


### [139] [Explicit Quantum Circuit Simulation of Nonlinear 1-Dimensional Fluid with Carleman-linearized Boltzmann Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12770)
*Keita Kanno,Kazumasa Ueno,Hayato Higuchi,Morimasa Okamoto,Yuya Yoshizuru,Ryoya Ishimaru,Towa Takagi,Kentaro Sakamoto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过二阶Carleman线性化将一维玻尔兹曼方程的非线性项纳入量子计算框架，并利用基于量子奇异值变换的泰勒展开ODE求解器在显式量子电路模拟中实现了最终态的制备。门和量子比特复杂度随网格尺寸、高阶Carleman线性化呈对数标度，为工业级量子CFD提供了成本基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体动力学的量子计算作为容错量子计算机的关键应用日益受到关注，但此前仅在线性情况下实现了显式高效的基元门级电路模拟，非线性情况尚未有显式量子电路模拟的演示。

Method: 采用二阶Carleman线性化处理一维玻尔兹曼方程的非线性项，结合基于量子奇异值变换（QSVT）的泰勒展开ODE求解器，通过显式量子电路模拟制备最终态。

Result: 门的复杂度和量子比特数随网格尺寸、高阶Carleman线性化呈对数标度增长；分析了高阶展开在泰勒ODE求解器中的实用价值。

Conclusion: 该构造为计算成本降低和进一步扩展（如高维、复杂几何、物理量提取）提供了具体基线，推动了面向工业应用的量子CFD发展。

Abstract: Quantum computation of fluid dynamics has attracted growing attention as a key application of fault-tolerant quantum computers anticipated in the coming decade, with lattice Boltzmann methods emerging as a particularly promising approach. Explicit and efficient elementary-gate-level circuit simulations, however, have so far been demonstrated only in the linear case. Here we include the leading nonlinearity through second-order Carleman linearization of the one-dimensional Boltzmann equation, and demonstrate, via explicit quantum-circuit simulation, the preparation of the final-time state using a Taylor-expansion-based ODE solver based on the quantum singular value transformation. With this construction, we analyze the gate and qubit complexities, which scale logarithmically with the grid size, the nonlinearity captured by the higher-order Carleman linearization, and the practical utility of higher-order expansions in the Taylor ODE solver. The construction provides a concrete baseline for computational cost reduction and further developments such as extensions to higher dimensions, complex geometries, and the extraction of physical quantities, towards industrially useful quantum CFD.

</details>


### [140] [Quantum Network Routing based on Surface Code Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12781)
*Tianjie Hu,Jindi Wu,Qun Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: SurfNet 是一种结合量子纠缠和直接传输优势的量子网络，采用表面码作为逻辑量子位编码消息，并通过两条并行通信通道容错传输表面码，利用新型网络架构和纠错解码器显著提高了通信保真度和网络吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络面临信道噪声和擦除错误，现有两类网络（基于纠缠的量子隐形传态和直接传输）各有局限，难以同时实现高可靠性和高效率。

Method: 提出SurfNet网络，使用表面码作为逻辑量子位编码消息；采用两条并行通信通道容错传输每个表面码；设计新型网络架构以更好集成表面码；提出新型纠错解码器以充分利用表面码的模块化特性。

Result: 仿真结果表明，SurfNet及其解码器显著提升了量子网络中的通信保真度。

Conclusion: SurfNet通过表面码和双通道并行传输，能够及时纠正操作错误和光子丢失错误，同时提高网络吞吐量，为构建高可靠、高效率的量子网络提供了一种有效方案。

Abstract: Quantum networks encounter unavoidable channel noises and erasure errors, presenting a huge obstacle in designing protocols that attain both high reliability and efficiency. Typically, quantum networks fall into two categories: those utilize quantum entanglements for quantum teleportation, and those directly transfer the actual quantum messages. In this paper, we present SurfNet, a quantum network that inherits the main advantages from both categories. It employs surface codes as logical qubits for encoding messages, and utilizes two parallel communication channels to fault-tolerantly transfer each surface code in a modular manner. Our approach of using surface codes can timely correct both operational and photon loss errors within the network, and the integration of the two channels within the network can greatly improve network throughput. For the implementation of SurfNet, we propose a novel network architecture, designed to better integrate surface codes into quantum networks. We also propose a novel error correction decoder, designed to fully utilize the modular characteristic of surface codes within our network. Simulation results demonstrate that SurfNet with its decoder significantly enhances the communication fidelity within quantum networks.

</details>


### [141] [Beyond-Third-Order Quantum Coherence in Two-Dimensional Spectroscopy via Order-Selective Isolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12794)
*Xue Zhang,De-Ran Zhang,Hui Dong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种计算辅助策略，结合旋转框架采集与帧移追踪算法，在二维光谱学中实现高阶非线性信号（如7阶）与低阶背景（如3阶）的分离，无需传统相位循环的复杂操作，并在铷蒸气实验中成功验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性光谱学中，高阶弱信号常被低阶强信号光谱重叠掩盖，传统相位循环方案随阶数增加复杂度急剧上升，成为二维光谱学扩展至高阶的瓶颈。

Method: 采用旋转框架采集结合帧移追踪算法，利用信号在框架中的依赖性产生不同的光谱位移，从而区分不同阶次的非线性贡献。

Result: 在铷蒸气实验中成功将7阶非线性信号从共存的3阶成分中分离出来，无需牺牲高脉冲强度操作，直接获取了高阶量子相干动力学信息。

Conclusion: 该方法与多维光谱平台广泛兼容，为探测超越三阶的多体和集体超快动力学提供了实用的技术路线。

Abstract: A central challenge in nonlinear spectroscopy is the order-selective readout of weak higher-order responses that spectrally overlap with dominant lower-order signals. This bottleneck is particularly severe in two-dimensional (2D) spectroscopy, where extending conventional phase-cycling schemes to higher orders rapidly increases measurement and analysis complexity. Here we introduce a computation-assisted strategy that combines rotating-frame acquisition with a frame-shift tracking algorithm to separate signals by their frame-dependent spectral shifts. In a rubidium vapor experiment, we use this approach to isolate a 7th-order nonlinear contribution from coexisting 3rd-order components, enabling direct access to higher-order quantum-coherence dynamics without sacrificing operation at comparatively high pulse intensities. The method is broadly compatible with multidimensional spectroscopy platforms and provides a practical route to probing many-body and collective ultrafast dynamics beyond third order.

</details>


### [142] [Quantum Reservoir Computing for Short-Term Power Load Forecasting in Resource-Constrained Energy Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12806)
*Mansi Od,Param Pathak,Nouhaila Innan,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种硬件高效的量子储层计算（QRC）框架用于短期负荷预测，通过固定量子储层提取时序特征，仅训练经典Elastic Net读出层，并采用后训练定点量化压缩读出层至6位精度，在保持全精度性能的同时减少81.2%的读出内存，且可在真实硬件噪声下直接迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备部署短期负荷预测模型面临有限内存、有限测量预算和硬件噪声等实际约束，现有方法难以兼顾精度与资源效率。

Method: 采用固定量子储层将时间窗输入映射为高维特征，仅训练经典Elastic Net读出层；在后训练阶段对读出层进行定点量化压缩（8位至2位）；在Tetouan和Spain数据集上分别采用精确态矢量模拟、512次有限采样模拟以及IBM FakeTorino/FakeMarrakesh真实硬件噪声模型进行验证。

Result: 6位读出精度可保持全精度预测性能，读入内存减少81.2%；低于6位时性能退化具有数据集依赖性（Tetouan更敏感，Spain更平缓）；训练好的读出层可直接迁移到噪声量子储层状态上，无需重新训练。

Conclusion: 量化QRC是一种资源感知的短期负荷预测方法，适用于近期的量子时间序列应用。

Abstract: Short-term load forecasting is essential for reliable energy management, but practical deployment on edge devices requires models that remain accurate under limited memory, finite measurement budgets, and hardware noise. This work proposes a hardware-efficient Quantum Reservoir Computing (QRC) framework for energy load forecasting, where a fixed quantum reservoir transforms temporal input windows into high-dimensional features and only a classical Elastic Net readout is trained. To reduce deployment cost, the trained readout is compressed using post-training fixed-point quantization at bit widths from 8 to 2 bits. The framework is evaluated on the Tetouan and Spain energy load datasets under exact statevector simulation, 512-shot finite sampling, and realistic hardware-noise models from IBM FakeTorino and IBM FakeMarrakesh. Results show that 6-bit readout precision preserves full-precision forecasting performance while reducing readout memory by 81.2%. Below this point, degradation becomes dataset dependent, with Tetouan showing stronger sensitivity and Spain degrading more gradually. Hardware-noise validation further shows that the trained readout transfers to noisy reservoir states without retraining. These findings support quantized QRC as a resource-aware forecasting approach for near-term quantum time-series applications.

</details>


### [143] [Accidental Symmetry in the Tavis-Cummings Model via the Schwinger Boson Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12813)
*Plato Deliyannis,Iman Marvian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现并分析了Tavis-Cummings (TC) Hamiltonians（多量子比特-单玻色子耦合系统）中一个先前未被注意到的"偶然对称性"，证明该对称性对n>2量子比特的可实现酉变换施加了强约束，并解释了其起源及对量子计算的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TC模型是量子光学和超导量子计算中的基本模型，一般认为其对称性仅为置换不变性和U(1)对称性。识别额外隐藏对称性对于理解系统的可控性和量子计算应用至关重要。

Method: 通过构建守恒可观测量来识别该偶然对称性，分析其对酉变换的约束，利用Schwinger玻色子角动量表示解释对称性起源，并考察$J_z$和$J_z^2$哈密顿量对该对称性的影响。

Result: 发现n>2量子比特TC哈密顿量存在一个独立于已知对称性的额外对称性，该对称性在添加全局$J_z$哈密顿量时仍存在，但加入$J_z^2$后被移除，尽管$J_z^2$保留了置换不变性和U(1)对称性。

Conclusion: 该偶然对称性严重限制了TC系统的可控性，对量子计算应用具有重要影响，相关讨论在姊妹论文中进一步展开。

Abstract: The Jaynes-Cummings (JC) Hamiltonian is a paradigmatic model of light-matter interaction and, more generally, qubit-boson interactions, widely used across atomic, optical, and superconducting qubit platforms. In the multi-qubit setting, where n qubits are identically coupled to a single boson mode, this interaction is known as the Tavis-Cummings (TC) Hamiltonian. The structure of the TC model is usually understood in terms of two standard symmetries: permutation invariance of the qubits and a U(1) symmetry associated with conservation of the total excitation number. Here we identify an additional, independent "accidental" symmetry of the TC Hamiltonian and construct the corresponding conserved observable. We show that, for n>2 qubits, this symmetry imposes strong constraints on the realizable unitary transformations. These constraints persist in the presence of the global $J_z$ Hamiltonian, but are removed by adding $J_z^2$, even though $J_z^2$ preserves both permutation invariance and the U(1) symmetry. Finally, we explain the origin of this previously unnoticed symmetry using Schwinger's boson representation of angular momentum. These restrictions have important implications for controllability of the TC system and for its applications to quantum computing, which are investigated further in a companion paper.

</details>


### [144] [Graph Reinforcement Learning for Calibration-Aware Quantum Circuit Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12816)
*Yash Vardhan Tomar,Dheeraj Peddireddy,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用同日IBM Heron r2校准数据的图强化学习量子线路路由方法，通过近端策略优化训练策略，在MQT Bench九个电路上平均精确保真度达0.727，优于SABRE-best20的0.440和目标感知SABRE的0.481。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准开销指标下看似高效的路由在穿过校准不佳的耦合器时仍会损失保真度，因此需要校准感知的路由方法。

Method: 采用图强化学习路由器，利用同日IBM Heron r2校准数据选择硬件边SWAP操作，使用近端策略优化训练策略。

Result: 平均精确保真度0.727，高于SABRE-best20的0.440和目标感知SABRE的0.481；保真度增益集中在5q和8q电路系列，但在固定树动作图下所有10q系列不如SABRE-best20。

Conclusion: 校准感知的机器学习路由能够在门计数驱动的编译之上进一步提升保真度。

Abstract: Quantum circuit routing is a key step in compiling programs for noisy intermediate-scale quantum processors. Routes that appear efficient by standard overhead metrics can still lose fidelity when they pass through poorly calibrated couplers. We study a calibration-aware graph reinforcement-learning router that uses same-day IBM Heron r2 calibration data to choose hardware-edge SWAPs. We train the policy with proximal policy optimization and evaluate it with exact simulated fidelity across nine Munich Quantum Toolkit (MQT) Bench circuits and three calibration snapshots. Across these evaluations, pooled mean exact fidelity is $0.727$, compared with $0.440$ for SABRE-best20 and $0.481$ for target-aware SABRE. Fidelity gains come with higher routed two-qubit counts and are concentrated in the 5q and 8q circuit families; under the fixed tree action graph, all 10q families favor SABRE-best20. Overall, our results show that calibration-aware learned routing can improve fidelity beyond gate-count-driven compilation.

</details>


### [145] [Quantum walk-based optimisation for capacitated vehicle routing with homogeneous and heterogeneous fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12856)
*Edric Matwiejew,Aidan Smith,Callum Neill,Paulo Santos,Ugo Varetto,Jingbo Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于量子游走的优化算法(QWOA)来解决带容量约束的车辆路径问题(CVRP)，通过在产品空间上执行连续时间量子游走，将每层门复杂度从O(n³log n)降低到O(n²log n)，并在最多8个客户和3辆车的实例上展示了更优的收敛性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CVRP因其组合复杂性和实际重要性成为量子优化的理想候选，但该问题的约束搜索空间对量子算法构成挑战，需要更高效的量子优化方法。

Method: 提出一种量子游走优化算法(QWOA)，在同质或异质车队场景下，通过在与CVRP解空间固有组合结构一致的产品空间上执行连续时间量子游走，并采用固定数量经典参数生成的电路参数化调度方案。

Result: 将每层门复杂度从O(n³log n)降低到O(n²log n)；在最多8个客户和3辆车的精确态矢量模拟中，以显著更少的目标函数评估次数实现更好的低代价解收敛，且优势随问题规模增大而扩大。

Conclusion: 结构化的产品空间量子游走是解决约束组合空间优化问题的一种有前景的工具。

Abstract: The capacitated vehicle routing problem (CVRP) is an appealing candidate for quantum optimisation due to its combinatorial complexity and practical importance. However, the problem's constrained search space poses a challenge for such quantum algorithms. We introduce a quantum walk-based optimisation algorithm (QWOA) for the CVRP with homogeneous or heterogeneous vehicle fleets, addressing this challenge through a continuous-time quantum walk over a product space that coincides with combinatorial structures intrinsic to the CVRP solution space. Relative to the prior QWOA-based formulation, this approach reduces the per-layer gate complexity from $\mathcal{O}(n^{3}\log n)$ to $\mathcal{O}(n^{2}\log n)$ and supports a circuit parameterisation schedule generated by a fixed number of classical parameters. Exact state-vector simulation on instances with up to $n=8$ customers and $K=3$ vehicles demonstrates improved convergence to low-cost solutions using markedly fewer objective function evaluations, with the advantage broadening as problem size increases. These results identify structured product-space walks as a promising tool for optimisation over constrained combinatorial spaces.

</details>


### [146] [Quantum Otto engine powered by an anisotropic Heisenberg XYZ model under independent local magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12877)
*Meiru Li,Maimaitiyiming Tusun,Fang Zhao,Hasiyatihan Abudoula,Tongcheng Wei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了一个以各向异性两量子比特海森堡XYZ模型为工质的量子奥托热机，系统分析了纵向耦合、各向异性、横向耦合和局域磁场对净功输出和效率的影响，发现降低纵向耦合可显著提升功和效率，各向异性存在最优值，两量子比特因不对称场和相互作用扮演不同热力学角色，纠缠变化与效率提升密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究各向异性两量子比特海森堡XYZ模型作为工质的量子奥托热机性能，揭示纵向耦合、各向异性、横向耦合和局域磁场等参数对热机功输出和效率的影响机制，以及量子纠缠在循环过程中的作用。

Method: 以各向异性两量子比特海森堡XYZ模型为工质，构建量子奥托热机循环，通过独立局域磁场分别控制每个自旋，系统扫描纵向耦合、各向异性参数、横向耦合和局域磁场等参数，计算净功输出和效率，并进行局域功分析和纠缠（并发度）演化分析。

Result: 降低纵向耦合显著提高最大功和峰值效率；各向异性参数存在最优值使性能最佳；由于不对称局域磁场和自旋-自旋相互作用，两量子比特热力学角色不同，相互作用项本身对总功有重要贡献；热和冷等磁冲程之间纠缠的显著变化与效率提升密切关联。

Conclusion: 本研究为量子奥托热机的运行原理和控制提供了新见解，揭示了通过调控自旋耦合参数和各向异性来优化热机性能的途径，并建立了纠缠变化与热机效率之间的关联。

Abstract: We study a quantum Otto heat engine whose working substance is an anisotropic two-qubit Heisenberg XYZ model. Independent local magnetic fields are used to control each spin individually. The influence of the longitudinal coupling, anisotropy, transverse coupling, and local fields on the net work output and efficiency is systematically examined. Reducing the longitudinal coupling is found to markedly improve both the maximum work and the peak efficiency. The engine performance reaches an optimum at a particular value of the anisotropy parameter. A local work analysis clarifies how work is produced during the cycle. Because of the asymmetric local fields and the intrinsic spin-spin interaction, the two qubits play markedly different thermodynamic roles; the interaction term itself contributes crucially to the total work. We further analyze the variation of quantum entanglement, quantified by concurrence, along the cycle. The results indicate that a pronounced change in entanglement between the hot and cold isomagnetic strokes is closely correlated with the efficiency enhancement. This work offers new insight into the operating principles and control of quantum Otto heat engines.

</details>


### [147] [Global Control with the Tavis-Cummings Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12906)
*Plato Deliyannis,Iman Marvian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在全局控制下，通过Tavis-Cummings相互作用和全局z场J_z控制多量子比特与单玻色模耦合系统的可控性。发现对于n>2量子比特，可实现酉变换受限于TC哈密顿量的“偶然”对称性；而加入J_z²项可打破该对称性，实现半普适性——即能实现满足置换对称和U(1)对称的任意酉变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在全局控制（所有量子比特接受相同控制脉冲）条件下，多量子比特与玻色模耦合系统的可控性边界，识别限制可实现酉操作的对称性因素。

Method: 对Tavis-Cummings相互作用与全局z场J_z生成的李代数进行对称性分析，识别“偶然”对称性；随后引入J_z²项打破该对称性，刻画可实现的酉变换群结构。

Result: 发现n>2量子比特时存在一个不同于标准U(1)和置换对称的“偶然”对称性限制了可控性；加入J_z²后系统达到半普适性，可实现满足置换对称和U(1)对称的任意酉变换。

Conclusion: 全局控制下TC系统的可控性受偶然对称性约束，但通过引入J_z²可突破该限制，为量子计算中的全局控制方案提供理论指导。

Abstract: We study the controllability of a system of qubits under global control, where control pulses act identically on all qubits. Specifically, we consider a collection of qubits identically coupled to a single bosonic mode, or harmonic oscillator, via the Jaynes-Cummings interaction. This collective coupling, known as the Tavis-Cummings (TC) interaction, has been realized in several quantum computing platforms, including superconducting and atomic qubit systems. Although the qubits do not interact directly with one another, they can become entangled through their common coupling to the bosonic mode. We characterize the group of unitaries that can be implemented on the joint Hilbert space of the qubits and bosonic mode using the TC interaction together with a global $z$ field $J_z$, corresponding to identical z rotations on all qubits. We show that for n>2 qubits the set of realizable unitaries is restricted by an "accidental" symmetry of the TC Hamiltonian, distinct from its "standard" U(1) and permutational symmetries. On the other hand, we find that the Hamiltonian $J_z^2$ breaks this accidental symmetry and, together with the TC interaction and $J_z$, achieves semi-universality: it allows the implementation of arbitrary unitaries that respect permutational and U(1) symmetry, up to certain constraints on the center of the group. In a companion paper, we further analyze this remarkable accidental symmetry and show that it can be understood through Schwinger's bosonic model of angular momentum.

</details>


### [148] [Non-Hermitian skin effect induced by spatial noncommutativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12961)
*Zheng Wei,Su-Peng Kou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现当两个空间坐标不对易时，仅沿一个方向施加增益-耗散势即可自动在另一方向产生非互易性，导致所有本征态在边界指数堆积，称为非对易趋肤效应。其逆趋肤长度与非对易参数成正比，且存在精确解析公式；正弦虚势下所有本征态趋肤方向在纯几何决定的参数点集体翻转，可作为非对易参数的零交叉测量方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非厄米趋肤效应中，驱动趋肤局域化的非厄米成分（如非对称跳跃或增益-耗散）总是需要人为地沿趋肤方向作为独立模型参数引入。本文旨在突破这一范式，探索当空间坐标非对易时，能否仅通过一个方向的增益-耗散势自动诱导另一方向上的非互易性，从而产生趋肤效应。

Method: 理论推导了非对易趋肤效应的解析公式，证明逆趋肤长度正比于非对易参数；通过格点模型的精确对角化进行数值验证；利用虚势的反射对称性给出效应存在与否的精确判据；分析了正弦虚势下趋肤方向集体翻转的几何条件，并提出零交叉测量方案。

Result: 建立了非对易趋肤效应的完整理论框架，得到了热力学极限下逆趋肤长度的精确解析公式；数值模拟与理论一致；发现所有本征态趋肤方向在纯几何决定的参数点集体翻转，翻转点与势强度无关；该效应可作为空间非对易性的非微扰探针。

Conclusion: 非对易趋肤效应突破了传统非厄米趋肤效应的范式，为探测空间非对易性提供了新方法；其Peierls相位结构在冷原子合成维度、光子谐振器和拓扑电路中原则上可实现。

Abstract: In all known schemes for the non-Hermitian skin effect, the non-Hermitian ingredient that drives the skin localization, whether asymmetric hopping or gain and loss, is invariably introduced by hand as an independent model parameter along the skin direction. Here we show that when two spatial coordinates do not commute, the skin effect can break free of this paradigm: a gain-loss potential applied along one coordinate automatically generates non-reciprocity along the other through the coordinate noncommutativity, driving all eigenstates to pile up exponentially at a boundary. We term this phenomenon the noncommutative skin effect. The inverse skin length is proportional to the noncommutativity parameter and is given by an analytic formula, exact in the thermodynamic limit and verified by exact diagonalization of lattice models; the reflection symmetry of the imaginary potential furnishes an exact criterion for the presence or absence of the effect, valid rigorously for finite-size systems. For a sinusoidal imaginary potential, the skin direction of all eigenstates flips collectively at parameter points fixed purely by geometry. Because the flip point is independent of the potential strength, the reversal constitutes a zero-crossing measurement scheme intrinsically robust against systematic errors, from which the noncommutativity parameter can be extracted directly. The qualitative transition of the eigenstates from uniform to exponentially localized renders the effect a nonperturbative probe of spatial noncommutativity, and the Peierls-phase structure of its lattice model is in principle accessible to cold-atom synthetic dimensions, photonic resonators, and topolectrical circuits.

</details>


### [149] [Quantum-Driven Neuromorphic Computing for Million-Qubit-Scale Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12968)
*Adams Ivanov,Samer Rahmeh,Erick Giovani Sperandio Nascimento,Daniela Herrmann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍Apollo，一个在16nm混合信号CMOS中制造的10000节点p-qubit神经形态处理器，室温运行，模拟核心功耗约0.5W。其基本单元p-qubit是利用集成量子熵单元注入真正量子衍生随机性的双稳态随机单元，实现超快随机转换。通过Hyperion 256互连拓扑高效嵌入稠密Ising和QUBO问题，并通过Suzuki-Trotter对应复现横向场量子退火的关键特性，无需低温冷却。在三维自旋玻璃基准上，Apollo达到比低温量子退火硬件更低的基态能量，且不同于经典模拟退火和模拟量子退火。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子退火硬件需要低温冷却、长寿命相干和微波控制，扩展性受限；同时稀疏退火平台嵌入稠密优化问题时存在大量minor embedding开销。本研究旨在开发一种室温运行、工业可扩展的量子驱动能量优化平台。

Method: 采用16nm混合信号CMOS工艺制造Apollo处理器，核心p-qubit为双稳态随机单元，利用集成量子熵源注入真正量子随机性驱动连续时间状态涨落。结合Hyperion 256高维互连拓扑，通过Suzuki-Trotter对应实现无需低温的量子退火动力学模拟。

Result: 在300个无序样本的三维自旋玻璃基准测试中，Apollo达到比低温量子退火硬件更低的基态能量，且性能与经典模拟退火和模拟量子退火存在显著差异。350nm验证芯片实验确认了核心p-qubit动力学和热力学采样正确性。

Conclusion: Apollo被确立为一个室温运行、工业可扩展的量子驱动能量优化平台，可用于能量基优化、概率推理、生成建模和混合经典量子工作流。

Abstract: We introduce Apollo, a 10000 node p-qubit neuromorphic processor fabricated in 16 nm mixed signal CMOS and operating fully at room temperature with a typical analog core power envelope of about 0.5 W. Its fundamental element, the p-qubit, is a bistable stochastic unit whose continuous time state fluctuations are driven by integrated quantum entropy units that inject true quantum derived randomness. This enables ultrafast stochastic transitions at low energy while preserving a classical state representation. Apollo combines these p-qubits with a high degree Hyperion 256 interconnect topology, allowing efficient embedding of dense Ising and QUBO problems with substantially reduced minor embedding overhead compared with sparse annealing platforms. We show that, through the Suzuki Trotter correspondence, the equilibrium statistics and annealing dynamics of the p-qubit network reproduce key properties of transverse field quantum annealing without cryogenic cooling, long lived coherence, or microwave control. Beyond device level validation, Apollo is evaluated on a three dimensional spin glass benchmark previously used to study quantum advantage in superconducting annealers. Across 300 disorder realizations, Apollo reaches substantially lower ground state energies than reported cryogenic quantum annealing hardware, while remaining distinct from classical simulated annealing and simulated quantum annealing. A 350 nm release candidate device experimentally validates the core p-qubit dynamics, thermodynamic sampling correctness, and continuous time annealing behavior. These results establish Apollo as a room temperature, industrially scalable platform for quantum driven energy based optimization, probabilistic inference, generative modeling, and hybrid classical quantum workflows.

</details>


### [150] [Experiment-compatible measurement--feedback quantum state preparation with reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13005)
*Xiaotian Nie,Tao Zhang,Linghui Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于部分可观测强化学习的自适应测量-反馈协议，仅利用实验可访问的测量历史来选择测量算符和反馈动作，用于量子基态制备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基态制备方案要么依赖手工设计的任务特定策略，要么需要全量子态信息（在真实实验中不可获取且对大多体系统不实用），缺乏可扩展且与硬件兼容的自适应方案。

Method: 基于部分可观测性的强化学习框架，控制器仅使用实验可得的测量结果历史来选择测量算符和反馈动作；引入基于单次测量随机哈密顿量分量的随机终止奖励，避免非物理的全态重构。

Result: 在Bose-Hubbard模型基态制备和GHZ态生成中验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 建立了一种可扩展且与硬件兼容的量子态制备路线。

Abstract: Ground-state preparation is a critical task in quantum simulation and quantum computing, as it enables the study of correlated phases and the generation of entangled resource states. While measurement--feedback control has emerged as a promising route to state preparation, existing schemes either rely on handcrafted, task-specific policies or are designed using full quantum-state information that is unavailable in real experiments and becomes impractical for large many-body systems. Here we develop an adaptive measurement--feedback protocol based on reinforcement learning under partial observability. The controller uses only the history of experimentally accessible measurement outcomes to choose both the measurement operator and the feedback action in real time. To make training compatible with experiments, we introduce a stochastic terminal reward built from one-shot measurements of randomly sampled Hamiltonian components, avoiding unphysical full-state reconstruction while remaining an unbiased estimator of the target energy. We demonstrate the method by preparing ground states of the Bose--Hubbard model and by generating GHZ states, establishing a scalable and hardware-compatible route to quantum state preparation.

</details>


### [151] [QuBE/Qubex: an integrated hardware-software system for superconducting qubit experiments with broadband control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13010)
*Akinori Machino,Kazuhisa Ogawa,Takefumi Miyoshi,Hidehisa Shiomi,Shinichi Morisaka,Ryo Matsuda,Nilton F. G. Filho,Koichiro Ban,Takafumi Miyanaga,Keisuke Koike,Ryutaro Ohira,Toshi Sumida,Yoshinori Kurimoto,Yuuya Sugita,Yosuke Ito,Yasunari Suzuki,Peter A. Spring,Shiyu Wang,Hiroto Mukai,Arvind Mamgain,Shuhei Tamate,Yutaka Tabuchi,Yasunobu Nakamura,Makoto Negoro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种集成化超导量子比特控制系统，结合了宽带微波硬件（瞬时跨度达1.6 GHz）和脉冲级软件栈，在64量子比特固定频率transmon芯片上实现了全芯片频率识别、双量子比特门保真度98.34%以及多级读取等演示，并开源了软硬件架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模超导量子比特系统的高保真操作需要兼具宽频率覆盖、低串扰、紧同步的控制硬件，以及协调系统配置、实验执行和数据分析的软件，现有系统在这两方面存在整合不足的问题。

Method: 设计了宽带微波硬件（控制输出瞬时带宽1.6 GHz）并开发了脉冲级开源软件栈，支持自动配置和内置实验工作流；在64量子比特固定频率transmon芯片上进行了全芯片频率识别、多单元远失谐交叉共振校准和基准测试、以及计算子空间外的多级读取实验验证。

Result: 成功在64量子比特芯片上完成全芯片频率识别；多单元远失谐交叉共振校准测得双量子比特门保真度为98.34%；实现了计算子空间外的多级读取。

Conclusion: 通过公开硬件架构并开源软件栈，该工作为可扩展的超导量子比特控制实验提供了一个可检查的软硬件基础，有助于推动超导量子计算系统的发展。

Abstract: Achieving high-fidelity operation in large-scale superconducting qubit systems requires not only control hardware with broad frequency coverage, low crosstalk, and tight synchronization but also software that coordinates system configuration, experiment execution, and data analysis. Here we present an integrated qubit-control system that combines broadband microwave hardware with a pulse-level software stack for scalable superconducting qubit experiments. The hardware provides broadband microwave coverage, including an instantaneous span of up to 1.6 GHz from a control output, while the software reduces setup and calibration overhead through automated configuration and built-in experiment workflows. We validate the system on a 64-qubit fixed-frequency transmon chip through full-chip frequency identification and representative demonstrations, including multi-unit far-detuned cross-resonance calibration and benchmarking that yields a measured two-qubit gate fidelity of 98.34%, and multilevel readout beyond the computational subspace. By disclosing the hardware architecture and releasing the software stack as open source, this work provides an inspectable hardware-software foundation for scalable superconducting qubit control experiments.

</details>


### [152] [A Quantum Algorithm for Random Number Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13034)
*Aastha Kataria,Devansh Desai,Ashwini Dalvi,Sagar Korde,Abhijeet Pasi,Irfan N A Siddavatam,Sudhir Ranjan Jain*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种量子随机数生成算法，利用QFT、受控相位旋转和Grover扩散算子，在top-to-random洗牌问题上实现二次加速，混合时间达到O(√(n log n))，并扩展至qudit架构降低电路深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Markov链混合在随机数生成中效率受限，尤其是top-to-random洗牌问题需要O(n log n)次迭代；量子计算有望通过振幅放大和谱分解实现加速。

Method: 集成三种量子原语：(1) QFT对角化Markov转移算子；(2) 受控相位旋转编码洗牌特征值谱；(3) Grover扩散算子作为量子版本的Aldous-Diaconis强均匀停时，每轮迭代对振幅关于均值反射。并推广至qudit架构降低电路深度。

Result: n量子比特寄存器混合时间为O(√(n log n))次迭代；m个qudit（局域维度d）将混合时间降至O(√(log_d N))，其中N=d^m；QFT电路深度从O(log² N)降至O(log_d² N)；在IBM超导硬件上验证了两种变体。
