Conclusion: 量子算法在随机数生成中实现了对经典Markov链混合的二次加速，qudit架构进一步降低了实现复杂度，且实际硬件验证可行。

Abstract: We present a quantum algorithm for random number generation that achieves a provable quadratic speedup over classical Markov chain mixing, building on the Diaconis-Shahshahani Fourier analysis of the top-to-random card shuffle. The algorithm integrates three quantum primitives into a unified mixing circuit: the Quantum Fourier Transform (QFT), which diagonalizes the Markov transition operator; controlled phase rotations, which encode the shuffle eigenvalue spectrum; and the Grover diffusion operator, which acts as a quantum analogue of the Aldous-Diaconis strong uniform stopping time by reflecting amplitudes about their mean at each iteration. For an n-qubit register, the mixing time is O(\sqrt{n \log n}) iterations. Extending to m qudits of local dimension d reduces this to O(\sqrt{\log_d N}) iterations, where N = d^m, compared to the classical O(n \log n) bound. The qudit formulation further reduces QFT circuit depth from O(\log^2 N) to O(\log_d^2 N) gates per layer by encoding the same N-state space using m = \log_d N subsystems instead of \log_2 N qubits. We validate both variants on IBM superconducting hardware.

</details>


### [153] [Simple analytical flux-tuned iSWAP pulses for leakage suppression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13052)
*Dimitrios Georgiadis,Boxi Li,Asier Galicia,Rami Barends,F. A. Cárdenas-López,Felix Motzoi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Φ-DRAG方法，通过解析通量控制抑制可调谐耦合器双量子比特门中的泄漏，实现15纳秒内保真度超99.99%的两比特门操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可调谐耦合器架构实现快速双量子比特门时，快速通量调制会诱导非绝热跃迁和布居泄漏到非计算态，限制了门性能，亟需有效的泄漏抑制方案。

Method: 提出Φ-DRAG（derivative removal by adiabatic gate）解析通量控制方法，与传统的微波DRAG实现有本质区别，推导了修正的通量调制协议以抑制泄漏。

Result: Φ-DRAG方法将快速纠缠门的泄漏抑制到$10^{-4}$以下，在15纳秒范围内实现高保真度两比特门，且对量子比特非简谐性的不对称性和不同电路参数具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Φ-DRAG为可调谐通量控制双量子比特门提供了一种有效的泄漏抑制方案，有望推动容错量子计算的发展。

Abstract: Fast, high-fidelity two-qubit gates are a key requirement for fault-tolerant quantum computation. Tunable coupler architectures provide a flexible approach for implementing entangling gates through flux control with large on-off ratios, but fast flux modulation can induce diabatic transitions and population leakage to non-computational states, limiting gate performance. Here we present an analytical flux control method enabling derivative removal by adiabatic gate ($Φ$-DRAG) for suppressing leakage in flux tunable two-qubit gates. We show that $Φ$-DRAG differs fundamentally from conventional microwave implementations and derive modified flux modulation protocols that suppress leakage below $10^{-4}$ for fast entangling gates. The method remains effective across a range of asymmetry between qubit anharmonicities and different circuit parameters, enabling high-fidelity two-qubit gates within the fifteen nanosecond range.

</details>


### [154] [Fibonacci Steady-States and Persistent Oscillations in an Ordered Multimode Dicke Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13072)
*Miriam J. Leonhardt,Kai Müller,Oliver Lunt,Andrew J. Daley*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有有序最近邻耦合几何的多模Dicke模型，发现超辐射相中平均场稳态解的数量随原子团簇数呈斐波那契标度，部分稳态表现出持续振荡，并通过截断Wigner近似和纯态层次方法验证了有限团簇尺寸下这些特征的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模光学腔中的超冷原子为光介导相互作用的多体现象提供了丰富实验平台，近期实验实现了自旋玻璃和联想记忆等无序耦合多模Dicke模型。然而，有序耦合几何的多模Dicke模型性质尚待探索。

Method: 研究了具有有序最近邻耦合几何的多模Dicke模型（n_c个原子团簇通过n_c-1个腔模耦合），采用平均场理论分析稳态解，并利用截断Wigner近似和纯态层次方法验证有限尺寸效应。

Result: 超辐射相中平均场稳态解的数量随原子团簇数呈斐波那契标度；部分稳态表现出持续振荡；有限团簇尺寸下这些特征依然存在。

Conclusion: 有序多模Dicke模型（如本文考虑的最近邻耦合几何）在当前实验技术条件下可实现，指向了一类具有类似丰富行为的强相互作用耗散系统。

Abstract: Ultracold atoms in multimode optical cavities provide a rich testbed for many-body phenomena enabled by light-mediated interactions. Recent experiments include realizations of spin glasses and associative memories, as described by multimode Dicke models with disordered couplings. However, the properties of multimode Dicke models with ordered coupling geometries remain largely unexplored. In this work, we investigate the stable steady-states of the multimode Dicke model with an ordered nearest-neighbor coupling geometry, where $n_c$ atomic clusters are coupled via $n_c-1$ cavity modes. We show that the number of mean-field stable steady-states in the superradiant phase exhibits Fibonacci scaling with the number of atomic clusters, and that a subset of these steady-states exhibit persistent oscillations. Using both the truncated Wigner approximation and the numerically-exact hierarchy of pure states, we further demonstrate that these features of the stable steady-state solutions persist for finite cluster sizes. Ordered multimode Dicke models, such as the nearest-neighbor coupling geometry considered here, are accessible with current experimental technologies and point toward a broader class of strongly interacting dissipative systems with similarly rich behavior.

</details>


### [155] [Hamiltonian-Aware ADAPT Variational Quantum Eigensolver for Molecular Ground-State Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13118)
*Runhong He,Chao Liu,Xin Hong,Qiaozhen Chai,Junyuan Zhou,Ji Guan,Guolong Cui,Shenggang Ying*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出哈密顿感知的HA-ADAPT-VQE算法，通过建立新的激发算子选择准则和冗余算子剪枝方法，解决了ADAPT-VQE中算子选择不当和退化算子累积的问题，在强关联分子系统上实现了更优的能量误差、拟设规模和测量成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ADAPT-VQE拟设面临两个挑战：不恰当的算子选择和退化算子的累积，导致能量平台和拟设效率低下。

Method: 1) 建立融入哈密顿信息的激发算子选择准则，打破局部约束，优先选择物理意义明确的算子，且无额外计算开销；2) 开发问题自适应的冗余算子判别与剪枝方法，平衡剪枝与收敛保证，适用于任意规模拟设。

Result: 在典型强关联分子系统的数值实验中，HA-ADAPT-VQE避免了能量平台，在能量误差、拟设大小和测量成本上均优于基线算法。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种高效、鲁棒的拟设构建范式，有助于大规模VQE在量子化学中的发展和实际部署。

Abstract: Designing compact ansätze in Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE) is crucial for solving energetic problems of practical molecules on near-term quantum devices. However, existing Adaptive Derivative-Assembled Pseudo-Trotter (ADAPT) ansätze face two challenges: improper operator selection and accumulation of degraded operators. In this paper, we propose the Hamiltonian-Aware (HA) ADAPT-VQE algorithm to address these issues. First, we establish a novel excitation operator selection criterion. It breaks the local constraint of existing criteria by incorporating Hamiltonian information, prioritizes physically meaningful excitation operators, and incurs no extra classical or quantum computational overhead. Furthermore, we develop a problem-adaptive method for discriminating and pruning redundant excitation operators stemming from improper selection and inevitable degradation. This method balances redundant operator pruning and convergence guarantee, and is applicable to ansätze with arbitrary scales. Systematic numerical experiments on typical strongly correlated molecular systems demonstrate that our HA-ADAPT-VQE avoids energy plateaus and outperforms baseline algorithms in terms of energy error, ansatz size, and measurement cost. This work offers an efficient, robust ansatz construction paradigm, facilitating the development and practical deployment of large-scale VQE in quantum chemistry.

</details>


### [156] [Robust Pretty Good Measurement via Hybrid Classical-Quantum Pseudoinverse Approximation and Circuit-Level Realization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13150)
*Bikash K. Behera,Andrés Camilo Granda Arango,Giuseppe Sergioli,Roberto Giuntini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Moore-Penrose伪逆的数值鲁棒PGM方法，结合经典光谱预处理与量子电路实现（块编码、谱变换、 oblivious振幅放大），在病态/秩亏情形下实现稳定量子态区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PGM在测量算符奇异或病态时数值不稳定，实际实现受限。

Method: 用阈值正则化伪逆替代标准逆平方根；经典-量子混合框架：经典端伪逆光谱预处理，量子端块编码+谱变换实现电路，支持感知处理秩亏情形，oblivious振幅放大提升成功概率。

Result: 数值与电路仿真显示理论预测与量子电路输出高度一致；在合成与真实数据集（含病态、退化场景）上标准PGM不稳定时本方法表现稳定。

Conclusion: 建立了实用的混合经典-量子鲁棒量子态区分框架，将PGM测试阶段推广至伪逆感知的测量设计。

Abstract: Pretty Good Measurement (PGM) is a near-optimal strategy for quantum state discrimination, but its practical realization becomes unstable when the ensemble operator is singular or ill-conditioned. We introduce a numerically robust PGM formulation based on the Moore-Penrose pseudoinverse, replacing the standard inverse square root with a threshold-regularized variant that remains well-defined across different spectral regimes. We develop a hybrid classical-quantum framework that combines pseudoinverse-based spectral preprocessing with quantum circuit realizations using block-encoding and spectral-transformation techniques. The framework incorporates support awareness, yielding physically meaningful measurement operators even in rank-deficient cases, and employs oblivious amplitude amplification to improve circuit-level success probabilities. Extensive numerical and circuit-level simulations show close agreement between theoretical predictions and quantum circuit outputs. Experiments on synthetic and real datasets, including ill-conditioned and degenerate scenarios, demonstrate stable discrimination performance where standard PGM becomes numerically unstable. The results establish a practical hybrid classical-quantum framework for robust quantum state discrimination and extend previous circuit-based implementations of the PGM testing stage toward pseudoinverse-aware measurement design.

</details>


### [157] [Interference of critical dynamics associated with zero modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13200)
*Zhi-Han Zhang,Han-Chuan Kou,Peng Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过广义Creutz梯子模型中的闭合淬灭路径，研究了与零模相关的临界动力学干涉（ICDZM）。发现当闭合路径连接两个拓扑非平凡相时，ICDZM模式可能消失或出现周期倍增现象，并通过WKB分析中的干涉相位积累予以解释。零模转移概率可通过边界粒子数偏离初始分数值来检测，同时作为边缘缺陷，它既能捕捉ICDZM振荡，也能捕捉非闭合淬灭路径中的反常缺陷产生。这些结果将ICDZM及相应边缘缺陷确立为探测拓扑零模临界动力学的有效探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索临界动力学中零模干涉（ICDZM）的规律，特别是闭合淬灭路径通过两个临界点时出现的丰富干涉模式，以及利用零模转移概率作为探测拓扑零模临界动力学的有效工具。

Method: 采用广义Creutz梯子模型，设计闭合淬灭路径使其依次经过两个临界点，通过读取最终零模转移概率来研究ICDZM干涉模式；结合WKB分析框架解释干涉相位积累；通过边界粒子数偏离初始分数值来检测零模转移概率。

Result: 发现闭合路径连接两个拓扑非平凡相时，ICDZM模式可能消失或出现周期倍增；零模转移概率能同时捕捉ICDZM振荡和非闭合淬灭路径中的反常缺陷产生。

Conclusion: ICDZM及相应边缘缺陷可作为探测拓扑零模临界动力学的有效探针。

Abstract: We study the interference of critical dynamics associated with zero modes (ICDZM) in the generalized Creutz ladders using closed quench paths that pass through two critical points successively. By reading out the final zero-mode transfer probability, we find rich ICDZM interference patterns dependent on the quench path. In particular, when the closed path links two topologically nontrivial phases, the ICDZM pattern may either vanish or exhibit period doubling. Within the framework of WKB analysis, this phenomenon is well clarified by the interference phase accumulated in the quench procedure. We also demonstrate that the zero-mode transfer probability can be detected by the deviation of the boundary particle number from its initial fractional value, which arises from the blending of bulk modes in the critical dynamics. As an edge defect, the zero-mode transfer probability captures both the ICDZM oscillation and the known anomalous defect production in a non-closed quench path. These results identify ICDZM and the corresponding edge defect as probes for critical dynamics associated with topological zero modes.

</details>


### [158] [Achieving Heisenberg limit under noisy conditions with quantum Zeno dynamics and dynamical decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13205)
*Ke Zeng,Bakmou Lahcen,Yu Jiang,Kok Chuan Tan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子Zeno动力学（QZD）和动态解耦（DD）在量子计量学中抑制噪声、实现海森堡极限（HL）的条件，证明了QZD和DD有用的充要条件，并展示了在马尔可夫机制下QZD/DD可能比量子纠错（QEC）更有效，以及两者结合可饱和HL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在量子计量学中，何时能够抑制噪声以及何时能够达到海森堡极限（HL），并明确QZD和DD这两种工具在这些目标中的有效条件。

Method: 通过理论分析，推导QZD和DD在量子计量学中实现噪声抑制和达到HL的充要条件；对比马尔可夫机制下QZD/DD与量子纠错（QEC）的效果；分析QZD与DD联合使用的性能。

Result: 给出了QZD和DD在量子计量学中实现噪声抑制和达到HL的充要条件；证明了在马尔可夫机制下，存在QZD/DD能实现HL而QEC无法实现的情形；表明QZD和DD的组合可以使各自不完美的策略饱和HL。

Conclusion: QZD和DD在量子计量学中具有重要实用价值，能够在一定条件下有效抑制噪声并达到HL，且其组合策略具有协同优势，在某些场景下优于传统的QEC方法。

Abstract: Quantum Zeno dynamics (QZD) and dynamical decoupling (DD) are useful tools that enable the effective suppression of noise in quantum systems. We consider the problem of when (i) noise can be suppressed and (ii) Heisenberg limit (HL) can be achieved in quantum metrology, and prove necessary and sufficient conditions for when QZD and DD are useful for achieving these two goals. We also show that in the Markovian regime, there are scenarios where preventing errors using QZD/DD may enable HL to be achieved where current QEC methods may not. Finally, we demonstrate that the combination of both techniques can allow individually imperfect QZD and DD strategies to saturate HL.

</details>


### [159] [Coupling-Grouped XY-QAOA for Joint Anomaly-Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13244)
*Pauli Taipale*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种联合样本-特征选择方法（CG-XY-QAOA），解决了传统特征优先排序在预算固定时忽视特征与样本耦合依赖的问题。理论分析表明校准误差在联合选择中不随样本数增长，并在IBM Heron R3芯片上实现了64量子比特（p=2）和36量子比特（p=3）的约束保持二分选择QAOA硬件执行，电路深度降低45.9%-61.3%，双量子比特门减少2.6%-5.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定预算下选择异常样本和解释性特征是一个耦合约束优化问题。传统的特征优先排序方法先选特征再选样本，当评分数据有限、含噪或漂移时，会忽略特征效用依赖于样本选择的情况。作者希望将任务重新表述为在相同固定计数下的联合样本-特征选择，以降低校准误差敏感性。

Method: 1) 将问题形式化为联合样本-特征选择的耦合约束优化；2) 理论分析表明特征优先排序的校准误差随样本数线性增长，而联合选择保持常数；3) 提出Coupling-Grouped XY-QAOA (CG-XY-QAOA)，一种约束保持的分组角度变体；4) 实现硬件感知的电路优化，在IBM Heron R3基准上测试。

Result: 1) 电路深度降低45.9%-61.3%，双量子比特门减少2.6%-5.2%；2) 实现最大64量子比特(p=2)和36量子比特(p=3)的约束保持硬件执行；3) 20量子比特p=5运行保留63%有效样本；4) 固定角度运行在36-64量子比特上产生比随机可行采样更低的能量可行样本；5) 暖启动将严格可行经典参考的差距缩小57.5%-80.5%；6) 近预算修复在36量子比特上匹配稀疏经典参考。

Conclusion: 在测试范围内，联合选择方法和硬件可实现的约束混合器执行是有效的，支持在平衡固定预算场景中的实际应用。

Abstract: Selecting anomalous samples and explanatory features under fixed budgets defines a coupled constrained-optimization problem. Sequential feature-first selection ranks features before choosing samples, which can overlook features whose utility depends on which samples are selected, especially when scores are calibrated from reference data that may be limited, noisy, or drifting. We instead formulate the task as joint sample-feature selection under the same fixed counts. In the analyzed formal model, calibration-error sensitivity grows linearly with the number of samples for feature-first ordering but stays constant for joint selection. We introduce Coupling-Grouped XY-QAOA, a constraint-preserving grouped-angle variant for the resulting optimization problem. On matched sparse IBM Heron R3 benchmarks, a hardware-aware implementation reduces circuit depth by 45.9%-61.3% and two-qubit gates by 2.6%-5.2% relative to Qiskit optimization level 3 on the CZ-basis target. It enables, to our knowledge, the largest reported width-depth configurations for constraint-preserving bipartite-selection QAOA hardware executions with feasible-sector retention: 64 qubits at p=2 and 36 qubits at p=3. The 20-qubit p=5 runs retain 63% valid samples. Across 36-64 qubits, fixed-angle runs yield lower-energy feasible samples than matched random-feasible sampling. Warm starts reduce the gap to strict-feasible classical references by 57.5%-80.5%, and near-budget repair matches the sparse classical reference at 36 qubits. Benchmarks show gains in balanced fixed-budget regimes, and noiseless simulations show that problem-structured angle grouping improves over same-depth XY-QAOA and matched-parameter, type-preserving randomization controls. Overall, the results support calibrated joint selection and hardware-realizable constrained-mixer execution in the tested regimes.

</details>


### [160] [Kubo-Martin-Schwinger conditions for non-Hermitian systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13251)
*Chen Lan,Luyao Ma,Hao Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了非厄米哈密顿量（具有实谱和双正交特征系统）的KMS热平衡条件扩展。核心结果是热力学表征了准厄米性：可对角化且具有实谱的H，其双正交Gibbs泛函对所有A满足正定性当且仅当H是准厄米的。在完全准厄米假设下，证明了η-Gibbs态满足所有三个解析KMS条件，使用了Hadamard三线定理和Bari定理。研究还分析了异常点和复谱的失败模式，以及与开放系统量子细致平衡条件的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将KMS热平衡条件拓展到非厄米哈密顿量体系，并建立准厄米性的热力学表征，旨在理解非厄米量子系统的热力学性质。

Method: 通过三条互补路径系统分析：利用Riesz表示定理从双正交Gibbs泛函的特征投影构造度量η（无需预先选择度量）；在准厄米假设下，使用Hadamard三线定理和Bari定理证明η-Gibbs态满足KMS条件；分析异常点和复谱的失效模式并与开放系统条件比较。

Result: 建立了双正交Gibbs泛函正定性与准厄米性的等价关系；证明了准厄米哈密顿量的η-Gibbs态满足所有三个解析KMS条件；发现传输态与等谱厄米伙伴的Gibbs态不同（当[η,h]≠0时），因此KMS性质不能通过相似变换从厄米理论推导；识别了与完整Haag-Hugenholtz-Winnink $C^*$-代数框架之间的差距。

Conclusion: 准厄米哈密顿量可以纳入KMS热力学框架，但标准$C^*$-代数方法需要扩展以适应非厄米结构；异常点和复谱存在本质的失效机制；与开放系统的量子细致平衡条件存在关联。

Abstract: We investigate the extension of the Kubo--Martin--Schwinger (KMS) thermal equilibrium condition to non-Hermitian Hamiltonians with real spectra and biorthogonal eigensystems, providing a systematic analysis through three complementary routes. Our central result is a thermodynamic characterisation of quasi-Hermiticity: for $H \in M_d(\mathbb{C})$ diagonalisable with real spectrum, the biorthogonal Gibbs functional $ω_{\rm{bi}}(A) = Z_{\rm{bi}}^{-1} \sum_n e^{-βE_n}\langleφ_n|A|ψ_n\rangle$ satisfies $ω_{\rm{bi}}(A^†A) \geq 0$ for all $A$ if and only if $H$ is quasi-Hermitian. The proof constructs the metric $η$ directly from the eigenprojectors of $ω_{\rm{bi}}$ via the Riesz representation theorem, with no prior choice of $η$, providing a metric-free certificate of quasi-Hermiticity outside the Mostafazadeh--Scholtz framework. Under the full quasi-Hermitian hypothesis, we prove that the $η$-Gibbs state $ω_η(A) = Z_η^{-1}\, \rm{Tr}[ηe^{-βH}A]$ satisfies all three analytic KMS conditions, using the Hadamard three-line theorem and Bari's theorem on Riesz bases. The result is non-trivial: the transported state $\hatω(X) = \rm{Tr}[e^{-βh}Xη]/Z_η$ differs from the Gibbs state of the isospectral Hermitian partner $h = η^{1/2}Hη^{-1/2}$ whenever $[η,h]\neq 0$, so the KMS property cannot be deduced from the Hermitian theory by similarity. The gap between this result and the full Haag--Hugenholtz--Winnink $C^*$-algebraic framework is identified. Failure modes at exceptional points and for complex spectra are analysed, and the relation to the Fagnola--Umanità quantum detailed balance condition for open systems is discussed.

</details>


### [161] [Where a Quantum Reservoir Works: A Transferable Operating Band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13284)
*Markus Baumann,Itamar Fink,Johannes Wittmann,Claudia Linnhoff-Popien,Jonas Stein*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了耗散型量子储备池计算中，控制参数空间（输入驱动强度、邻近量子比特耦合强度、耗散率）对性能的影响，发现存在一个单一且定义良好的最优操作区域，该区域跨任务、跨初始化、跨架构转移，具有清晰的机制基础，并可通过简单的记忆诊断在任务运行前低成本定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子储备池计算中，固定量子系统的动力学参数在整个训练和推理过程中保持不变，因此如何选择这些动力学参数使其具备良好学习能力是一个开放问题。本文旨在通过映射控制空间中的性能分布来回答这一关键问题。

Method: 通过系统性地映射三个核心物理控制参数（输入驱动强度、邻近量子比特耦合强度、耗散率）的性能表现，分析性能集中区域的特征，并验证其跨任务、跨初始化、跨架构的普适性，同时通过消融实验探究其机制基础。

Result: 性能集中在一个单一且定义良好的操作区域；该区域跨任务和储备池初始状态迁移，且在架构变化下保持相同的记忆定义域；该区域在移除其产生的任何机制时消失；可通过简单的记忆诊断在运行任何任务前低成本定位。

Conclusion: 耗散型量子储备池的控制空间中存在一个普适的最优操作区域，该区域由明确的物理机制支撑，且可低成本预先定位，为量子储备池计算的实用化提供了重要指导。

Abstract: In quantum reservoir computing, a fixed quantum system transforms an input signal, while learning reduces to training a simple linear readout on its measured outputs. Since the quantum dynamics themselves are never optimized, the method is well suited to today's hardware. Yet these dynamics must still be chosen carefully, because their settings remain fixed throughout training and inference. It therefore remains an open question where, in its control space, a fixed quantum system learns well. We address this question for a dissipative reservoir by mapping performance over three central physical controls: the strength of the input drive, the coupling between neighboring qubits, and the rate of dissipation. Good performance concentrates in a single, well-defined operating region of this control space. This region transfers across tasks and reservoir initializations, and the same memory-defined regime persists under architectural changes. It is also mechanistically grounded, since it disappears whenever any of the mechanisms that create it is removed. Finally, the region can be located cheaply before any task is run, using a simple memory diagnostic.

</details>


### [162] [Driven-dissipative entanglement of distant giant atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13375)
*Aziza Almanakly,Ariadna Soro,Alejandro Vivas-Viaña,Beatriz Yankelevich,Caspar Groiseau,David Pahl,Junyoung An,Gabriel Cutter,Michael E. Gingras,Bethany M. Niedzielski,Hannah Stickler,Renée DePéncier Piñero,Mollie E. Schwartz,Kyle Serniak,Max Hays,Jeffrey A. Grover,Anton Frisk Kockum,William D. Oliver*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过超导巨原子系统实现了驱动-耗散协议下的远程纠缠生成与保持，Bell态保真度达0.89±0.02，证明了巨原子的受控耗散是量子网络中分发纠缠的可行方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有纠缠产生方案多依赖精确校准的相干可逆脉冲，而驱动-耗散协议虽能通过连续驱动和关联耗散稳定纠缠，但耗散过程本身在协议结束后会限制纠缠的效用。因此需要一种既能利用耗散生成纠缠、又能后续抑制耗散以保护纠缠的方法。

Method: 构建两个超导巨原子与波导顺序耦合的系统，利用耦合点间的干涉实现可调个体耗散和关联耗散；通过波导连续驱动原子利用关联耗散生成远程纠缠，随后原位调谐量子比特频率以抑制个体耗散，从而保护纠缠。

Result: 成功实现Bell态保真度F=0.89±0.02的远程纠缠生成与保持。

Conclusion: 巨原子的受驱动耗散是实现量子网络中纠缠分发的可行途径。

Abstract: Quantum interconnects distribute entanglement via controlled light-matter interactions for quantum computing and sensing applications. Many entanglement generation schemes use coherent, reversible interactions that require precisely calibrated pulses to execute. In contrast, driven-dissipative protocols use a continuous-wave drive in the presence of correlated dissipation to stabilize entanglement in protected (dark) states. However, the same dissipation that generates the entanglement also limits its utility once the stabilization protocol ends. Here, we engineer a superconducting system of two giant artificial atoms coupled sequentially to a waveguide, with tunable individual and correlated dissipation enabled by interference between coupling points. Continuously driving the atoms through the waveguide exploits correlated dissipation to generate remote entanglement. We then tune the qubit frequencies in situ to suppress individual dissipation and thereby preserve the entanglement, achieving a Bell-state fidelity F = 0.89 +/- 0.02. This demonstration indicates that the driven dissipation of giant atoms is a viable approach for distributing entanglement across quantum networks.

</details>


### [163] [Steady-State Noise Signatures of Lindbladian Exceptional Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13377)
*Shihang Pan,Gianmichele Blasi,Géraldine Haack*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了林德布拉德算符中的奇异点（EPs）可以通过稳态电流噪声而非平均电流来探测，并通过两个相互作用量子比特与两个热库耦合的模型，展示了过阻尼、欠阻尼和临界三种不同动力学 regime 下的噪声特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放量子系统中，奇异点已在瞬态可观测量的信号中被识别，但稳态平均电流无法直接提供奇异点结构的证据。本文旨在探索是否可以通过稳态电流噪声来访问林德布拉德奇异点的特征。

Method: 在林德布拉德主方程框架下推导了电流相关函数的一般表达式，分析了延迟时间依赖行为；以两个相互作用量子比特与两个热库耦合的典型模型为例进行验证。

Result: 发现稳态噪声能够清晰区分过阻尼、欠阻尼和临界三种 regime，电流相关函数的行为会受奇异点影响，展示了奇异点在稳态噪声中的可观测特征。

Conclusion: 电流相关函数可以作为开放量子系统中林德布拉德奇异点的稳态探针，弥补了稳态平均电流无法直接探测奇异点结构的不足。

Abstract: Exceptional points (EPs) are non-Hermitian degeneracies at which two or more eigenvalues and their corresponding eigenvectors coalesce. In open quantum systems, exceptional points can arise in the Lindbladian governing the dissipative dynamics. Their signatures have so far been mainly identified in finite-time observables, such as transient currents, while steady-state average currents generally provide no direct evidence of the underlying exceptional-point structure. In this work, we demonstrate that signatures of Lindbladian EPs can nevertheless be accessed in the steady-state regime through current noise. We derive general expressions for current correlation functions within a Lindblad master-equation framework and show, in particular, how exceptional points affect their behaviour as a function of the time delay. We illustrate these results with the paradigmatic example of two interacting qubits coupled to two reservoirs, where the steady-state noise clearly distinguishes overdamped, underdamped, and critical regimes. Our results establish current correlation functions as a steady-state probe of Lindbladian EPs in open quantum systems.

</details>


### [164] [An LLM System for Autonomous Variational Quantum Circuit Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13380)
*Kenya Sakka,Wataru Mizukami,Kosuke Mitarai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于大语言模型的自主智能体框架，用于在显式设计约束下进行迭代量子电路设计，在量子特征映射和变分量子本征求解器任务上取得了有竞争力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能量子电路的设计高度依赖人类专家经验，缺乏自动化设计方法，因此需要引入AI系统实现量子电路的自主设计。

Method: 构建了一个包含探索、生成、讨论、验证、存储、评估和审查七个组件的闭环智能体框架，结合网络知识获取、文献驱动的批判、可执行代码生成和实验反馈，利用LLM进行迭代电路设计。

Result: 在图像分类任务中，生成的特征映射优于代表性量子特征映射，在大比特数下超越经典径向基函数核；在7种分子的基态能量估计中，生成的ansatz在满足缩放约束的同时达到与化学启发式及硬件高效结构相当的精度。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的智能体系统可作为自动化量子电路设计的可行范式，展示了AI系统参与跨学科迭代科学优化流程的潜力。

Abstract: The design of high performing quantum circuits remains largely dependent on human expertise. We introduce an autonomous agentic framework that employs large language models (LLMs) to conduct iterative quantum circuit designs under explicit design constraints. Our system integrates seven components: Exploration, Generation, Discussion, Validation, Storage, Evaluation, and Review. These components form a closed-loop workflow that combines web-based knowledge acquisition, literature-grounded critique, executable code generation, and experimental feedback. We evaluate the framework on two tasks: quantum feature map construction for quantum machine learning and ansatz generation for variational quantum eigensolver applications in quantum chemistry. In image classification benchmarks, the best generated feature map outperforms representative quantum feature maps and, when scaled to larger qubit counts, surpasses the classical radial basis function kernel. In molecular ground state estimation across seven molecules, the generated ansatz attains competitive accuracy with widely used chemically inspired and hardware-efficient constructions while satisfying the imposed scaling constraints. These results establish LLM driven agentic system as a viable paradigm for automated quantum circuit design and illustrate how AI systems can participate in iterative scientific optimization workflows across scientific domains.

</details>


### [165] [Beyond the Unruh vacuum: multi-time correlations in black hole collapse and evaporation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13383)
*Konstantinos Xenos,Charis Anastopoulos,Andreas F. Terzis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过多时间量子场关联机制，证明黑洞蒸发过程中塌缩前信息可以存活于晚期多时间关联中，而非单时间观测量所体现的热辐射特性，从而指出基于单时间观测量的黑洞信息悖论表述是不完整的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞信息悖论源于霍金辐射的热力学特性，但热力学仅约束单时间观测量，多时间量子关联的结构尚未被充分探索。作者旨在探究多时间关联是否能为塌缩前信息在黑洞蒸发中的存续提供具体机制。

Method: 使用二维引力塌缩与蒸发模型，计算并比较晚期多时间关联与Unruh真空的对应结果，分析其中依赖于塌缩前状态参数的贡献。

Result: 晚期多时间关联不能被Unruh真空完全复现；其中包含显式依赖于塌缩前状态参数的贡献，尽管渐进辐射具有热力学特征。

Conclusion: 可测量的多时间关联是霍金辐射中信息的载体；仅基于单时间观测量的黑洞信息悖论表述是不完整的。

Abstract: The black hole information paradox originates from the thermal character of Hawking radiation, which appears to erase information about the collapsing matter. However, thermality constrains only observables defined at a single time and leaves the structure of temporal quantum correlations largely unexplored. Here we show that multi-time quantum-field correlations provide a concrete mechanism for the survival of pre-collapse information in black hole evaporation. Using a two-dimensional model of gravitational collapse and evaporation, we demonstrate that late-time multi-time correlations are not fully reproduced by the Unruh vacuum. In particular, they contain a contribution that depends explicitly on parameters characterizing the pre-collapse state, despite the thermal character of the asymptotic radiation. Our results identify measurable multi-time correlations as carriers of information in Hawking radiation and suggest that formulations of the black hole information paradox based solely on single-time observables are incomplete.

</details>


### [166] [Representation-Induced Symmetry Trapping in Adaptive Variational Quantum Simulations of Multi-Reference Topologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13387)
*Hermawan Kresno Dipojono*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统揭示了在自适应量子化学算法中，费米子到量子比特的映射表示（如Bravyi-Kitaev变换）与非对称分子畸变结合时会产生优化陷阱效应，即编码锁定的贫瘠高原问题。通过对比对称拉伸基线（BeH₂），证明点群对称性的保持能结构性保护优化景观，但映射表示的选择仍是关键因素。此外，引入基于协方差的自适应动态分配滤波器，通过实时监控梯度精度阈值，自动修剪死亡对称通道并触发电路终止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解自适应量子化学算法在多参考静态关联下的可训练性，特别是分子几何结构如何与表示拓扑相互作用影响优化景观。

Method: 系统评估自旋守恒SUSD算子池在强拉伸构型（2倍平衡键长）下的不对称LiH、对称BeH₂和不对称H₂O分子；比较Bravyi-Kitaev变换下的非局部映射约束与对称拉伸基线；提出协方差驱动的自适应动态分配滤波器。

Result: 非对称畸变下Bravyi-Kitaev变换导致优化陷阱效应（编码锁定的贫瘠高原）；对称性保持（如BeH₂对称拉伸）能保护优化景观；映射表示是影响可训练性的关键因素。

Conclusion: ansatz的对称性限制是必要的但不充分，必须考虑费米子到量子比特映射表示的影响；提出的动态分配滤波器为早期容错架构上的深度变分算法提供了资源高效的策略。

Abstract: Evaluating the trainability of adaptive quantum chemistry algorithms under multi-reference static correlation requires understanding how representation topologies intertwine with molecular geometry. We systematically expose a deep physical dependence on point-group symmetry by evaluating a spin-conserved SUSD operator pool across highly stretched configurations (2 x Re) of asymmetric LiH, symmetric BeH2, and asymmetric H2O. Under asymmetric distortions, the non-local mapping constraints of the Bravyi-Kitaev transformation create an optimization trapping effect--an encodement-locked manifestation of the broader barren plateau crisis. Crucially, by comparing these to the symmetrical stretching baseline of BeH2, we demonstrate that the preservation of point-group symmetry structurally protects the optimization landscape, proving that ansatz symmetry restrictions are necessary but insufficient without accounting for the underlying fermion-to-qubit representation. While current methods rely on numerical pruning to throttle pool sizes, our structural approach establishes that the mapping representation remains a critical factor in maintaining landscape trainability. Furthermore, exploiting structural overlap within our pool, we introduce a covariance-driven, adaptive shot-allocation filter. Diverging from static energy-variance minimization frameworks, our allocation engine operates as a dynamic runtime diagnostic tool. By continuously monitoring the gradient precision threshold epsilon, it aggressively prunes dead symmetry channels and triggers an automated circuit-termination sequence upon detecting representation-induced flat-lined states (dE/dtheta approx 0). This integration of algebraic measurement reuse with topology-aware statistical filtering provides a promising, resource-efficient strategy for executing deep variational algorithms on early fault-tolerant architectures.

</details>


### [167] [Characterizing the functional role of quantum coherence in energy transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13404)
*Hallmann Óskar Gestsson,Alexandra Olaya-Castro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文通过Nakajima-Zwanzig投影算子推导出一个通用的记忆核恒等式，从而能够定量表征和量化本征能量基下的量子相干性对能量转移广义速率的影响，并以二聚体-结构声子浴模型展示了相干性如何调控能量转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管量子相干性被认为在开放量子系统的激发能转移中发挥作用，但缺乏定量评估其影响的方法。

Method: 利用Nakajima-Zwanzig投影算子方法，推导出通用记忆核恒等式，用以表征和量化本征能量基下相干性对能量转移广义速率的影响，并将其应用于耦合结构声子浴的二聚体电子动力学模型。

Result: 成功推导出记忆核恒等式，并在二聚体模型中展示了量子相干性如何调控能量转移过程。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够定量评估量子相干性对能量转移的影响，为理解开放量子系统中相干性作用提供了新工具。

Abstract: Quantum coherence is understood to play a role in excitation energy transfer in open quantum systems, yet a quantitative approach to assessing its influence on the transfer process is still missing. Using Nakajima-Zwanzig projection operators, we derive a general memory kernel identity that enables us to characterize and quantify the impact of coherence in the eigenenergy basis on a generalized rate of energy transfer. Applying our approach to the electronic dynamics of a dimer coupled to a structured phonon bath, we demonstrate how quantum coherence acts to modulate energy transfer.

</details>


### [168] [Kerr-induced nonreciprocal transparency and group delay in a hybrid cavity magnomechanical system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13412)
*M. Amghar,Noura Chabar,Mohamed Amazioug,Reem Altuijri,Ameenah N. Al-Ahmadi,Abdel-Haleem Abdel-Aty*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出在混合腔磁力系统中利用YIG球的Kerr非线性实现非互易透明、Fano共振和慢/快光效应，展示了方向相关的透射特性和可控群延迟切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现非互易透明、Fano共振和慢/快光效应，探索Kerr非线性在磁力系统中的调控能力。

Method: 构建包含两个YIG球和机械谐振子的混合腔磁力系统，利用磁振子Kerr非线性诱导方向依赖的频率偏移，改变腔光子、磁振子和声子之间的干涉路径。

Result: 系统支持多重透明窗口，Kerr相互作用重塑透明特征产生非对称Fano线形，实现可控非互易传输；色散呈现显著方向不对称性，群延迟出现巨大差异，可实现慢光与快光的可逆切换。

Conclusion: Kerr工程化的磁力系统为集成非互易微波光子学和量子信息技术提供了有前景的平台。

Abstract: We propose a scheme for realizing nonreciprocal transparency, Fano resonances, and slow/fast light in a hybrid cavity magnomechanical system containing two YIG spheres and a mechanical resonator. The nonreciprocal behavior originates from the magnon Kerr nonlinearity, which induces direction-dependent frequency shifts and modifies the interference pathways among cavity photons, magnons, and phonons. We show that the hybrid system supports multiple transparency windows arising from magnon- and magnomechanical-induced interference processes. The Kerr interaction strongly reshapes these transparency features, producing asymmetric Fano line shapes and enabling controllable nonreciprocal transmission. Furthermore, the associated dispersion exhibits pronounced directional asymmetry, leading to giant differences in the group delay for opposite propagation directions and allowing reversible switching between slow- and fast-light regimes. We investigate the roles of hybrid coupling strengths and dissipation channels and identify parameter regimes where the nonreciprocal response is maximized. These findings establish Kerr-engineered magnomechanical systems as promising platforms for integrated nonreciprocal microwave photonics and quantum information technologies.

</details>


### [169] [Foundations of Practical Quantum Advantage in Quantum-Informed Machine Learning for Predicting Chaos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13422)
*Maida Wang,Xiao Xue,Minh Chung,Peter V. Coveney*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为混沌动力系统的量子信息机器学习建立了理论基础，提出了一种高阶量子统计先验（Q-Priors）机制，在表示阶段利用叠加和纠缠压缩存储不变测度的非因子化空间相关性，在提取阶段通过联合贝尔测量实现与量子比特数无关的拷贝计数，证明了量子-经典分离的拷贝测量复杂性。在湍流通道流和ERA5中期天气预报两个案例中验证了该机制，后者将异常相关技能提升10-39%，为容错量子硬件之前实现实际量子优势提供了候选路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为混沌动力系统的量子信息机器学习建立理论基础，探索在容错量子硬件实现之前，利用当前量子处理器获得实际量子优势的可行机制。

Method: 提出k索引的高阶量子统计先验（Q-Priors）族，将不变测度的k点边际存储在n_q = kq个量子比特上；在表示阶段利用叠加和纠缠编码非因子化空间相关性；在提取阶段通过双副本联合贝尔测量估计任意事后泡利函数；在IQM超导处理器上实现仿真验证。

Result: 双副本贝尔测量所需的副本对计数与n_q无关，而任何自适应单副本协议完成全泡利读出需要Ω(2^(n_q))个副本，证明了量子-经典分离；在ERA5天气预报中，k≤2的Q-Prior引导Koopman展开，在48-240小时提前期将异常相关技能提升10-39%，减少了长时程展开向静态平均场的坍缩。

Conclusion: 该机制在两个互补层面满足了实际量子优势的定义条件，为在容错量子硬件之前实现实际量子优势提供了候选路线。

Abstract: We develop theoretical foundations for a practical quantum-advantage mechanism in quantum-informed machine learning for chaotic dynamical systems. A family of k-indexed higher-order quantum statistical priors (Q-Priors) hosts the k-point marginal of the invariant measure on n_q = kq qubits, extending the single-site construction of prior work. We prove a two-stage advantage. In the representation stage, superposition and entanglement compactly store non-factorisable spatial correlations of the invariant measure on n_q qubits. In the extraction stage, joint Bell measurements on two copies estimate any post hoc Pauli functional with a copy-pair count independent of n_q, whereas any adaptive single-copy protocol for the corresponding full-Pauli read-out requires Omega(2^(n_q)) copies; this is a provable quantum-classical separation in copy-measurement complexity. The two-copy read-out is realised in simulation and on IQM superconducting processors. Two case studies instantiate the mechanism in workflows of independent scientific value: a turbulent channel-flow study in which the two-copy read-out yields a named non-diagonal correlator of the invariant measure (the velocity-direction coherence), and a medium-range weather forecasting workflow on the European Centre for Medium-Range Weather Forecasts ERA5 reanalysis in which the diagonal k <= 2 Q-Prior steers a Koopman rollout, improves anomaly-correlation skill by 10-39% across 48-240 h lead times, and reduces the long-horizon collapse of rollouts onto a static mean field. The two conditions of our practical-advantage definition are met at complementary levels, identifying a candidate route to practical quantum advantage before fault-tolerant hardware.

</details>


### [170] [Quantum optical photoelectron interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13447)
*Jonathan Dubois,Viviane Cotte,Richard Taïeb,Camille Lévêque,Jérémie Caillat,Pranshu Dave,Pascal Salières,David Bresteau,Charles Bourassin-Bouchet,Anne L'Huillier,David Busto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了量子光场驱动多光子过程的理论框架，揭示了光子统计与光电子观测量的直接映射关系，并以RABBIT光谱学为例展示了量子光统计特性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光电子光谱理论多基于经典光场假设，忽略了量子光场的统计特性对多光子过程的影响，需要建立统一的量子理论框架来桥接光子统计与光电子观测量的关系。

Method: 建立了量子光场驱动多光子过程的一般理论框架，推导出自相关/互相关函数与光电子谱的映射关系；以RABBIT方案为例，分析了不同量子配置（关联红外和谐波模式、非经典谐波统计的非关联情况）下边带信号的振幅、对比度和相位；并与数值模拟进行对比验证。

Result: 证明了光子统计的自相关和互相关函数直接映射到光电子谱；在RABBIT方案中，边带信号的振幅、对比度和相位揭示了光的量子本质；经典与非经典关联之间的相互作用决定了光电发射过程的相干性；理论分析与压缩相干态红外场的数值模拟吻合良好。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了量子光统计与阿秒科学之间的桥梁，揭示了光场的量子特性对阿秒时间尺度上光电发射过程相干性的关键影响，为量子光学在阿秒科学中的应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We present a general theoretical framework for multiphoton processes driven by quantum light fields, establishing a direct link between photon statistics and photoelectron observables. Our results show that the autocorrelation and cross-correlation functions, which quantify the underlying photon statistics, are directly mapped onto the resulting photoelectron spectra. Although our framework is broadly applicable, we demonstrate specifically in the example of reconstruction of attosecond beating by interference of two-photon transitions (RABBIT) the influence of the light statistical properties. In this approach, the amplitude, contrast and phase of the oscillations of the sideband signal as a function of pump-probe delay reveal the quantum nature of light. We analyze these observables across several quantum configurations, including correlated infrared and harmonic modes, as well as the uncorrelated case with non-classical harmonic statistics, thereby establishing a general framework for quantum-light RABBIT spectroscopy. We compare the analytical theory with numerical simulations for the case of classical harmonics and an infrared field in a squeezed coherent state, obtaining excellent agreement. Our results reveal how the interplay between classical and quantum correlations dictates the coherence of the photoemission process, providing a new window into the quantum-optical foundations of attosecond science.

</details>


### [171] [Invariant Measures and Weak-Magic-Injection Asymptotics in Random Monitored Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13470)
*Guocheng Zhen,Xuanrong Yang,Chengkai Zhu,Ranyiliu Chen,Xin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为随机受控量子电路建立了严格的数学分析框架，证明了稳态分布的existence and uniqueness，并解析得到了弱魔法注入极限下稳态魔法的leading asymptotics，揭示了不同资源度量在稳定子层附近的几何差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对受控量子电路的理解主要依赖数值模拟和唯象描述，缺乏严格的动力学理论。本文旨在填补这一空白，发展一个解析框架为随机受控量子动力学奠定严格的数学基础。

Method: 研究一类由随机Clifford驱动的受控量子电路。首先证明稳态律的存在唯一性（遍历性描述），然后解析求解弱魔法注入极限下稳态魔法的leading asymptotics。

Result: 在奇数素数局域维度下，稳态Gross-Wigner mana具有线性leading asymptotic；在qubit系统中，稳态2-stabilizer Rényi熵具有二次leading asymptotic。两者不同的幂次反映了两种资源度量在稳定子层附近的不同局域几何。

Conclusion: 本文建立了随机受控量子电路稳态遍历动力学的解析框架，严格揭示了不同魔法资源度量在弱注入极限下的本质差异。

Abstract: Monitored quantum circuits provide a natural setting in which scrambling, measurements, and measurement-conditioned updates compete within a stochastic many-body dynamics. From the viewpoint of nonstabilizer resource theory, this competition is especially relevant because Clifford-compatible operations preserve the stabilizer structure, while weak non-Clifford perturbations inject magic resource. Most of the existing understanding of monitored quantum circuits has been shaped by numerical simulations and phenomenological descriptions, while a rigorous dynamics theory remains less developed. In this paper, we address this gap by developing an analytical framework which lays a rigorous mathematical foundation for the study of random monitored quantum dynamics. Specifically, we study a class of monitored quantum circuits driven by random Clifford. We prove the existence and uniqueness of the stationary law, which gives an ergodic description of the long-time dynamics. We then resolve the leading asymptotics of steady magic in the weak-magic-injection limit. This tangent description makes the contrast between resource measures transparent: in odd-prime local dimension, the steady Gross--Wigner mana has a linear leading asymptotic, whereas in qubit systems the steady 2-stabilizer Rényi entropy has a quadratic leading asymptotic. These different powers reflect the distinct local geometries of the two resource measures near the stabilizer layer. In this way, this work develops an analytical framework that first establishes the stationary ergodic dynamics of random monitored quantum circuits.

</details>


### [172] [Observation of Non-Gaussian Magnon Dynamics in a Two-Dimensional Long-Range XY Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13499)
*S. -A. Guo,J. -Y. Tan,J. Ye,Y. Jiang,L. Zhang,Y. -X. Chen,H. -J. Chen,H. -Y. Hu,W. -X. Guo,B. -X. Qi,L. He,Z. -C. Zhou,Y. -K. Wu,L. -M. Duan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用离子阱量子模拟器在二维XY模型中演示了从高斯到非高斯动力学的交叉，通过测量高阶自旋关联揭示了超越平均场和Holstein-Primakoff近似的非高斯行为，并验证了其对抗定标误差的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体系统中高阶自旋关联的非高斯演化是理解量子多体系统重要性质的关键，但实验中的退相干、统计涨落和参数误差阻碍了非高斯动力学的观测。本文旨在克服这些障碍，在可控的量子模拟平台上演示并验证非高斯动力学。

Method: 使用离子阱量子模拟器实现具有长程空间结构化相互作用的二维XY模型；通过制备不同初始磁振子激发密度，测量单自旋可观测量动力学；将高阶自旋关联与平均场解和Holstein-Primakoff近似进行比较，并采用对抗定标误差的方式验证非高斯行为。

Result: 成功观察到高斯到非高斯动力学的交叉；高阶自旋关联偏离平均场和Holstein-Primakoff近似预测；非高斯行为的验证对定标误差具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一条从经典可模拟动力学到可能展现量子优势的区域的可验证路径，为在量子模拟器中研究非高斯量子多体动力学建立了可靠方法。

Abstract: Non-Gaussian evolution of high-order spin correlations characterizes important properties of quantum many-body systems. In practice, decoherence, statistical fluctuation and miscalibration of experimental parameters all hinder the witness of non-Gaussian dynamics. Here we demonstrate the crossover between Gaussian and non-Gaussian dynamics on a two-dimensional XY model with long-range and spatially structured interaction using a trapped ion quantum simulator. We prepare different initial densities of magnon excitations and verify the dynamics of single-spin observables for the engineered Hamiltonian. Then we compare the high-order spin correlations with the mean-field solution and the Holstein-Primakoff approximation, and demonstrate the non-Gaussian behavior in a way independent of the calibration errors. Our work provides a verifiable path from classically simulatable dynamics to regimes where quantum advantage may emerge.

</details>


### [173] [A refined thermodynamic analysis of nonsecular master equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13504)
*Mohamed Boubakour,Talia Szikman,Cyril Elouard*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对非世俗主方程进行了系统的热力学分析，研究了部分世俗近似和几何-算术近似下系统动力学的热力学一致性，建立了统一的热力学框架，并揭示了稳态非吉布斯态下熵产生率与Spohn不等式的差异，指出对于单热浴情况，差异仅为瞬态，无法从稳态中循环提取功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当系统跃迁频率过小无法应用全世俗近似时，需要采用部分世俗近似或几何-算术近似以确保动力学正定性，但这些近似会导致系统驰豫到非吉布斯稳态。现有热力学框架无法统一描述这类动力学，因此需要建立一致的热力学框架。

Method: 从基于系统-环境关联的微观第二定律表达式出发，采用系统微扰理论保持第二定律的正定性；比较了微观熵产生与基于约化动力学收缩性（Spohn不等式）定义的熵产生；通过简单例子进行验证。

Result: 建立了非世俗主方程的统一热力学框架；发现系统-浴相互作用能和Lamb位移参与能量平衡，产生系统对浴的功；证明了非世俗主方程中两种熵产生率因定态相干性而不同；单热浴情况下差异仅为瞬态，无法循环提取功。

Conclusion: 非世俗主方程的热力学框架需考虑系统-浴相互作用能和Lamb位移的贡献；定态相干性导致两种熵产生率不等，但在单热浴下无实际热力学收益；完善了量子马尔可夫动力学的热力学图景。

Abstract: We present a systematic thermodynamic analysis of nonsecular master equations. We consider master equations resulting either from the partial secular and the geometric-arithmetic approximations, two approximations ensuring the positivity of the system's dynamics when some of its transition frequencies are too small to enable the full secular approximation. Both cause the system to relax towards a steady state which is not the Gibbs state of its bare Hamiltonian. Nonetheless, we build a unified, consistent thermodynamic framework for those dynamics. Starting from a microscopic expression of the second law based on system-environment correlations, we employ a systematic perturbation theory to preserve the positivity of the second law despite the approximations done on the dynamics. We show that, in spite of the weak system-bath coupling, the system-bath interaction energy participates to the energy balance, as well as the Lamb-shift. Those extra contributions give rise to work performed by the system on the bath when the former is out of equilibrium. We compare this microscopic entropy production with the definition based on the contractivity of the reduced system dynamics (Spohn inequality). We show that, unlike for secular master equations, the two entropy production rates differ because of the presence of non-vanishing stationary coherences in the energy eigenbasis. However, in the case of a single thermal bath, the difference is purely transient, and no work can be cyclically extracted from the steady-state despite its non-Gibbs form. Finally, we illustrate our results with a simple example, clarifying and completing the thermodynamic picture of Markovian dynamics in the quantum regime.

</details>


### [174] [Quantum Logic Codes: Complete Transversal Logical Clifford Instruction Sets for High-Rate Stabilizer Quantum Error Correcting Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13521)
*Adam Holmes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了稳定子量子纠错码的结构与横向逻辑能力，提出了生成完整逻辑Clifford代数的电路深度下界，构造了多种新型横向逻辑门（包括旋转表面码中的深度一横向S门和2D-toric码中的深度一横向CZ门），并构建了一族高码率非LDPC CSS码，该码族具有常数深度的完整2-局部横向逻辑Clifford基指令集架构（ISA），且在某些子族中可达深度一，称为量子逻辑码（Quantum Logic Codes）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索稳定子量子纠错码的横向逻辑门能力，特别是突破现有构造限制，实现更高效、更低深度的横向逻辑门操作，为容错量子计算提供更优的编码方案。

Method: 通过理论分析建立逻辑Clifford代数的电路深度通用下界；提出旋转表面码中深度一横向S门和2D-toric码中深度一横向CZ门的构造；构建从[[n₀,2,d₀]]小码出发、通过拼接与级联扩展的高码率非LDPC CSS码族，证明其保持深度一完整横向Clifford基ISA。

Result: 得到生成完整逻辑Clifford代数的通用电路深度下界；实现旋转表面码中深度一横向S门和2D-toric码中所有奇数距离及L≥3的深度一横向CZ门；构造参数为[[n,√n,Θ(n^β)]]（β≈0.2823）的高码率CSS码族，其完整逻辑Clifford基ISA在拼接核心间可达深度一至深度二操作。

Conclusion: 提出了量子逻辑码这一新型码族，在保持高码率和良好距离参数的同时，实现了常数深度甚至深度一的完整横向逻辑Clifford基指令集架构，为实用规模容错量子计算提供了有前景的编码方案。

Abstract: We study the structure and transversal logical capabilities of stabilizer quantum error correcting codes. Among our results, we identify universal lower bounds on circuit depth to generate a full logical Clifford algebra, and develop novel constructions of logical transversal gates including a new depth-one transversal phase $\mathrm{\overline{S}}$ gate in the rotated surface code and a depth-one intra-block $\mathrm{\overline{CZ}}$ gate in the 2D-toric code that generalizes to all odd distances and all lengths $L\ge3$, respectively. Finally, we construct a high-rate non-LDPC CSS code family with parameters $[[n,\sqrt{n},Θ({n^β})]]$ where $β\approx 0.2823$ in one demonstrated case, that provably possesses a constant-depth complete 2-local transversal logical Clifford basis instruction set architecture (ISA) composed of all individually targeted $\mathrm{\overline{S}}$, $\mathrm{\overline{SHS}} = \sqrt{X}$, and $\mathrm{\overline{CZ}}$ gates. This ISA is depth-one for certain subfamilies that we design and generally constant-depth under certain conditions. The code family is built from a small code with parameters $[[n_0, 2, d_0]]$, and is tunable in the standard way: it tiles out to form utility-scale logical qubit counts, and it scales up through concatenation to achieve higher distances and error suppression. We show that this construction preserves the depth-one complete transversal logical Clifford basis ISA when composed with these commuting construction actions, inheriting structure from the core codes so that at scale the complete logical Clifford basis ISA remains depth-one up to depth-two addressable operations between tiled cores. We call these Quantum Logic Codes.

</details>


### [175] [Generalized Exact Fractional Quantum Information Model with Memory Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13525)
*Abdelmalek Bouzenada,Allan R. P. Moreira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在分数阶量子力学框架下，利用Riemann-Liouville导数形式重新定义了Shannon熵和Fisher信息，将其推广到非局部微分算子描述的分数阶量子系统，并以量子谐振子为例导出解析表达式，揭示了分数阶参数对概率密度局域化性质和信息度量产生的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典量子信息度量（Shannon熵和Fisher信息）推广到分数阶量子力学体系，建立适用于非局部动力学系统的信息论框架，并研究分数阶参数如何改变量子系统的信息特性。

Method: 采用Riemann-Liouville分数阶导数形式，重新定义分数阶量子系统中的Shannon熵和Fisher信息，构建数学表达式，并将其应用于量子谐振子系统以导出解析结果。

Result: 分数阶导数改变了概率密度的局域化性质，导致信息含量和敏感度出现非平凡变化；分数阶参数是控制偏离标准量子信息度量的关键因素。

Conclusion: 该研究为描述非局部动力学量子系统的信息论特性建立了自洽框架，分数阶参数在调节信息度量中起核心作用。

Abstract: In this paper, we analyze quantum information measures in fractional quantum mechanics using the Riemann-Liouville derivative formalism adopted here. In this case, we initially reconsider the conventional definitions of Shannon entropy and Fisher information, subsequently extending them to fractional quantum systems described by nonlocal differential operator frameworks adopted. Within this generalized formulation, fractional expressions of Shannon entropy and Fisher information are constructed and their mathematical structures examined thoroughly. Also, the formalism is then applied to the quantum harmonic oscillator, yielding explicit analytical expressions derived as functions of the fractional parameter therein. The obtained results demonstrate that fractional derivatives alter the localization properties of probability densities and generate nontrivial variations in information content and sensitivity across system behavior. In this context, the fractional parameter plays a central role in controlling deviations from the standard quantum information measures framework. Also, the study establishes a consistent framework for describing information-theoretic properties of quantum systems governed by nonlocal dynamics.

</details>


### [176] [Quantized time in quantum walks under weak rank-K measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13552)
*Klaus Ziegler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现，在量子行走中，通过多通道强监测或间接监测（通过辅助比特耦合实现），投影子空间中的平均返回时间也是量子化的，这反映了高维演化中普遍的时间量子化现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在多通道强监测或间接监测条件下，量子行走在投影子空间中的平均返回时间是否仍具有量子化特性，从而验证时间量子化在高维演化中的普遍性。

Method: 通过多通道强监测或间接监测（通过辅助比特耦合实现）对量子行走进行测量，分析其在投影子空间中的平均返回时间统计特性。

Result: 在投影子空间中，平均返回时间也呈现量子化特性，反映了高维演化中的普遍时间量子化。

Conclusion: 平均返回时间的量子化不仅存在于一维空间中的强监测和间接监测，还扩展到高维演化中的多通道监测场景，体现了时间量子化的普适性。

Abstract: Measurements can be used to monitor the evolution of quantum systems and may lead to a universally quantized time statistics. It is known that the mean return time is quantized for strong and indirect monitoring through the winding number of the return amplitude in a one-dimensional space. Here we discuss that under multi-channel strong or indirect monitoring, where the latter is achieved through ancilla coupling, the mean return time of a quantum walk in the projected subspace is also quantized. This reflects a universal time quantization for a higher dimensional evolution.

</details>


### [177] [Approximate quantum error correction theory of non-isometric codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13559)
*Yixu Wang,Yijia Xu,Zi-Wen Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了非等距量子纠错码的一般性系统理论，利用近似量子纠错框架定量研究了非等距编码对纠错精度和逻辑操作实现的基本限制，并将其应用于GKP码和tiger码的能量约束分析以及全息原理的讨论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非等距编码在连续变量编码的实验实现（如有限能量非理想码字）和全息量子引力中普遍存在，但缺乏统一的理论框架来理解其基本限制。

Method: 采用近似量子纠错（AQEC）框架，对非等距编码进行一般性系统分析，定量研究其对纠错精度和逻辑操作的约束。

Result: 建立了非等距量子纠错码的一般理论，并将其应用于GKP码和tiger码在能量约束下的分析，同时讨论了全息原理中的相关含义。

Conclusion: 非等距编码对量子纠错的精度和逻辑操作有根本性的限制，该理论为连续变量码和全息量子引力中的非理想编码提供了统一的分析工具。

Abstract: Non-isometric encoding arises in various important contexts in quantum error correction, most notably in the finite-energy, non-ideal codewords inevitable in experimental realizations of continuous-variable codes, and holographic quantum gravity. In this work, we present a general and systematic theory of non-isometric quantum error-correcting codes. In particular, we employ the approximate quantum error correction framework to quantitatively study the fundamental limitations imposed by non-isometric encodings on the accuracy of quantum error correction and implementation of logical operations. We apply our theory to analyze GKP and tiger codes under energy constraints, and discuss the implications to holography.

</details>


### [178] [Approximability limits for bounded-degree max-LINSAT and implications for decoded quantum interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13570)
*Maximilian J. Kramer,Carsten Schubert,Jens Eisert*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了有界度最大k-XORSAT问题在一般有限域上的近似硬度，证明以随机猜测的基准值r/q超过O_{q,r}(1/√D)的任何近似都是NP难的。结果还揭示了量子解码是DQI匹配该复杂度的关键，而经典解码则面临1/√(D log D)的信息论障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然布尔实例上最优的1/√D缩放已有结论（Trevisan的硬度结果和Barak等的算法保证），但一般有限域上的情况以及这对量子算法的影响尚未建立。本文旨在弥合这一空白。

Method: 通过建立一般有限域上max-Ek-LINSAT(q,r)与已知硬度结果的联系，将有界度下的NP-hard界限推广到任意有限域F_q，并分析了DQI中经典解码与量子解码的不同信息论屏障。

Result: 证明在有界度D下，超过r/q + O_{q,r}(1/√D)的近似是NP难的。对于DQI：(1) 经典解码器面临1/√(D log D)的信息论屏障；(2) 量子解码器可兼容1/√D的缩放。

Conclusion: 量子解码是DQI实现与复杂度理论最优缩放匹配的关键成分；而经典解码无法做到这一点。任何量子优势在有界度实例上仅限于常数前因子。

Abstract: For general max-k-XORSAT with $k \geq 3$, no polynomial-time algorithm can do substantially better than random guessing on worst-case instances unless $\mathsf{P} = \mathsf{NP}$: approximating beyond the random-assignment value of $1/2$ is $\mathsf{NP}$-hard. The picture changes when each variable appears in at most $D$ constraints. In that bounded-degree setting, polynomial-time algorithms can provably beat the random baseline by an additive amount of order $1/\sqrt{D}$. For Boolean instances, this scaling is known to be optimal: the matching hardness result is due to Trevisan, while the corresponding algorithmic guarantee was established by Barak et al. Whether the same holds over general finite fields, and what it implies for quantum algorithms, has not been established. We make this connection explicit and extend the hardness to max-E$k$-LINSAT$(q,r)$ with bounded degree $D$ and over arbitrary finite fields $\mathbb{F}_q$, proving that it is $\mathsf{NP}$-hard to exceed $r/q + \mathcal{O}_{q,r}(1/\sqrt{D})$. These results provide the complexity-theoretic benchmark for the bounded-degree instances targeted by decoded quantum interferometry (DQI), QAOA, and classical heuristics. Any quantum advantage on bounded-degree instances is therefore confined to the constant prefactor. We further show that in the context of DQI and on $(k,D)$-regular instances, this prefactor is sensitive to the nature of the decoder: DQI with classical decoders faces an information-theoretic $1/\sqrt{D \log D}$ barrier that prevents it from matching the hardness scaling, while DQI with quantum decoders is compatible with the $1/\sqrt{D}$ scaling -- identifying quantum decoding as the key ingredient for matching the complexity-theoretic scaling with DQI.

</details>


### [179] [Diffusive Dynamics of Nonstabilizerness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13606)
*Zhenyu Xiao,Shinsei Ryu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了U(1)对称性下多体量子系统中的非稳定化资源（nonstabilizerness）动力学，发现其晚期趋近随机态值的标度律为1/t，属于扩散普适类，并通过iTEBD数值计算和流体力学论证加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称性对多体系统量子信息动力学至关重要，但对称性如何影响nonstabilizerness（互补于纠缠的资源）尚未被充分理解。

Method: 计算U(1)对称一维随机电路中的稳定子Rényi熵；利用四副本张量网络描述 disorder-averaged 动力学，采用S4-适配的无限时间演化块消减（iTEBD）在热力学极限下直接计算；结合流体力学论证分析。

Result: nonstabilizerness趋近随机态值的方式遵循扩散普适类，稳定子Rényi熵隙按1/t标度闭合；该标度律在能量守恒的非可积Ising链中得到验证。

Conclusion: 本文建立了nonstabilizerness生成的流体力学视角，为理解哈密顿动力学中近似Haar随机态的制备提供了新见解。

Abstract: Symmetries shape the quantum-information dynamics of many-body systems, but their effect on nonstabilizerness, the resource complementary to entanglement, is less understood. We compute the stabilizer Rényi entropy, a measure of nonstabilizerness, in $\mathrm{U}(1)$-symmetric one-dimensional random circuits. The disorder-averaged dynamics is captured by a four-replica tensor network, which we evaluate by $S_4$-adapted infinite time-evolving block decimation (iTEBD) directly in the thermodynamic limit. Together with a hydrodynamic argument, our results identify a diffusive universality class for the late-time approach of nonstabilizerness to its random-state value, with the stabilizer Rényi entropy gap closing as $1/t$. The same scaling is verified in an energy-conserving nonintegrable Ising chain. More broadly, our framework provides a hydrodynamic perspective on nonstabilizerness generation and offers insight into the design of approximate Haar-random states in Hamiltonian dynamics.

</details>


### [180] [Optimal classical shadow estimation of unitary channels at Heisenberg limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13638)
*Entong He,Zihao Li,Noam Scully,Sisi Zhou,Yuxiang Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了并行、非自适应的经典阴影估计酉信道（CSEU）协议，仅需O(dε^{-1})次查询即可实现海森堡标度的精度，并证明该查询复杂度是最优的。该协议为量子学习理论提供了强大工具，可应用于酉信道层析、哈密顿学习、浅层电路学习等多个基础任务，并首次证明了并行查询即可实现最优酉信道层析，弥合了并行与序列协议之间的效率差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完整重构未知量子演化资源消耗巨大，而许多场景仅需预测特定属性，因此需要更高效的估计方法。现有方法在查询效率和精度标度方面仍有提升空间，亟需达到海森堡标度且查询最优的协议。

Method: 提出并行、非自适应的CSEU协议，使用O(dε^{-1})次查询来预测期望值tr[O·UρU†]，适用于常秩输入态或可观测量。同时给出了匹配的Ω(dε^{-1})查询下界证明。

Result: 该协议在常秩条件下达到了海森堡标度（ε^{-1}）的精度，且查询复杂度最优。并行协议即可实现最优酉信道层析，弥合了并行与序列协议间的效率差距。

Conclusion: 该CSEU协议是量子学习理论的基础工具，可显著提升多个量子学习任务的性能极限，特别是在需要高精度的关键任务中具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Full tomography of an unknown quantum evolution is resource-intensive and often unnecessary when the goal is only to predict selected properties. This motivates the study of classical shadow estimation of unitary channels (CSEU), a task in which one queries an unknown $d$-dimensional unitary $U$ and stores classical data that can later be used to predict expectation values $\mathrm{tr}[O \cdot UρU^\dagger]$ up to additive error $\varepsilon$ for arbitrary input states $ρ$ and observables $O$. We propose a parallel, non-adaptive CSEU protocol using $\mathcal{O}(d\varepsilon^{-1})$ queries when the input states or observables have constant rank. This achieves Heisenberg scaling with respect to $\varepsilon$ and is query-optimal, as we prove a matching $Ω(d\varepsilon^{-1})$ lower bound that remains valid even with stronger access to the unknown unitary. Our query-optimal CSEU protocol provides a versatile and powerful tool for quantum learning theory, pushing the performance limits of several fundamental learning tasks, including unitary channel tomography, Hamiltonian learning, boundary-regime quantum channel tomography, Pauli transfer matrix learning, inverse-free amplitude estimation, pure-state property estimation, and shallow-circuit learning. Remarkably, we show that optimal unitary channel tomography can be achieved using only parallel queries, closing the gap between the best achievable efficiency of parallel and sequential tomography protocols. Together, these applications establish our framework as a fundamental tool for learning properties of quantum processes, particularly for certain key tasks that require high precision.

</details>


### [181] [Generalized two-qubit Hamiltonian for Projective Quantum Feature Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13641)
*Rafael Simões do Carmo,Edson Amaro Junior,Felipe Fanchini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于广义两比特哈密顿量的投影量子特征映射（PQFM），通过局部泡利场和双比特泡利相互作用统一编码经典特征，提高浅层电路信息密度，并在四个生物医学数据集上展示了优于经典基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投影量子特征映射在编码能力上存在局限，缺乏统一的、与硬件兼容的编码框架，且浅层电路的信息密度有限。

Method: 提出广义两比特哈密顿量基PQFM，允许不同经典变量沿同一量子比特的不同泡利轴嵌入，并通过Python库pqfmlib实现。在IBM量子处理器（最多156量子比特）和态矢量仿真上生成量子特征，采用嵌套交叉验证和配对统计检验。

Result: 广义两比特哈密顿量系列在所有方法中提供了最一致的统计显著增益模式，性能依赖于数据集、编码策略、测量算符和硬件条件。

Conclusion: 广义哈密顿量PQFM是面向近近期量子实用性的有前景方案。

Abstract: Projected quantum feature maps provide a strategy for using quantum processors as feature generators for classical machine-learning models. Building on counterdiabatic Ising-glass and one-dimensional Heisenberg PQFMs, we introduce a generalized two-qubit Hamiltonian-based PQFM that provides a unified way to encode classical features through local Pauli fields and pairwise two-qubit Pauli interactions. This construction allows distinct classical variables to be embedded along different Pauli axes of the same qubit, increasing the information density of shallow circuits while remaining compatible with hardware constraints. We develop and implement these methods in pqfmlib, a publicly available Python library for constructing, executing, and benchmarking Hamiltonian-based PQFMs.We then benchmark the generalized Hamiltonian PQFMs against reference PQFMs on four biomedical classification datasets under a nested cross-validation protocol with paired statistical tests. Quantum features are generated using both IBM quantum processors with up to 156 qubits and statevector simulations. Our results show that the generalized two-qubit Hamiltonian family provides the most consistent pattern of statistically supported gains over matched classical baselines, although the performance of all methods depends on the dataset, encoding strategy, measured observables, and hardware conditions. These findings support generalized Hamiltonian PQFMs as a promising route toward near-term quantum utility.

</details>


### [182] [To Cool, or Not to Cool? Displacement Sensing with Hot Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13650)
*Piotr T. Grochowski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在玻色子位移传感中，是否必须将振子冷却到近基态才能实现量子增强，或者可以直接从热态生成敏感探针。作者发现通过奇偶性投影和相干叠加机制，热态混合性可以与增强的位移灵敏度兼容，且完全冷却并非普遍最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子增强位移传感假设振子需冷却到近基态，但冷却过程本身消耗资源且可能引入噪声。作者质疑这一前提的必要性，探索是否可以从热态直接生成非经典探针以实现量子增强，从而降低对初始冷却的依赖。

Method: 分析了三种热态探针制备方案（压缩、数增加、薛定谔猫态生成）对位移灵敏度的影响。识别出两种机制：奇偶性投影消除热混合对量子Fisher信息的抑制；相反位移的相干叠加保留灵敏度。基于这两种机制对热态协议进行分类，并构建了考虑退相干的实际优化问题。

Result: 热态混合性可通过奇偶性选择或相干叠加机制与增强位移灵敏度兼容。在这些机制下，位移量子Fisher信息甚至可随初始热占据数增加而增长。完全冷却并非在所有实验条件下都是最优策略。

Conclusion: 热态工程是实现量子增强玻色子位移传感的一条可行途径，无需强制性的基态初始化，为实验设计提供了更灵活的资源权衡选项。

Abstract: Quantum-enhanced displacement sensing with bosonic systems is typically formulated assuming that the oscillator is cooled close to its ground state before nonclassical probe preparation. We investigate whether such near-ground-state initialization is necessary, or whether sensitive probes can instead be generated directly from thermal states. We analyze hot quantum probes produced by squeezing, number-raising, and Schrödinger-cat-state generation applied to thermal inputs. We identify two distinct mechanisms by which thermal mixedness can remain compatible with enhanced displacement sensitivity. First, projecting a mixed probe onto a definite parity sector removes the usual thermal suppression of the displacement quantum Fisher information, which can then increase with initial thermal occupation. Second, coherent superpositions of opposite displacements can retain sensitivity through coherence between their displaced components, even when the underlying state is mixed. We use these two mechanisms to classify hot-state protocols according to whether their sensitivity comes from parity selection, coherence between displaced components, or both. Finally, we formulate an experimentally relevant optimization problem comparing initial cooling with direct hot-state preparation under realistic decoherence and show that complete cooling is not universally optimal. Our results establish hot-state engineering as a route to quantum-enhanced bosonic displacement sensing without mandatory ground-state initialization.

</details>


### [183] [Semi-Device-Independent Certification for Nonlocality without Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13667)
*Hanwool Lee,Joonwoo Bae*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究最大置信度区分（涵盖最小错误和 unambiguous 区分）在可分离态系综中的应用，比较全局测量与可分离测量的性能，证明全局测量优于可分离测量，从而在置信度层面建立无纠缠非定域性；同时，验证可实现的置信度可认证全局测量，实现半设备无关的无纠缠非定域性认证，且该方法兼容非完美探测效率，适于当前实验实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在可分离态系综中，探索最大置信度区分这一细粒度的状态识别策略，旨在建立无纠缠非定域性的置信度表征，并实现半设备无关的认证。

Method: 比较全局测量与可分离测量在最大置信度区分中的性能，利用最大置信度测量仅依赖探测结果的特点，分析置信度层面的非定域性，并通过可达置信度来认证全局测量。

Result: 全局测量在最大置信度区分中优于可分离测量，在置信度层面确立了无纠缠非定域性；并且，通过可实现的置信度可以半设备无关地认证全局测量。

Conclusion: 该方法使无纠缠非定域性能在当前量子测量设备下实验演示，即便探测效率非单位，因为最大置信度测量仅依赖被探测到的测量结果。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate maximum-confidence discrimination, which encompasses minimum-error and unambiguous discrimination, for ensembles of separable states by considering global and separable measurements. We demonstrate that global measurements outperform separable ones, thereby establishing nonlocality without entanglement (NLWE) in terms of confidence in a detection event, a fine-grained state-identification strategy that maximizes the probability of a correct guess given a measurement outcome. Conversely, verifying achievable confidence in measurement outcomes can certify global measurements, namely, semi-device-independent certification of NLWE. Our results make it feasible to experimentally demonstrate NLWE using present-day quantum measurement devices, even with non-unit detection efficiencies, since maximum-confidence measurements rely only on detected measurement outcomes.

</details>


### [184] [Analog Quantum Asynchronous Event-Based Graph Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11000)
*Kristian Sotirov,Shaheen Acheche,Antonio A. Gentile,Osvaldo Simeone*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于中性原子量子计算机的量子模拟异步事件图神经网络(QA-AEGNN)，将事件流数据映射到中性原子阵列，利用里德伯哈密顿量模拟消息传递，并采用混合量子-经典训练方案优化参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用中性原子量子计算机的可编程模拟量子计算平台，实现高效的事件型图神经网络计算，以处理事件相机产生的稀疏、高时间分辨率数据。

Method: 将流式事件数据映射到中性原子阵列（每个原子代表一个图节点），通过里德伯哈密顿量编程模拟消息传递计算，原子量子态作为节点特征嵌入，原子间相互作用实现图边；采用混合量子-经典训练方案，利用经典反馈优化模拟哈密顿量参数（如激光脉冲幅度和失谐量）。

Result: 利用中性原子量子系统的连续哈密顿动力学和大规模并行能力，自然地执行事件型图计算，具有潜在的精度提升。

Conclusion: QA-AEGNN框架为在量子硬件上实现事件驱动图神经网络提供了新路径，通过量子模拟计算可能带来精度和效率上的改进。

Abstract: Asynchronous, event-based graph neural networks (AEGNNs) have recently emerged as an efficient paradigm for processing the sparse and high-temporal-resolution data from event cameras. In this paper, we propose quantum analog AEGNNs (QA-AEGNNs), a novel framework to implement an AEGNN on a neutral-atom quantum computer. Neutral-atom quantum processors offer a programmable analog quantum computing platform based on controllable Rydberg-atom interactions. To this end, we map the streaming event data to an array of trapped neutral atoms, where each atom represents a graph node (event) and is positioned such that geometric proximity reflects the spatio-temporal neighborhood of events. The native Rydberg Hamiltonian of the quantum processor is programmed to mirror the message-passing computations of the AEGNN, with atomic qubit states serving as node feature embeddings and inter-atom interactions realizing graph edges. Furthermore, we propose a hybrid quantum-classical training scheme in which the analog Hamiltonian parameters (e.g., laser pulse amplitudes and detunings) are optimized using classical feedback to learn the quantum AEGNN model from data. Our approach leverages the continuous Hamiltonian dynamics and massive parallelism of neutral-atom quantum systems to natively execute event-based graph computations with potential accuracy improvements

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [Feature-preserving Latent-EnKF for Data Assimilation of Flows with Shocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12559)
*Hemanth Chandravamsi,Hangchuan Hu,Ponkrshnan Thiagarajan,Tamer A. Zaki*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 针对集合卡尔曼滤波（EnKF）在含间断解（如激波）中因多模态分布导致伪振荡的问题，提出了一种特征保持的潜空间EnKF方法，通过在学习的低维潜空间中执行集合更新（激波特征在该空间中呈平滑流形），再经共享解码器映射回物理状态，从而保留间断特征。在Sod激波管和Mach 2激波-圆柱相互作用实验上，该方法能准确恢复激波和接触间断，无伪振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准EnKF在处理含激波等间断解时，不确定的激波位置导致集合统计呈多模态分布，违背高斯假设，产生大尺度伪振荡。

Method: 提出特征保持的潜空间EnKF：在学习的低维潜空间中进行集合更新（间断特征在此空间呈平滑流形），再通过共享解码器将更新后的潜状态映射回物理状态。该方法消除了先前方法中逐个成员的排序训练和正数下限处理。

Result: 在Sod激波管和Mach 2激波-圆柱相互作用的数值实验中，使用稀疏含噪观测，该方法能准确恢复激波和接触间断，无伪振荡。

Conclusion: 潜空间EnKF通过流形表征有效解决了EnKF处理间断时的多模态问题，为含激波等强间断的流场数据同化提供了一种有效方案。

Abstract: The ensemble Kalman filter (EnKF) is widely adopted for sequential data assimilation, but fails for solutions with discontinuities, such as shocks in compressible flows. Uncertainty in shock location induces multimodal ensemble statistics that violate the Gaussian assumptions underlying the EnKF, producing large-scale spurious oscillations in the analysis state. We introduce a feature-preserving latent-EnKF that performs the ensemble update in a learned low-dimensional latent space, where shock and flow features admit a smooth manifold representation, thereby preserving sharp features during EnKF analysis. The updated latent state is mapped back to physical state through a shared decoder for all ensemble members. The algorithm eliminates the member-specific ordered training and positivity flooring used in prior approaches. Numerical experiments on a Sod shock tube and Mach 2 shock interaction with a 2D cylinder, using sparse and noisy observations, show accurate feature recovery of shocks and contact discontinuities without spurious oscillations.

</details>


### [186] [REMAL: Residual Equilibrium Manifold Active Learning for Surrogate-Based Multidisciplinary Design Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13245)
*Kail Yuan,Ashwin Renganathan*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: REMAL是一种基于残差流形代理建模的框架，通过多任务高斯过程模型学习耦合系统的联合残差流形，结合熵驱动的主动学习策略，在仅使用训练好的代理模型时通过非线性最小二乘优化恢复平衡状态，在多个工程基准测试中显著降低了多学科耦合系统平衡计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定点迭代在多学科设计优化、不确定性量化、数字孪生更新等外环任务中需要在每个设计点分别求解耦合系统的平衡状态，当学科评估成本高昂时计算开销巨大，亟需一种高效的代理建模方法来降低重复平衡求解的计算成本。

Method: REMAL框架：1) 使用多任务高斯过程模型对耦合系统的联合残差流形进行代理建模；2) 采用基于熵的主动学习策略在不确定的零等高线区域附近选择额外的残差评估点；3) 针对新的设计输入，仅使用训练好的代理模型通过求解非线性最小二乘优化问题来恢复平衡状态。

Result: 在四个工程耦合系统基准测试（卫星模型、气动结构模型、燃气轮机有限元传热与经济模型、带反馈耦合的改进涡轮模型）上，REMAL在需要跨设计空间重复进行固定点评估时一致地展示了成本效益。理论上证明了在温和假设下REMAL的预测固定点误差有界。

Conclusion: REMAL为耦合多学科系统的平衡状态计算提供了一种高效且理论上可保证精度的代理建模方案，特别适用于需要大量外环迭代的任务场景。

Abstract: Multidisciplinary design analysis of coupled engineering systems requires the computation of equilibrium states in which all disciplinary coupling variables are mutually consistent. Conventional fixed-point iteration resolves this consistency problem separately at each design point, which can become expensive when disciplinary evaluations are costly and many analyses are required in outer-loop tasks such as multidisciplinary design optimization, uncertainty quantification, or digital twin updating. This paper introduces REMAL, a residual manifold surrogate modeling framework for coupled systems. Instead of approximating each discipline independently or directly learning converged coupling variables, the proposed method learns a surrogate model of the joint residual manifold via multitask Gaussian process models. An entropy-based active learning strategy selects additional residual evaluations near uncertain zero-contour regions, and equilibrium states for new design inputs are recovered by solving a nonlinear least squares optimization problem using only the trained surrogate. The method is evaluated on four engineering coupled system benchmarks: a satellite model, an aerostructural model, a finite-element gas-turbine heat-transfer and economics model, and a modified turbine model with added feedback coupling. Across these cases, REMAL consistently demonstrates the cost effectiveness when repeated evaluations of the fixed point across the design space are necessary. Theoretically, we show that, under mild assumptions, REMAL's predictive fixed point error is bounded.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [187] [An approximate application of quantum gravity to the rotation problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12461)
*R. Michael Jones*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出量子引力（特别是路径积分方法）可以通过相位干涉自然解决广义相对论中的“旋转问题”，即在宇宙早期（大爆炸后约0.25秒）将物质与惯性系的相对旋转速率压低至极小值（~10^{-51} rad/year，依赖于暴胀e-fold数），且该结果对量子引力理论细节不敏感，仅依赖于可见宇宙尺度、普朗克时间、光速、哈勃参数和暴胀e-fold数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 爱因斯坦场方程允许物质与惯性系之间存在任意大的相对旋转，但观测表明该旋转速率极低。这一“旋转问题”长期缺乏物理解释，本文旨在用量子引力机制（无需微调）自然解释为何观测到的相对旋转如此之小。

Method: 采用量子引力的路径积分方法，将每条“路径”视为具有不同均方根相对旋转率的宇宙学；使用爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用量（包含大尺度相对旋转项），计算不同旋转率下路径的相位干涉；通过作用量在零旋转率处取极值，筛选出对路径积分有显著贡献的宇宙学，并估计其旋转率上限。

Result: 在暴胀50 e-foldings下，约0.25秒时显著贡献的宇宙学相对旋转率<~10^{-51} rad/year；55或60 e-foldings时更低。即使无暴胀，同一时刻旋转率上限也仅为~10^{-32} rad/year。结果对量子引力理论细节不敏感。

Conclusion: 量子引力（路径积分相位干涉）能够自然解释旋转问题，无需特殊初始条件或精细调节；暴胀进一步增强了压制效果，但并非必要条件；计算的关键物理参数仅涉及可见宇宙尺度、普朗克时间、光速和哈勃参数等基础量。

Abstract: Arbitrary initial conditions allow solutions of Einstein's field equations for General Relativity to have arbitrarily large relative rotation of matter and inertial frames. The ``Rotation Problem'' is to explain why the measured relative rotation rate is so small. Nearly any reasonable theory of quantum gravity can solve the rotation problem by phase interference. Even as early as about a quarter of a second after the initial singularity, quantum cosmology would limit the cosmologies that contribute significantly to a path integral calculation to have relative rms rotation rates less than about $10^{-51}$ rad/year. Those calculations are based on using 50 e-foldings during inflation. For 55 or 60 e-foldings, the cosmologies contributing significantly to the path integral would have even smaller relative rotation rates. In addition, although inflation dominates the calculation, even if there had been no inflation, the cosmologies contributing significantly to the path integral would have relative rotation rates less than about $10^{-32}$ rad/year at about a quarter of a second after the initial singularity. These calculations are insensitive to the details of the theory of quantum gravity because the main factor depends only on the size of the visible universe, the Planck time, the free-space speed of light, the Hubble parameter, and the number of e-foldings during inflation. These calculations use the Einstein-Hilbert action in quantum gravity, including large-scale relative rotation of inertial frames and the matter distribution, in which each ``path'' is a cosmology with a different rms relative rotation rate. The calculation shows that the action is an extremum at zero rms relative rotation rate.

</details>


### [188] [Black Hole Radiation Sparsity and Bekenstein Entropy Loss in Non-Commutative Schwarzschild Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12470)
*Abdellah Touati*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在非对易规范引力理论框架下研究史瓦西黑洞的辐射稀疏性、贝肯斯坦熵损失及总粒子发射，发现非对易几何消除了温度的发散行为，熵出现对数修正，总发射粒子数与熵行为成比例，且在蒸发最后阶段辐射极其稀疏并趋于发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非对易规范引力理论对史瓦西黑洞热力学性质（特别是辐射稀疏性、熵损失和粒子发射）的修正效应，以解决经典黑洞热力学中温度发散等问题。

Method: 基于非对易规范引力理论框架，计算变形霍金温度和熵，推导贝肯斯坦熵损失和总发射粒子数的非对易修正，并分析霍金辐射的稀疏性参数η̂。

Result: 非对易几何消除了温度发散行为，熵出现对数修正；总发射粒子数与熵行为成比例，符合非热辐射特征；非对易史瓦西黑洞辐射极其稀疏（η̂ ≫ 1），在蒸发最后阶段发散。

Conclusion: 非对易规范引力理论有效修正了经典黑洞热力学中的发散问题，但导致黑洞在蒸发末期辐射极度稀疏，最终停止辐射。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the sparsity of black hole radiation, the Bekenstein entropy loss, and the total particle emission of a Schwarzschild black hole (SBH) within the framework of non-commutative (NC) gauge theory of gravity. First, we provide a brief review of black hole (BH) thermodynamics, computing both the deformed Hawking temperature and entropy. In this geometry, the divergent behavior of the temperature is removed, and a logarithmic correction to the entropy emerges. We then present the NC corrections to the Bekenstein entropy loss of the SBH alongside the total number of emitted particles. Our results show that the total number of emitted particles is proportional to the entropy behavior of the NC SBH, which is consistent with non-thermal radiation. Finally, we analyze the sparsity of Hawking radiation in this geometry, finding that the NC SBH exhibits extremely sparse radiation ($\hatη \gg 1$), which diverges at the final stage of evaporation as the black hole ceases to radiate.

</details>


### [189] [Gotta light? Illuminating AGN disks with LISA EMRIs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12531)
*Federico Fantoccoli,Francisco Duque,Jonathan Gair*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文研究了LISA探测任务通过极端质量比旋进（EMRI）与吸积盘相互作用来约束气体力矩的能力。采用完全贝叶斯方法分析发现，LISA可以同时估算盘面密度和吸积率（或总光度），无需电磁对应体。力矩振幅可被约束在~10%以内，这增强了对（亚）微秒差距尺度吸积物理的探测能力，并有助于回答超大质量黑洞如何生长和与宿主星系共同演化等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前基于牛顿结果的预测认为LISA无法有效约束EMRI系统的气体力矩参数，而实际强引力场环境中的相对论效应可能带来不同的物理图像，因此需要基于更准确的相对论模型重新评估LISA的探测能力。

Method: 采用完全贝叶斯方法，结合近期发展的相对论性双星-吸积盘相互作用模型，对LISA观测EMRI系统的能力进行统计分析，并与线性信号近似（Fisher矩阵）方法进行对比。

Result: 完全贝叶斯分析表明LISA可同时估算盘面密度和吸积率（总光度），无需电磁对应体；力矩振幅约束精度可达~10%；线性信号（Fisher矩阵）近似方法对这些系统无效。

Conclusion: LISA能够探测（亚）微秒差距尺度上的吸积物理，补充电磁观测，有助于理解超大质量黑洞增长及其与宿主星系的共同演化，并通过与AGN星表交叉关联改进EMRI作为（暗）标准汽笛在宇宙学中的应用。

Abstract: We study the ability of the upcoming Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) to constrain gas torques acting on extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) when these are embedded in accretion disks, using recently developed relativistic models for the binary-disk interaction. Using a fully Bayesian setup, we find that, contrary to previous forecasts based on Newtonian results, these observations can provide simultaneous estimates of the disk surface density and the accretion rate (or, equivalently, its total luminosity) without the need for an electromagnetic counterpart. Our analysis also indicates that simpler measurement constraints based on the linear-signal (Fisher matrix) approximation are not valid for these systems. For typical EMRI observations, the torque amplitude can be constrained to within ~10%, strengthening the prospect of probing accretion physics at (sub)microparsec scales, deep in the strong-field gravity regime and complementing electromagnetic observations. This also strengthens LISA's ability to help answering questions such as how massive black holes grow and coevolve with their host galaxies and, by helping to identify the EMRI's host galaxy through cross-correlation with AGN catalogues, to improve the use of these sources as (dark) sirens for cosmology.

</details>


### [190] [Maxwell equations in Schwarzschild spacetime for static and freely falling observers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12630)
*F. L. Carneiro,L. V. A. Cunha*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在基于tetrad的框架下将经典电动力学推广到弯曲时空，重点关注Schwarzschild时空中静态观测者和径向自由下落观测者所测量的电磁场，通过有效介质类比揭示了引力场对电磁场方程的几何修正和运动学耦合效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立一套保持电磁场物理量直接可观测性的弯曲时空电动力学形式体系，并系统研究Schwarzschild引力场对不同类型观测者电磁场方程的影响。

Method: 采用tetrad（标架）方法构建弯曲时空电动力学，分别针对Schwarzschild时空中静态观测者和径向自由下落观测者推导Maxwell方程组，分析弱场和近地平线极限行为，并通过有效介质类比进行物理解释。

Result: 静态观测者：Maxwell方程保持球对称结构，引力在径向和时间方向引入几何修正（通过度规函数体现），可解释为固有径向距离、固有时间和lapse函数的径向变化；自由下落观测者：径向局域boost引入运动学贡献，电荷密度与径向电流混合，电磁场在时间-径向投影中耦合，而角向方程结构保持不变；通过有效介质类比，Schwarzschild几何对静态观测者表现为静止的非均匀介质，对自由下落观测者表现为径向运动的有效介质。

Conclusion: 该tetrad框架能够清晰分离弯曲时空电动力学中的几何效应和运动学效应，有效介质类比为理解引力场对电磁场的影响提供了直观的物理图像。

Abstract: Classical electrodynamics in curved spacetime is formulated within a tetrad-based framework that preserves the direct physical interpretation of the electromagnetic fields measured by an observer. The formalism is applied to Schwarzschild spacetime for two distinct families of observers described in the same coordinate system: static observers and radially free-falling observers. For static observers, Maxwell equations retain their usual spherical structure, while the gravitational field introduces geometrical corrections in the radial and temporal sectors through the metric function. These corrections are interpreted in terms of proper radial distance, proper time, and the radial variation of the lapse function. For radially free-falling observers, additional kinematical contributions arise from the local radial boost relating the free-falling frame to the static one.
