As a consequence, charge density mixes with radial current, and the electric and magnetic sectors become intertwined in the temporal-radial projections of Maxwell equations, whereas the angular Ampère-Maxwell and Faraday equations retain the same structure found for the static observer. The weak-field and near-horizon regimes are also examined, and the results are discussed through an effective-medium analogy, in which the Schwarzschild geometry behaves as an inhomogeneous geometrical medium at rest for static observers and as a radially moving effective medium in the temporal-radial sector for free-falling observers.

</details>


### [191] [Higher Dimensional Loop Quantum Black hole in de Sitter Spacetime: Quasinormal Modes and Shadow Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12698)
*Kourosh Nozari,Sara Saghafi,Ali Mohammadpour*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了de Sitter背景下高维圈量子修正黑洞的动力学和光学性质，通过三种方法计算标量扰动的准正则模，发现圈量子修正引起中等频谱偏移，而时空维度影响更大；同时分析了零测地线和黑洞阴影，利用M87*的Event Horizon Telescope观测数据对模型参数空间进行了约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索圈量子引力效应和高维时空结构在强引力体系中的可观测特征，通过准正则模和黑洞阴影两种互补的观测手段检验圈量子引力理论。

Method: 采用三种方法计算准正则模：时域演化结合Prony提取、矩阵方法和WKB近似；通过零测地线方法构建黑洞阴影，并与EHT对M87*的观测数据进行参数约束。

Result: 圈量子修正导致准正则频谱中等程度偏移，而时空维度的增加显著提高振荡频率和衰减率；所有模式的负虚部表明系统在探索参数范围内对标量扰动是动力学稳定的；额外维度通常减小阴影半径。

Conclusion: 准正则模和黑洞阴影可作为圈量子引力效应及高维时空结构的互补探针，通过EHT观测数据可以对模型参数空间施加有效约束。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamical and optical properties of a higher-dimensional loop-quantum-corrected black hole in a de Sitter background. The quasinormal modes of massless scalar perturbations are computed using time-domain evolution with Prony extraction, the matrix method, and the WKB approximation, showing good agreement among the three approaches. We find that loop quantum corrections induce moderate shifts in the quasinormal spectrum, whereas the spacetime dimensionality has a much stronger impact, leading to higher oscillation frequencies and damping rates. The negative imaginary parts of all modes indicate dynamical stability against massless scalar perturbations within the explored parameter range. We also analyze null geodesics and construct the corresponding black hole shadow. The shadow radius depends sensitively on the loop quantum parameter, the cosmological constant, and the number of spacetime dimensions, with extra dimensions generally reducing the shadow size. By comparing the theoretical shadow radius with the Event Horizon Telescope constraints for M87$^{\ast}$, we obtain bounds on the parameter space of the model. These results suggest that quasinormal modes and black hole shadow observables can provide complementary probes of loop quantum gravity effects and higher-dimensional spacetime structure in the strong-gravity regime.

</details>


### [192] [Asymmetric quantum steering harvested near a Lorentz-violating BTZ black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12766)
*Si-Yu Liu,Xin-Ze Song,Xiang-Yue Yu,Wentao Liu,Xiao-Li Huang,Shu-Min Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在洛伦兹破坏的BTZ黑洞时空中，两个位于不同径向位置的Unruh-DeWitt探测器对量子导引及其方向不对称性的研究。发现受更高有效温度的探测器反而表现出更强的导引能力，揭示了弯曲时空中热直觉的反转现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究弯曲时空中引力红移效应对量子导引不对称性的影响，以及洛伦兹对称性破坏如何调制这种量子关联。

Method: 采用两个Unruh-DeWitt探测器模型，分别置于BTZ黑洞不同径向位置，分析量子导引在洛伦兹破坏背景下的演化行为。

Result: 发现了有效温度更高的探测器反而具有更强导引能力的反直觉现象；量子导引仅在有限能隙窗口内存在并存在最优区间；洛伦兹破坏在最优能隙处对导引的抑制最强。

Conclusion: 洛伦兹破坏作为时空的几何约束，同时限制了量子关联的强度和方向性，揭示了弯曲时空中量子信息容量的新特性。

Abstract: We investigate the harvesting of quantum steering and its directional asymmetry between two Unruh-DeWitt detectors in a Lorentz-violating BTZ black hole spacetime. Since the detectors are located at different radial positions outside the black hole, they experience inequivalent local environments induced by gravitational redshift, causing Alice to undergo stronger effective thermal noise than Bob. Remarkably, we uncover a counterintuitive phenomenon in which the detector subjected to a higher effective temperature exhibits stronger steerability than the other one, revealing a nontrivial inversion of thermal intuition in curved spacetime. Furthermore, quantum steering survives only within a finite window of detector energy gaps and reaches its maximum within an optimal regime. We find that Lorentz violation suppresses steering most strongly near this optimal energy gap, indicating an enhanced sensitivity of maximal correlation extraction to symmetry breaking effects. Our results demonstrate that Lorentz violation acts as a geometric constraint on the quantum information capacity of spacetime, simultaneously restricting both the strength and the directionality of quantum correlations.

</details>


### [193] [Constraining Kerr supermassive black hole properties using gravitational waves from inspiraling stellar-mass binary black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12784)
*Jie Wu,Jin-Tao Yao,Mengfei Sun,Jin Li,Zhoujian Cao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究未来空间引力波探测器通过观测绕超大质量黑洞（SMBH）旋转的恒星级双黑洞（BBH）来约束SMBH参数的能力。基于LISA配置和二阶延迟干涉技术，利用Fisher信息矩阵估计参数不确定性。结果表明，外半长轴对参数精度影响最大，SMBH自旋和偏心率主要影响自身不确定性。高信噪比下SMBH质量和轨道参数相对不确定度可达10⁻⁵量级，自旋大小和方向可约束在百分之几以内。应用于M87*类似系统，引力波观测比当前电磁观测能更精确测量SMBH质量和自旋。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用未来空间引力波探测器观测绕SMBH运动的恒星级BBH（三级系统），以高精度约束SMBH属性（质量、自旋等），弥补当前电磁观测手段在SMBH参数测量上的不足。

Method: 建模稳定三级系统的BBH在Kerr时空中的运动，采用后牛顿波形结合运动源变换计算调制引力波信号；基于LISA配置和二阶延迟干涉技术，使用Fisher信息矩阵估计参数不确定性。

Result: 外半长轴对参数精度影响最显著；高信噪比下SMBH质量和轨道参数相对不确定度约10⁻⁵，自旋大小和方向可约束在百分之几以内；对M87*类似系统，引力波测量精度优于当前电磁观测。

Conclusion: 空间引力波天文学有潜力以高精度探测SMBH属性，为超越现有电磁观测手段提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We study the capability of future space-based gravitational-wave (GW) detectors to constrain supermassive black hole (SMBH) properties through observations of inspiraling stellar-mass binary black holes (BBHs) orbiting them. Focusing on stable hierarchical triple systems, we model the BBH motion in Kerr spacetime and compute the modulated GW signals using the post Newtonian waveform combined with moving-source transformation. Based on the LISA configuration and second-generation time delay interferometry technology, we estimate parameter uncertainties with the Fisher information matrix. Our results show that the outer semimajor axis has the strongest influence on parameter precision, while the SMBH spin and eccentricity mainly affect their own uncertainties. For high-SNR signals, the SMBH mass and orbital parameters can be measured with relative uncertainties on the order of $10^{-5}$, while the spin magnitude and its orientation can be constrained to within a few percentages. Applying the method to an M87*-like system, GW observations provide more precise measurements of the SMBH mass and spin compared with current electromagnetic observations, highlighting the potential of space-based GW astronomy to probe SMBH properties with high accuracy.

</details>


### [194] [Strong deflection limit-analysis using Picard-Fuchs equation in Einstein-Maxwell-Dilaton spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12819)
*Tadashi Sasaki*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-狄拉顿理论中带电黑洞的光线偏折问题，通过Picard-Fuchs方程和Painlevé VI方程系统，严格求解了强偏折极限下的对数公式常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展对带电黑洞光线偏折的理论理解，特别是强偏折极限下偏转角对数公式中常数的精确确定，超越以往数值或近似方法。

Method: 将偏转角表示为椭圆积分，推导出满足Picard-Fuchs二阶线性偏微分方程；利用可积性导出强偏折极限常数满足的一阶常微分方程；借助与Painlevé VI方程关联的哈密顿系统求解；通过零电荷极限（Schwarzschild情形）确定唯一解。

Result: 成功得到了两个强偏折极限常数$\bar{a}$和$\bar{b}$作为电荷变量$s$的显式解析表达式，且在零电荷极限下自动退化为Schwarzschild情形的已知结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为带电狄拉顿黑洞的强引力透镜效应提供了严格的解析框架，揭示了偏折角与背景参数间的深层数学结构。

Abstract: We consider the deflection of light by a spherically symmetric and electrically charged black hole solution in the Einstein-Maxwell-Dilaton theory with specific values of the dilaton coupling constant where the deflection angle can be represented as elliptic integrals. We show that the deflection angle as a function of two dimensionless variables $(s,z)$, which are related to the background charge and the impact parameter, respectively, satisfies a system of 2nd-order linear partial differential equations called the Picard-Fuchs (PF) equations. For each case of the dilaton coupling, the PF equations lead to 1st-order ordinary differential equations with respect to the variable $s$ for the constants $\bar{a}$ and $\bar{b}$ in the log-formula for the strong deflection limit. Using the Hamiltonian system associated with Painlevé VI equations, which holds as a result of the integrability of the PF equations, we solve the equations for $\bar{a}$ and $\bar{b}$. By requiring consistency with the Schwarzschild case in the zero-charge limit, $\bar{a}$ and $\bar{b}$ for nonzero charge are uniquely determined.

</details>


### [195] [Tests of general relativity at the fourth post-Newtonian order with GW230627 and GW250114](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12931)
*Xi-Min Liang,Yuan-Zhu Wang,Tao Zhu,Wen Zhao,Xin Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA探测到的两个特殊引力波事件（最高信噪比事件GW250114_082203和最长旋进阶段事件GW230627_015337），首次对广义相对论进行了4PN和4.5PN阶的后牛顿波形检验，结果与广义相对论预测完全一致，为当前和下一代引力波探测器的高精度零假设检验奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的参数化后牛顿形式中的波形检验仅限于约束到3.5PN阶的旋进系数，而理论突破已将解析致密双星相位扩展到4.5PN阶，因此需要首次利用实际观测数据对这些高阶效应进行约束，以检验广义相对论在强场、高度动力学和相对论性区域的有效性。

Method: 选取LVK网络中两个特殊事件（最高SNR事件GW250114_082203和最长旋进相位、最高旋进SNR事件GW230627_015337），对与4PN和4.5PN系数相关的无量纲偏差参数（δφᵢ）进行贝叶斯推断，获取其后验分布和90%置信区间。

Result: 四个偏差参数的当前90%置信区间量级为𝒪(1)-𝒪(10)，广义相对论的零值（δφ̂ₐ=0）完全包含在这些区间内，结果与广义相对论预测完全一致。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了4PN和4.5PN旋进阶段检验广义相对论的第一个经验基线，为利用当前和下一代引力波探测器进行高精度广义相对论零假设检验铺平了道路。

Abstract: Gravitational wave (GW) observations provide an unprecedented laboratory for testing general relativity (GR) in the strong-field, highly dynamic, and relativistic regimes. Within the parameterized post-Newtonian (PN) formalisms, waveform generation tests have conventionally been limited to constraining inspiral coefficients up to the 3.5PN order. Leveraging the recent theoretical breakthrough that extended the analytical compact binary phasing to the 4.5PN order, we present the first observational constraints on these higher-order effects. Our analysis utilizes two exceptional events detected by the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA (LVK) network: GW250114\_082203, which boasts the highest signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) recorded to date, and GW230627\_015337, which features a uniquely prolonged inspiral phase and the highest inspiral phase SNR to date. By performing Bayesian inference on the dimensionless deviation parameters ($δφ_i$) associated with the 4PN and 4.5PN coefficients, we find that our results are fully consistent with the predictions of GR. While the current 90\% credible intervals for the four deviation parameters are of order $\mathcal{O}(1) \text{-} \mathcal{O}(10)$, the general relativistic null values ($δ\hatφ_a= 0$) are entirely encapsulated within the bounds. This investigation establishes the first empirical baseline for 4PN and 4.5PN inspiral tests of GR, paving the way for high-precision null tests of GR with current and next-generation GW detectors.

</details>


### [196] [Tidal Love numbers and the dynamical instability of AdS bubbles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13014)
*Gerui Chen,Yu Tian,Hongbao Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了AdS气泡的非径向扰动及其潮汐Love数(TLNs)。在k→∞极限下计算了l≤6的奇偶宇称TLNs，发现奇宇称TLNs为负，偶宇称TLNs在υ²_s=-1时为正。随着l增大，TLNs趋于零。在0到∞的完整参数空间内计算了l≤41的偶宇称TLNs，发现在p/σ>0区域，随l增大越来越多的TLNs变为负值。对于l=41，除接近p/σ零点的一个狭窄区域外，TLNs均为负，这与Yang等人提出的自引力膜在eikonal极限下的不稳定性判据吻合良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究AdS气泡的非径向扰动及其潮汐Love数，探索其稳定性行为，并与自引力膜的不稳定性判据进行对比验证。

Method: 在k→∞极限下计算l≤6的奇偶宇称TLNs；在完整参数空间(0到∞)内计算l≤41的偶宇称TLNs；采用eikonal极限分析不稳定性判据。

Result: 奇宇称TLNs为负，偶宇称TLNs在υ²_s=-1时为正；随l增大TLNs趋近于零；在p/σ>0区域，高l时TLNs几乎全为负，仅在p/σ零点附近狭窄区域为正。

Conclusion: 计算结果与Yang等人提出的自引力膜在eikonal极限下的不稳定性判据高度一致，验证了该判据的有效性。

Abstract: In this work, we study non-radial perturbations of AdS bubbles and their tidal Love numbers (TLNs). The odd- and even-parity TLNs are computed up to $l=6$ in the limit $k \to \infty$. The odd-parity TLNs are found to be negative, while the even-parity TLNs are positive for $\upsilon^2_s=-1$. As $l$ increases, the tidal Love numbers approach zero. The TLNs of the even-parity sector up to order $l=41$ are also calculated over the entire parameter space of $k$, from $0$ to $\infty$. We find that in the region where $p/σ>0$, an increasing number of TLNs become negative as $l$ increases. For $l = 41$, the highest order we have examined, the TLNs are negative everywhere except in a narrow region very close to the zero of $p/σ$, which agrees well with the instability criterion in the eikonal limit for self-gravitating membranes proposed by Yang {\it et al.}\ [P. R. L. {\bf 130}, 011402 (2023)].

</details>


### [197] [Thermodynamics of polymerized vacuum regular black holes in anti-de Sitter spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13167)
*Sepideh Bakhoda,Ioannis Soranidis*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于有效圈量子引力动力学推导出一类真空规则黑洞，并将其推广到渐近反德西特时空。通过解参量化的Lemaître-Tolman-Bondi形式（引入辅助尘埃场仅用于定义内禀时间而非物质源），在球对称下将动力学约化为独立径向壳层，得到因子化壳层哈密顿量和Birkhoff型性质。构建了具有德西特核和反德西特核的规则黑洞模型，并在扩展相空间中研究其热力学，重点关注Hawking-Page相变。对于所考虑的模型类别，主导相变为Hawking-Page型，由黑洞自由能与热AdS背景的交叉决定。正则化通过变形物理外视界分支（包括其端点结构）影响相变温度的定量值。在大AdS半径区域，德西特核解表现出比反德西特核解更高的Hawking-Page温度，而在AdS尺度下限附近排序可能反转。因此，两类模型的热力学差异并非仅由正则性导致，而是核变形如何相对于热AdS参考背景修改视界分支的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受有效圈量子引力动力学启发，推导一类真空规则黑洞并推广到渐近AdS时空，研究其热力学性质特别是Hawking-Page相变。

Method: 采用解参量化的Lemaître-Tolman-Bondi形式，引入辅助尘埃场作为内禀时间（非物质源），在球对称下将动力学约化为独立径向壳层，得到因子化壳层哈密顿量和Birkhoff型性质。构建具有dS核和AdS核的规则黑洞模型，在扩展相空间中分析热力学。

Result: 主导相变为Hawking-Page型。正则化通过变形外视界分支影响相变温度。大AdS半径下dS核解Hawking-Page温度更高，AdS尺度下限附近排序可能反转。热力学差异源于核变形如何修改视界分支与热AdS背景的关系。

Conclusion: 两类模型的热力学差异不仅是正则性的结果，而是由核心变形如何修改视界分支相对于热AdS参考背景所决定。

Abstract: We derive a class of vacuum regular black holes inspired by effective loop quantum gravity dynamics and extend the construction to asymptotically anti-de Sitter spacetimes. The derivation is based on a deparameterized Lemaître--Tolman--Bondi formulation, where an auxiliary dust field is introduced only to define an internal time and does not act as a matter source. In spherical symmetry, the dynamics reduces to a set of independent radial shells, giving rise to a factorized shell Hamiltonian and to a Birkhoff-type property: for a fixed reconstruction function and cosmological constant, the static geometry is uniquely determined by the mass. Within this framework, we construct several regular black hole models with de Sitter cores and corresponding models with anti-de Sitter cores. We then study their thermodynamics in the extended phase space, with particular emphasis on the Hawking--Page transition. For the class of models considered, the dominant transition is of Hawking--Page type, determined by the crossing of the black hole free energy with the corresponding thermal-AdS background. The regularization affects the quantitative transition temperature by deforming the physical outer-horizon branch, including its endpoint structure. In the large anti-de Sitter radius regime, the de Sitter core solutions exhibit a higher Hawking--Page temperature than their anti-de Sitter-core counterparts, while the ordering can be modified close to the lower admissible range of the AdS scale. Thus, the thermodynamic differences between the two classes are not a consequence of regularity alone, but arise from how the core deformation modifies the horizon branch relative to the thermal-AdS reference background.

</details>


### [198] [Absorption cross section of a Schwarzschild black hole for a massive vector field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13217)
*Kaustubh Mukund Vispute,Rajesh Karmakar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在FKKS基底下，首次完整计算了Schwarzschild黑洞对任意频率有质量矢量场的吸收截面，发现质量项引入了纵向自由度，破坏了无质量情况下的宇称简并性，并展示了这些特征在透射和吸收谱中的体现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于超轻玻色场在黑洞时空中的研究近年来取得重要进展，而有质量矢量玻色子在黑洞中的透射特性尚未被充分理解，因此需系统研究Schwarzschild黑洞对有质量矢量场的吸收截面。

Method: 在Frolov-Krtouš-Kubizňák-Santos (FKKS)基底下，通过规范化的Proca场的守恒流自然导出吸收截面与反射/透射系数的关系，并在任意频率下进行数值计算。

Result: 与无质量（光子）情况不同，有质量矢量玻色子展现出由场质量引起的新特征：质量项引入了纵向自由度（超出横模）；在偶宇称横模中引入了标量型分支，从而打破了无质量情况下的奇偶宇称简并。

Conclusion: 这些特征在透射和吸收谱中均有明显体现，对有质量矢量玻色子透射特性的分析对超轻玻色场研究具有重要意义。

Abstract: In the present work, we study the absorption cross section of a Schwarzschild black hole for a massive vector field over arbitrary frequencies. Working in the Frolov-Krtouš-Kubizňák-Santos (FKKS) basis, we show how the conserved flux of the normalized Proca field naturally leads to the usual definitions of the absorption cross section in terms of the reflection and transmission coefficients. We then numerically compute these quantities over arbitrary frequencies. In contrast to the massless (photon) case, massive vector bosons exhibit new features arising from the field mass. In particular, the mass term introduces a longitudinal degree of freedom in addition to the transverse modes. Furthermore, it leads to a scalar-type branch in the even parity transverse modes, thereby breaking the usual degeneracy with the odd parity sector found in the massless case. We illustrate how these characteristics manifest themselves in the transmission and absorption spectrum. Given the recent developments in the study of ultralight bosonic fields in black hole spacetimes, the present analysis of the transmission properties of massive vector bosons bears particular significance.

</details>


### [199] [Probing cosmic dynamics in $f(T)$ teleparallel gravity: Constraints from logarithmic and log-periodic deceleration ansatzes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13261)
*Khomesh R. Patle,G. P. Singh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在修正的teleparallel引力框架下，采用幂律形式$f(T)=α(-T)^{n}$，通过对减速参数的对数和对数周期参数化来描述宇宙膨胀动力学，并利用宇宙计时器(CC)和联合(CC+Pantheon)数据集通过贝叶斯分析约束模型参数，结果表明模型偏离标准宇宙学常数行为，指向动态演化的暗能量场景，且热力学一致，宇宙年龄与观测约束兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在修正teleparallel引力框架下，通过幂律形式$f(T)=α(-T)^{n}$和对减速参数的两种特定参数化（对数和对数周期），探索宇宙的宇宙学演化，以研究偏离标准$\Lambda$CDM模型的动力学暗能量场景。

Method: 采用幂律形式$f(T)=α(-T)^{n}$的修正teleparallel引力；使用对数和对数周期参数化描述减速参数；推导哈勃参数作为红移的函数；通过宇宙计时器(CC)和联合(CC+Pantheon)数据集，基于$\chi^{2}$最小化的贝叶斯分析约束模型参数；详细分析减速参数、能量密度、压强、状态方程参数和能量条件的演化；采用几何诊断检验与$\Lambda$CDM的偏离。

Result: 几何诊断表明模型明显偏离标准宇宙学常数行为，指向动态演化的暗能量场景；模型在热力学上保持一致；估算的宇宙年龄与观测约束兼容。

Conclusion: 该框架的鲁棒性和可行性得到验证，表明在修正teleparallel引力框架下采用所述参数化方案能够有效描述宇宙膨胀历史，并支持动态暗能量演化图景。

Abstract: In this study, we probe the cosmological evolution of the universe within the framework of modified teleparallel gravity by considering a power-law form of the function $f(T)=α(-T)^{n}$. To characterize the expansion dynamics, we employ logarithmic and log-periodic parametrizations of the deceleration parameter. These specific parametrizations provide a flexible and well-structured description of the cosmic expansion history across different cosmological epochs. The corresponding Hubble parameter is obtained as a function of redshift, facilitating a systematic investigation of the background dynamics. The model parameters are constrained using cosmic chronometer (CC) and joint (CC+Pantheon) datasets through a Bayesian analysis based on the $χ^{2}$-minimization approach. The evolution of key cosmological quantities, including the deceleration parameter, energy density, pressure, equation of state parameter and energy conditions, is examined in detail. The geometrical diagnostics indicate a clear departure from the standard cosmological constant behavior, pointing toward a dynamically evolving dark energy scenario. Further, the proposed models remain thermodynamically consistent and the estimated age of the universe is found to be compatible with observational constraints, thereby reinforcing the robustness and viability of the framework.

</details>


### [200] [Solar-System Bounds on Ricci-flat Spindle Deformations of Schwarzschild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13325)
*Zhong-Xi Yu,Hong-Da Lyu,Shoulong Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用太阳系经典引力测试（行星近日点进动和卡西尼时间延迟）约束了一种新型非渐近平直纺锤形黑洞度规中的形变参数B，得到B≲10⁻²⁴–10⁻²³ cm⁻¹（近日点进动）和B≲10⁻²¹ cm⁻¹（时间延迟）的强上限，表明此类几何形变在太阳系尺度上必须被极度压制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一种新发现的四维广义相对论变形黑洞解具有非渐近平直的纺锤形几何结构（由参数B控制），虽然目前尚无天体物理机制能产生这种形变，但从现象学角度自然要问：若该形变延伸到弱场太阳系外部区域，其大小能有多大？

Method: 利用两个经典太阳系测试——行星近日点进动修正（要求B诱导的进动小于观测不确定度）和类卡西尼时间延迟构型下的光旅行时间修正——推导出B的领先阶修正表达式，并通过观测数据给出约束边界。

Result: 近日点进动给出最强约束|B|≲10⁻²⁴–10⁻²³ cm⁻¹，卡西尼时间延迟给出互补约束|B|≲10⁻²¹ cm⁻¹。两者均表明该纺锤形变在太阳系尺度上必须被极度压制。

Conclusion: 任何此类纺锤形几何形变若延伸到太阳系外部，其参数B必须在极小的量级上，从而排除了该形变在太阳系尺度上有可观测效应的可能性。

Abstract: Recently, a new class of deformed black-hole exact solutions was constructed in four-dimensional general relativity. The deformation is controlled by a parameter $B$, which survives after demagnetizing a black hole immersed in an external Bertotti-Robinson magnetic field and changes the global structure of the spacetime into a non-asymptotically flat spindle geometry. Although no astrophysical mechanism for generating such a deformation is currently known, it is natural to ask phenomenologically how large such a geometric deformation could be if it extended over the weak-field solar exterior. Using two classical Solar-System tests, we derive the leading corrections to planetary perihelion precession and to the light travel time in a Shapiro-type configuration. Requiring the \(B\)-induced perihelion advance to be smaller than the observational uncertainties in the supplementary perihelion precessions of planets gives the strongest bounds, \( |B|\lesssim 10^{-24}\text{--}10^{-23}\ {\rm cm}^{-1}\), while a Cassini time delay estimate gives a complementary null-geodesic sensitivity at the level \( |B|\lesssim 10^{-21}\ {\rm cm}^{-1}\). These results show that any such spindle deformation, if extended to the solar exterior geometry, must be extremely suppressed on Solar-System scales.

</details>


### [201] [Bounds on $Λ$ at the Galactic Center](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13356)
*Prajwal Hassan Puttasiddappa,Muzammil Mushtaq,Willian Ramirez,David F. Mota*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用围绕银河系中心Sgr A*运行的S2、S1和S14三颗恒星的天体测量和光谱观测数据，通过数值积分Schwarzschild-de Sitter时空中的类时测地线并加入相对论红移和时间延迟修正，结合贝叶斯MCMC分析，给出了银河系中心宇宙学常数Λ的上限：68%置信度下Λ≲6.9×10⁻⁴⁸ m⁻²，95%置信度下Λ≲1.0×10⁻³⁸ m⁻²。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用银河系中心恒星轨道动力学约束宇宙学常数Λ，探索通过局域天文观测限制全局宇宙学参数的可能性。

Method: 数值积分Schwarzschild-de Sitter时空中S2、S1、S14三颗恒星的类时测地线，加入相对论红移和时间延迟修正，采用贝叶斯MCMC方法推断轨道参数和时空参数。

Result: 综合三颗恒星的约束，Λ的上限为：68%置信度Λ≲6.9×10⁻⁴⁸ m⁻²，95%置信度Λ≲1.0×10⁻³⁸ m⁻²。

Conclusion: 银河系中心恒星的精确轨道观测可以对宇宙学常数Λ给出有意义的约束上限，展示了一种利用局域引力系统检验宇宙学模型的新途径。

Abstract: We constrain the cosmological constant $Λ$ using astrometric and spectroscopic observations of the S2, S1, and S14 stars orbiting Sgr A$^*$. The stellar motion is modelled by numerically integrating timelike geodesics in Schwarzschild-de Sitter spacetime, including relativistic redshift and time-delay corrections. Orbital and spacetime parameters are inferred using a Bayesian MCMC analysis. The resulting posterior distributions place upper bounds on the magnitude of $Λ$ at the Galactic Center (GC). Combining the independent constraints from the S2, S1, and S14 orbits yields upper bounds of $Λ\lesssim 6.9\times10^{-48} \mathrm{m}^{-2}$ at 68\% credibility and $Λ\lesssim 1.0\times10^{-38} \mathrm{m}^{-2}$ at 95\% credibility.

</details>


### [202] [Beyond the Metric: Geometrical Measurability as a Constraint on Quantum Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13522)
*Matteo Tuveri*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出量子引力必须满足一个认识论约束：在恢复广义相对论几何的同时，必须恢复客观几何测量的物理可能性。该约束通过有效度规、连续极限或类爱因斯坦动力学以及测量设备稳定性、因果可及性、记录形成和规范不变性等条件来实现，并通过Rindler视界、黑洞热力学、引力波探测和共形引力四个案例加以论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论的经验意义依赖于客观几何测量的物理条件（如时钟、光信号、自由落体等），但这些条件在量子引力中因时空的涌现性、热力学性、关系性或框架依赖性而变得非平凡，因此需要明确这些条件以约束量子引力理论。

Method: 通过分析广义相对论中隐含的测量条件（动力学稳定性、因果可及性、记录形成、规范不变性），将其应用于四个案例（Rindler视界与Unruh效应、黑洞热力学与Jacobson态方程推导、引力波探测、Weyl共形引力），检验这些条件在量子引力语境中的非平凡性。

Result: 得出一般性认识论约束：可行的量子引力方法必须在恢复几何本身的同时，恢复客观几何测量的物理可能性；共形引力作为临界案例表明，标度依赖的时空测量若无法物理固定将违反该约束。

Conclusion: 量子引力理论必须同时恢复几何及其可测量性，这一约束为评估量子引力方案（如涌现引力、量子参考系）提供了认识论判据。

Abstract: This paper develops an epistemological constraint on quantum gravity grounded in the empirical meaning of general relativity. The central claim is that a complete recovery of general relativity requires an effective metric, a continuum limit, or Einstein-like dynamics together with the physical conditions under which relational geometrical quantities can be objectively determined. These conditions concern the dynamical stability of measuring devices and reference systems, causal accessibility among physical systems, record formation, and invariance under admissible descriptions. In classical general relativity, they are usually implicit in the use of clocks, rods, light signals, freely falling bodies, detectors, and gauge-invariant observables. In quantum gravity, however, they become non-trivial because spacetime geometry may be emergent, effective, thermodynamic, relational, or frame-dependent. This claim is developed through four cases: Rindler horizons and the Unruh effect, black-hole thermodynamics and Jacobson's equation-of-state derivation, gravitational-wave detection, and Weyl and conformal gravity. The latter is discussed as a critical limiting case in which conformal invariance raises a sharp question about whether scale-dependent measurements of space and time can be physically fixed. Implications for quantum gravity are also discussed using emergent gravity and quantum reference frames as examples. The perspective developed in the study suggests a general epistemological constraint on quantum gravity: any viable approach must recover the physical possibility of objective geometrical measurement together with geometry itself.

</details>


### [203] [Trapped Surface as a Cosmic Censor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13592)
*Hideo Furugori,Daisuke Yoshida,Kaho Yoshimura*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于局域几何的闭捕获面判据，用于判断黑洞过充电/过自旋实验中弱宇宙审查是否被违反。该判据不依赖渐近荷或极端条件，排除了多种超极端终态和裸奇点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有弱宇宙审查检验多依赖全局渐近荷或极端条件，需要一种更普适的局域几何判据来判定黑洞过充电/过自旋过程中裸奇点是否形成。

Method: 在零收敛条件和一般性条件下，物质注入使黑洞视界截面转变为闭捕获面；若最终时空无法容纳该闭捕获面，则判定弱宇宙审查被违反。

Result: 该闭捕获面判据成功排除了超极端Reissner-Nordström、Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter、Kerr-Newman终态以及Weyl类裸奇点。

Conclusion: 提出了一种不依赖渐近荷或极端条件的局域几何判据，为弱宇宙审查提供了更普适的检验工具。

Abstract: We formulate a local geometric criterion for weak cosmic censorship in black hole overcharging and overspinning thought experiments. Under the null convergence and generic conditions, matter injection turns a horizon cross section into a closed trapped surface. Any final spacetime unable to accommodate this surface is ruled out. This trapped surface criterion excludes superextremal Reissner-Nordström, Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter, and Kerr-Newman final states, as well as Weyl-class naked singularities. Our criterion does not rely on asymptotic charges or on an extremal condition characterizing naked singularities.

</details>


### [204] [Geometrically Regular Black Object Solutions in Lower-Dimensional Gauss-Bonnet Gravity and Its Unimodular Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13635)
*G. Alencar,T. M. Crispim,C. R. Muniz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究在低维爱因斯坦-高斯-邦内（EGB）引力中构建正则致密天体。通过非线性电动力学、Simpson-Visser黑洞弹跳几何以及单模引力的扩展，成功构造了正则黑洞和黑洞弹跳解，并分析了其热力学性质。结果表明低维EGB引力是研究高曲率修正与正则致密天体相互作用的有效框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准BTZ黑洞在低维EGB引力中会出现起源处的真正曲率奇点，表明高曲率修正恶化了时空的紫外行为。为了恢复正则性同时保持BTZ-like的渐近结构，需要构造合适的物质场。

Method: 1. 通过非线性电动力学从场方程直接构造正则黑洞解并确定相应的电磁拉格朗日量；2. 扩展到Simpson-Visser黑洞弹跳几何，获得曲率不变量有限的平滑喉部构型；3. 提出低维EGB引力的单模扩展，通过时空依赖的宇宙学函数实现真空与物质扇区之间的动力学交换，由标准Maxwell场支持正则几何。

Result: 成功构造了正则黑洞解和黑洞弹跳解，发现物质扇区改变了蒸发过程，允许残留物形成，并产生非平凡的相变。在黑洞弹跳情形下，热力学量在适当极限下平滑恢复EGB-BTZ行为。

Conclusion: 低维EGB引力为探索高曲率修正、正则致密天体、非线性电动力学和单模引力之间的相互作用提供了有用的实验室。

Abstract: We investigate the construction of regular compact objects in the recently proposed lower-dimensional Einstein--Gauss--Bonnet (EGB) gravity obtained through regularized dimensional reduction. Unlike the standard BTZ black hole, the corresponding vacuum EGB solution develops a genuine curvature singularity at the origin, providing an interesting setting in which higher-curvature corrections deteriorate the ultraviolet behavior of spacetime. To address this issue, we reconstruct matter sectors capable of restoring regularity while preserving the BTZ-like asymptotic structure. First, we derive regular black-hole solutions supported by nonlinear electrodynamics and determine the corresponding electromagnetic Lagrangians directly from the field equations. We then extend the analysis to Simpson--Visser black-bounce geometries, obtaining smooth throat configurations with finite curvature invariants throughout the spacetime. As an alternative regularization mechanism, we formulate a unimodular extension of lower-dimensional EGB gravity and show that standard Maxwell fields can support regular geometries through a dynamical exchange between the vacuum and matter sectors mediated by a spacetime-dependent cosmological function. We further investigate the thermodynamic properties of the regular black-hole and black-bounce solutions, showing that the matter sector modifies the evaporation process, allows for remnant formation, and produces nontrivial phase transitions. In the black-bounce case, the thermodynamic quantities smoothly recover the EGB-BTZ behavior in the appropriate limit. These results demonstrate that lower-dimensional EGB gravity provides a useful laboratory for exploring the interplay between higher-curvature corrections, regular compact objects, nonlinear electrodynamics, and unimodular gravity.

</details>


### [205] [Hierarchical formulation of the self-gravitating, n-dimensional, charged scalar field in spherical symmetry in affine null formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13636)
*Laura Bridera,Emanuel Gallo,Thomas Mädler*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文发展了n维球对称爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-带电复标量场系统的仿射零特征形式，将主场方程转化为径向超曲面方程的层级系统，并讨论了初始-边值问题、渐近量及平衡律，作为一致性检验恢复了无标量场的Reissner-Nordström-Tangherlini族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏一个系统性的仿射零特征形式来处理高维带电标量场与引力-电磁场的耦合动力学，需要建立统一的框架以研究带电标量动力学和精确黑洞解。

Method: 引入辅助变量将主场方程转化为径向超曲面方程的层级系统，并补充标量场的输运方程；构造适用于渐近、顶点和零边界配置的特征初始-边值问题；推导渐近量和平衡律。

Result: 成功构造了n维球对称下爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-带电复标量场系统的仿射零特征形式；从层级系统中直接恢复了无标量场的Reissner-Nordström-Tangherlini族（包括非极端和极端分支）。

Conclusion: 该框架为在高维仿射零坐标下研究带电标量动力学和精确黑洞解提供了系统性工具。

Abstract: We develop an affine-null characteristic formulation of the Einstein-Maxwell system coupled to a charged complex scalar field in $n$-dimensional spherical symmetry. By introducing suitable auxiliary variables, the main field equations are cast into a hierarchical system of radial hypersurface equations, supplemented by a transport equation for the scalar field. We discuss the associated characteristic initial-boundary value problem for asymptotic, vertex and null-boundary configurations, and derive the corresponding asymptotic quantities and balance laws. As a consistency check, we recover the scalar-free Reissner-Nordström-Tangherlini family, including both the non-extremal and extremal branches, directly from the hierarchy. The resulting framework provides a systematic setting for the study of charged scalar dynamics and exact black-hole solutions in higher-dimensional affine-null coordinates.

</details>


### [206] [Search for High-Frequency Gravitational Waves via Geomagnetic Conversion with Radio Telescopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13642)
*Hongliang Tian,Lei Wu,Xiaolong Yang,Qiang Yuan,Bin Zhu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文首次利用甚大阵列（VLA）和阿塔卡马大型毫米波/亚毫米波阵列（ALMA）等射电望远镜，通过地球磁场中的逆Gertsenshtein效应寻找高频引力波（>10 kHz）转化为电磁辐射的信号。未发现统计显著信号，在1 GHz–1 THz波段获得新的特征应变上限（最严格约束达 h_c ≲ 10⁻¹⁸），将现有约束提升三个数量级，为下一代设施（如SKA）的探测铺平道路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高频引力波（>10 kHz）是探测 exotic 天体物理现象和新物理的重要探针，但此前该频段观测存在空白，亟需开展实验搜寻。

Method: 利用VLA和ALMA等射电望远镜，搜索HFGW在地球磁场中通过逆Gertsenshtein效应转换为电磁辐射的信号。

Result: 未发现统计显著信号；在1 GHz–1 THz波段获得新的特征应变上限，最严格约束达 h_c ≲ 10⁻¹⁸，比现有最好约束提升最多三个数量级。

Conclusion: 这些结果显著推进了对 exotic 引力波源未知参数空间的探索，并为未来SKA等下一代设施发现HFGW奠定了基础。

Abstract: The detection of high-frequency gravitational waves (HFGWs) above 10 kHz provides a crucial probe of exotic astrophysical phenomena and new physics. We report the first search for HFGWs via their conversion to electromagnetic radiation through the inverse Gertsenshtein effect in Earth's magnetic field, utilizing radio telescopes including the Very Large Array (VLA) and the Atacama Large Millimeter/submillimeter Array (ALMA). Since no statistically significant signal is observed, we obtain new upper limits on the characteristic strain across the 1 GHz -- 1 THz band, with the most stringent constraint reaching $h_c \lesssim 10^{-18}$, improving upon existing bounds by up to three orders of magnitude. These results significantly advance the exploration of uncharted parameter space for exotic gravitational-wave sources, paving the way for future discoveries with next-generation facilities such as the Square Kilometre Array (SKA).

</details>


### [207] [Quantum Reference Fields Transformations in Linearized Quantum Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09344)
*Lin-Qing Chen,Flaminia Giacomini*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在线性化量子引力中引入四个动力学标量场作为量子参考场，实现了物质与引力自由度的关系性描述，并推导了不同量子参考场视角间的幺正变换，该变换对应局域量子坐标变换，其结构类似于由量子场替换经典参数的线性化微分同胚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论的核心特征是微分同胚不变性，在没有外部参考结构时，物质与几何必须通过内部子系统（参考系）进行关系性描述。在量子引力中，这些参考系本身必须被量子化处理，因此需要建立量子参考系框架下的关系性描述方案。

Method: 在线性化量子引力中引入四个动力学标量场作为量子参考场，使其应力-能量张量进入引力约束；推广量子参考系的中性视角构造，建立规范不变的可观测量在不同量子参考场视角下的约化描述，并推导视角间的幺正变换。

Result: 得到了量子参考场视角间的幺正变换，实现了局域量子坐标变换；该变换作用于线性化引力场，其结构与线性化微分同胚类似，但经典规范参数被替换为物理量子场；构建了关系性von Neumann测量方案，展示了约化可观测量如何在量子参考场视角下被操作性地获取。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子引力中的关系性描述提供了场论层面的量子参考系框架，通过量子参考场实现了微分同胚对称性的量子化处理，并为从内部视角操作性地访问引力可观测量奠定了基础。

Abstract: Diffeomorphism invariance is a central feature of general relativity. Without external reference structures, matter and geometry must be specified relationally, with respect to internal subsystems serving as reference frames. In quantum gravity, these reference systems must themselves be treated as quantum, motivating the use of quantum reference frames. In this work, we address how such a relational description could be formulated within linearized quantum gravity. To this purpose, we introduce quantum reference fields, i.e. sets of four dynamical scalar fields whose stress-energy tensors enter the gravitational constraints. These fields extend the notion of quantum reference frames to local field-theoretic reference systems, allowing matter and gravitational degrees of freedom to be described relationally with respect to physical quantum systems. By generalizing the perspective-neutral construction of quantum reference frames, we show that relational, gauge invariant observables admit reduced descriptions in the perspective of each quantum reference field, and we derive the unitary transformations relating them. The resulting unitary maps implement local quantum coordinate changes between different internal perspectives, and act on the linearized gravitational field with an analogous structure to a linearized diffeomorphism, but with the classical gauge parameter replaced by a physical quantum field. Finally, we construct a relational von Neumann-type measurement scheme, showing how the corresponding reduced observables can be accessed operationally from the perspective of a quantum reference field.

</details>
